### FILE: tests/hardware/test_hardware_distance.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import sys
import time

try:
    from gpiozero import DigitalInputDevice, DigitalOutputDevice
except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
    raise SystemExit(
        "Le module gpiozero n'est pas installé. "
        "Exécute d'abord : sudo ./scripts/install_rpigpio.sh"
    ) from exc

BAR_WIDTH = 32
FULL_BLOCK = "█"
EMPTY_BLOCK = "░"


def wait_for_level(echo, expected_level: int, timeout_s: float = 0.05) -> bool:
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout_s
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if echo.value == expected_level:
            return True
        time.sleep(0.00001)
    return False


def measure_distance(trigger, echo, max_distance_cm: float = 200.0) -> float | None:
    """Mesure la distance du premier objet devant le capteur."""
    trigger.on()
    time.sleep(0.00001)
    trigger.off()

    if not wait_for_level(echo, 1, timeout_s=0.05):
        return None
    echo_start = time.monotonic()

    if not wait_for_level(echo, 0, timeout_s=0.05):
        return None
    echo_end = time.monotonic()

    elapsed = echo_end - echo_start
    if elapsed <= 0:
        return None

    distance_cm = (elapsed * 34300.0) / 2.0
    if distance_cm > max_distance_cm:
        return None
    return distance_cm


def meter(value_cm: float, min_cm: float = 0.0, max_cm: float = 200.0) -> str:
    if value_cm <= min_cm:
        return EMPTY_BLOCK * BAR_WIDTH
    if value_cm >= max_cm:
        return FULL_BLOCK * BAR_WIDTH

    ratio = (value_cm - min_cm) / (max_cm - min_cm)
    filled = int(round(ratio * BAR_WIDTH))
    filled = max(0, min(BAR_WIDTH, filled))
    return FULL_BLOCK * filled + EMPTY_BLOCK * (BAR_WIDTH - filled)


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Test du capteur HC-SR04 : affiche la distance du premier objet détecté."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--trigger-pin", type=int, default=17, help="GPIO BCM du signal TRIG.")
    parser.add_argument("--echo-pin", type=int, default=27, help="GPIO BCM du signal ECHO.")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=5.0, help="Durée de mesure totale en secondes.")
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=0.1, help="Intervalle entre deux mesures en secondes.")
    parser.add_argument("--max-distance", type=float, default=200.0, help="Distance maximale en cm avant retour None.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    trigger = DigitalOutputDevice(args.trigger_pin)
    echo = DigitalInputDevice(args.echo_pin, pull_up=False)

    print(f"Capteur HC-SR04 — TRIG={args.trigger_pin}, ECHO={args.echo_pin}")
    print("Mesure la distance du premier objet devant le capteur.")
    print(f"Durée du test : {args.duration:.1f}s")

    distances = []
    start = time.monotonic()

    try:
        while time.monotonic() - start < args.duration:
            distance = measure_distance(trigger, echo, max_distance_cm=args.max_distance)
            if distance is None:
                line = f"Aucun objet détecté dans la plage ({args.max_distance:.0f} cm)"
            else:
                distances.append(distance)
                line = f"Distance : {distance:5.2f} cm  {meter(distance, 0.0, args.max_distance)}"
            sys.stdout.write("\r" + line)
            sys.stdout.flush()
            time.sleep(args.interval)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption clavier : arrêt du test distance.")
    finally:
        trigger.close()
        echo.close()

    print()
    if distances:
        avg = sum(distances) / len(distances)
        print(f"Mesures : {len(distances)}")
        print(f"Distance min : {min(distances):.2f} cm")
        print(f"Distance max : {max(distances):.2f} cm")
        print(f"Distance moyenne : {avg:.2f} cm")
    else:
        print("Aucune distance n'a été mesurée durant le test.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

### FILE: tests/hardware/test_hardware_gpio.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse
import time

try:
    from gpiozero import DigitalOutputDevice
except ModuleNotFoundError as exc:
    raise SystemExit(
        "Le module gpiozero n'est pas installé. "
        "Exécute d'abord : uv sync --project ."
    ) from exc


def activate_pin(pin: int, duration: float) -> None:
    output = DigitalOutputDevice(pin)
    print(f"Activation du GPIO{pin} pendant {duration}s")
    output.on()
    time.sleep(duration)
    output.off()
    output.close()


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Teste les sorties GPIO du Raspberry Pi avec gpiozero."
    )
    parser.add_argument("pins", nargs="*", type=int, help="Liste des GPIO à tester (ex: 5 6 16 24)")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=0.5, help="Durée d'activation de chaque GPIO en secondes")
    parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="Teste les GPIO 5, 6, 16, 24 par défaut")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    pins = args.pins or ([5, 6, 16, 24] if args.all else [])
    if not pins:
        parser.error("Aucun GPIO fourni. Exemple : uv run gpio-test 5 6 16 24 --duration 0.5")

    try:
        for pin in pins:
            activate_pin(pin, args.duration)
            time.sleep(0.1)
        print("Test terminé.")
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption clavier : arrêt du test GPIO.")
    finally:
        for pin in pins:
            try:
                DigitalOutputDevice(pin).close()
            except Exception:
                pass


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

### FILE: tests/hardware/test_hardware_led.py
import argparse
import sys
import time

import spidev

BUS = 0
DEVICE = 1
MAX_SPEED_HZ = 8000000
NUM_LEDS = 3

START_FRAME = [0x00] * 4
END_FRAME = [0xFF] * 4


def setup_spi(bus, device):
    spi = spidev.SpiDev()
    spi.open(bus, device)
    spi.max_speed_hz = MAX_SPEED_HZ
    return spi


def led_frame(brightness, red, green, blue):
    return [(0b11100000 | (brightness & 0x1F)), blue, green, red]


def show(spi, colors, brightness=31):
    frames = []
    for i in range(NUM_LEDS):
        if i < len(colors):
            r, g, b = colors[i]
        else:
            r, g, b = 0, 0, 0
        frames.extend(led_frame(brightness, r, g, b))
    spi.xfer2(START_FRAME + frames + END_FRAME)


def raw_to_colors(raw):
    acc = 0
    for token in raw.split(","):
        token = token.strip().lower()
        if not token:
            continue
        if token in ("r", "red"):
            acc = acc | 0x04
        elif token in ("g", "green"):
            acc = acc | 0x02
        elif token in ("b", "blue"):
            acc = acc | 0x01
        elif token in ("rouge",):
            acc = acc | 0x04
        elif token in ("vert",):
            acc = acc | 0x02
        elif token in ("bleu",):
            acc = acc | 0x01
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Couleur inconnue : {token!r}")
    return [
        (0xFF if acc & 0x04 else 0),
        (0xFF if acc & 0x02 else 0),
        (0xFF if acc & 0x01 else 0),
    ]


def scan_pattern(spi, steps=6):
    base = [
        (0xFF, 0, 0),
        (0, 0xFF, 0),
        (0, 0, 0xFF),
    ]
    for step in range(steps * 3):
        colors = [base[(step + i) % 3] for i in range(NUM_LEDS)]
        show(spi, colors)
        time.sleep(0.3)


def rainbow_cycle(spi, steps=36):
    for step in range(steps):
        colors = []
        for i in range(NUM_LEDS):
            phase = (i * 2 + step) % 36
            if phase < 6:
                r, g, b = 0xFF, 51 * phase, 0
            elif phase < 12:
                r, g, b = 0xFF - 51 * (phase - 6), 0xFF, 0
            elif phase < 18:
                r, g, b = 0, 0xFF, 51 * (phase - 12)
            elif phase < 24:
                r, g, b = 0, 0xFF - 51 * (phase - 18), 0xFF
            elif phase < 30:
                r, g, b = 51 * (phase - 24), 0, 0xFF
            else:
                r, g, b = 0xFF, 51 * (phase - 30), 0xFF - 51 * (phase - 30) if phase < 33 else 0
            colors.append((r, g, b))
        show(spi, colors)
        time.sleep(0.1)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Test des LEDs APA102 du ReSpeaker HAT")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="mode", required=True)

    p_static = sub.add_parser("static", help="LEDs allumées en continu")
    p_static.add_argument("colors", nargs="*", help="Couleurs des LED 1..3 (ex: red green blue)")

    sub.add_parser("scan", help="Balayage séquentiel LED par LED")
    sub.add_parser("rainbow", help="Cycle arc-en-ciel")
    sub.add_parser("off", help="Éteindre toutes les LEDs")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:
        spi = setup_spi(BUS, DEVICE)
    except OSError as exc:
        print(
            f"Erreur : impossible d'ouvrir /dev/spidev{BUS}.{DEVICE} ({exc}).\n"
            "  - Le SPI est-il activé (dtparam=spi=on) et le Pi redémarré ?\n"
            "  - /dev/spidev* existe-t-il ? (ls /dev/spidev*)"
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    try:
        if args.mode == "static":
            if not args.colors:
                colors = [(0xFF, 0x50, 0)] * NUM_LEDS
            else:
                try:
                    colors = [raw_to_colors(c) for c in args.colors]
                except ValueError as exc:
                    print(f"Erreur : {exc}")
                    sys.exit(1)
            if len(colors) != NUM_LEDS:
                print(f"Erreur : attendu {NUM_LEDS} couleurs, reçu {len(colors)}")
                sys.exit(1)
            show(spi, colors)
            details = ", ".join(f"LED{i + 1}=#{r:02x}{g:02x}{b:02x}" for i, (r, g, b) in enumerate(colors))
            print(f"LEDs allumées : {details}")
            print("Appuie sur Ctrl+C pour éteindre…")
            while True:
                time.sleep(1)
        elif args.mode == "scan":
            print("Balayage LED par LED (rouge, vert, bleu)…")
            scan_pattern(spi)
            print("Terminé.")
        elif args.mode == "rainbow":
            print("Cycle arc-en-ciel…")
            rainbow_cycle(spi)
            print("Terminé.")
        elif args.mode == "off":
            print("Extinction des LEDs.")
        else:
            parser.print_help()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nInterruption : extinction des LEDs…")
    finally:
        try:
            show(spi, [(0, 0, 0)] * NUM_LEDS)
        finally:
            spi.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
### FILE: tests/hardware/test_hardware_micro.py
import math
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import wave

os.environ.setdefault("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "xcb")

import numpy as np

RATE = 16000
CHANNELS = 2
FORMAT = "S32_LE"
SAMPLE_WIDTH = 4
DURATION = 5.0
CARD_NAME = "wm8960"

BAR_WIDTH = 32
FULL_BLOCK = "█"
EMPTY_BLOCK = "░"


def find_wm8960_card():
    """Retourne l'index ALSA de la carte WM8960, ou None."""
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(
            ["arecord", "-l"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10
        )
    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    if out.returncode != 0:
        return None
    for line in out.stdout.splitlines():
        m = re.search(r"card (\d+):\s*(\S+)", line)
        if m and CARD_NAME in m.group(2).lower():
            return int(m.group(1))
    return None


def rms_db(samples):
    samples = samples.astype(np.float64)
    rms = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(samples ** 2)))
    if rms <= 1:
        return -100.0
    return 20.0 * math.log10(rms / (2 ** 31))


def meter(value_db, min_db=-60.0, max_db=0.0):
    ratio = max(0.0, min(1.0, (value_db - min_db) / (max_db - min_db)))
    filled = int(round(ratio * BAR_WIDTH))
    return FULL_BLOCK * filled + EMPTY_BLOCK * (BAR_WIDTH - filled)


def main():
    card = find_wm8960_card()
    if card is None:
        print(
            "Erreur : aucune carte WM8960 détectée.\n"
            "  - Le HAT ReSpeaker est-il bien branche sur les broches ?\n"
            "  - 'dtoverlay=wm8960-soundcard' est-il dans /boot/firmware/config.txt ?\n"
            "  - Avez-vous redemarré apres l'installation ?"
        )
        return

    device = f"plughw:{card},0"
    print(f"Carte WM8960 détectee (index {card}) → {device}")

    out_path = os.path.abspath(
        os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "..", "micro_test.wav")
    )

    print(f"Enregistrement de {DURATION}s à {RATE} Hz, {CHANNELS} canaux ({FORMAT})")
    print("Parlez face aux micros du HAT…\n")

    args = [
        "arecord",
        "-D", device,
        "-f", FORMAT,
        "-r", str(RATE),
        "-c", str(CHANNELS),
        "-t", "raw",
    ]

    proc = subprocess.Popen(args, stdout=subprocess.PIPE)
    frames_per_chunk = 1024
    bytes_per_chunk = frames_per_chunk * CHANNELS * SAMPLE_WIDTH
    raw = bytearray()
    peak = -100.0

    start = time.time()
    try:
        while time.time() - start < DURATION:
            chunk = proc.stdout.read(bytes_per_chunk)
            if not chunk:
                break
            raw.extend(chunk)
            samples = np.frombuffer(chunk, dtype=np.int32).reshape(-1, CHANNELS)
            left = rms_db(samples[:, 0])
            right = rms_db(samples[:, 1])
            peak = max(peak, left, right)
            line = f" L {meter(left)} {left:6.1f} dB  R {meter(right)} {right:6.1f} dB"
            sys.stdout.write("\r" + line)
            sys.stdout.flush()
    finally:
        proc.terminate()
        proc.wait()

    print(f"\r Pic sonore : {peak:6.1f} dBFS" + " " * (BAR_WIDTH * 2 + 24))

    if not raw:
        print("Erreur : aucun échantillon capturé. La carte ne délivre pas de signal.")
        return

    with wave.open(out_path, "wb") as wf:
        wf.setnchannels(CHANNELS)
        wf.setsampwidth(SAMPLE_WIDTH)
        wf.setframerate(RATE)
        wf.writeframes(bytes(raw))

    duration = len(raw) / (RATE * CHANNELS * SAMPLE_WIDTH)
    print(f"Fichier enregistré : {out_path} ({duration:.1f}s)")

    print("Lecture du fichier enregistré… (casque ou haut-parleur sur le HAT)")
    try:
        subprocess.run(["aplay", "-D", device, out_path], check=True)
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
        print("Problème lors de la lecture (périphérique de sortie WM8960 introuvable).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
### FILE: tests/integration/test_face_recognition_real_photos.py
"""Reconnaissance de visages sur de vraies photos publiques (YuNet + SFace).

Photos : Wikimedia Commons (domaine public) et OpenCV samples (lena.jpg).
Le prénom de test est « Barack » (galerie `gallery/Barack.jpg`).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.face_gallery import FaceGallery

ASSETS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "assets" / "face_recognition"
_DEFAULTS = RuntimeSettings()
_MODELS_PRESENT = (
    Path(_DEFAULTS.camera_face_model_path).is_file()
    and Path(_DEFAULTS.camera_face_recognition_model_path).is_file()
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(not _MODELS_PRESENT, reason="modèles YuNet/SFace absents")


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def gallery() -> FaceGallery:
    settings = RuntimeSettings(camera_face_gallery_dir=str(ASSETS / "gallery"))
    loaded = FaceGallery.load(settings)
    assert loaded.names() == ["Barack"]
    return loaded


def _best_score(gallery: FaceGallery, image: np.ndarray, name: str = "Barack") -> float | None:
    """Meilleure similarité entre la cible et les visages détectés (None si aucun visage)."""
    target = gallery.get(name)
    assert target is not None and gallery.embedder is not None
    detector = cv2.FaceDetectorYN.create(
        _DEFAULTS.camera_face_model_path, "", (image.shape[1], image.shape[0]),
        score_threshold=_DEFAULTS.camera_face_score_threshold, nms_threshold=0.3, top_k=5000,
    )
    _, faces = detector.detect(image)
    if faces is None:
        return None
    scores = [gallery.embedder.similarity(target, gallery.embedder.embed(image, face)) for face in faces]
    return max(scores)


def _read(name: str) -> np.ndarray:
    image = cv2.imread(str(ASSETS / name))
    assert image is not None, name
    return image


def test_same_person_on_other_photo_is_recognized(gallery: FaceGallery) -> None:
    score = _best_score(gallery, _read("same_person.jpg"))
    assert score is not None
    assert gallery.embedder.is_match(score), score


def test_other_face_is_not_recognized(gallery: FaceGallery) -> None:
    score = _best_score(gallery, _read("other_face.jpg"))
    assert score is None or not gallery.embedder.is_match(score), score


def test_dog_is_not_recognized(gallery: FaceGallery) -> None:
    score = _best_score(gallery, _read("dog.jpg"))
    assert score is None or not gallery.embedder.is_match(score), score


def test_target_is_found_among_several_faces(gallery: FaceGallery) -> None:
    """Deux visages côte à côte : la cible doit être reconnue parmi eux."""
    height = 480
    left = _read("other_face.jpg")
    right = _read("same_person.jpg")
    left = cv2.resize(left, (int(left.shape[1] * height / left.shape[0]), height))
    right = cv2.resize(right, (int(right.shape[1] * height / right.shape[0]), height))
    composed = cv2.hconcat([left, right])
    score = _best_score(gallery, composed)
    assert score is not None
    assert gallery.embedder.is_match(score), score


def test_unknown_name_is_not_in_gallery(gallery: FaceGallery) -> None:
    assert gallery.resolve("Quentin") is None
    assert gallery.resolve("barack") == "Barack"

### FILE: tests/integration/test_voice_runtime_llm_integration.py
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import requests

from irobot.app.interfaces.intent.llm_intent_parser import warm_up_llm
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot

LLM_BASE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8080"


def _candidate_llama_binaries() -> list[str]:
    bins = []
    for candidate in (
        shutil.which("llama-server"),
        shutil.which("llm-server"),
        "/usr/local/bin/llama-server",
        "/usr/local/bin/llm-server",
        str(Path.home() / ".local" / "bin" / "llama-server"),
        str(Path.home() / "tools" / "llama.cpp" / "build" / "bin" / "llama-server"),
    ):
        if candidate and os.path.exists(candidate):
            bins.append(candidate)
    return bins


def _candidate_model_paths() -> list[str]:
    paths: list[str] = []
    candidates = [
        Path.home() / "models",
        Path.home() / ".cache" / "llama.cpp",
        Path("/opt"),
    ]
    for base in candidates:
        if not base.exists():
            continue
        for file in base.rglob("*.gguf"):
            paths.append(str(file))
    return paths


def _find_llm_launch_config() -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    binaries = _candidate_llama_binaries()
    if not binaries:
        return None
    models = _candidate_model_paths()
    if not models:
        return None
    return binaries[0], models[0]


def _wait_for_llm_ready(timeout: float = 30.0) -> bool:
    deadline = time.time() + timeout
    while time.time() < deadline:
        try:
            response = requests.get(f"{LLM_BASE_URL}/v1/models", timeout=2)
            if response.status_code == 200:
                return True
        except requests.RequestException:
            pass
        time.sleep(1)
    return False


def _start_llm_if_needed() -> bool:
    if _wait_for_llm_ready(timeout=2):
        return True

    config = _find_llm_launch_config()
    if config is None:
        return False

    binary, model = config
    try:
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            [binary, "-m", model, "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", "8080", "--ctx-size", "4096", "--threads", "4", "--parallel", "1"],
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
        )
    except OSError:
        return False

    try:
        return _wait_for_llm_ready(timeout=40)
    finally:
        if process.poll() is not None and process.returncode not in (0, None):
            process.wait(timeout=5)


def test_voice_runtime_uses_llm_plan_when_available():
    if not _start_llm_if_needed():
        pytest.skip("Aucun LLM local ou modèle GGUF détecté; test d'intégration ignoré.")

    # Préchauffage comme en production (app.py) : sans lui, la première requête dépasse le read timeout (10 s) et le plan retombe sur le parseur local.
    assert warm_up_llm(), "Préchauffage du LLM en échec."

    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True)
    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("suis la balle rouge")

    # Sans perception visuelle, le suivi de balle échoue par conception : on vérifie le plan, pas l'exécution.
    assert result["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert result["steps"][0]["intent"].name in {"follow_red_ball", "unknown"}
    assert len(result["steps"]) > 0

### FILE: tests/unit/fake_robot.py
"""Robot factice utilisé pour les tests unitaires des skills.

Le but est de simuler un robot sans dépendre des GPIO ou du matériel réel.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable


class FakeRobot:
    """Implémentation minimale d'un robot pour les tests unitaires.

    `time_fn` (optionnel) horodate chaque commande dans `timed_commands` (horloge simulée).
    """

    def __init__(self, time_fn: Callable[[], float] | None = None):
        self.commands: list[str] = []
        self.timed_commands: list[tuple[float, str]] = []
        self.time_fn = time_fn
        self.speed = 0.5
        self.obstacle_detected = False

    def _record(self, command: str) -> None:
        self.commands.append(command)
        if self.time_fn is not None:
            self.timed_commands.append((self.time_fn(), command))

    def forward(self) -> None:
        self._record("forward")

    def backward(self) -> None:
        self._record("backward")

    def left(self) -> None:
        self._record("left")

    def right(self) -> None:
        self._record("right")

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._record("stop")

    def set_speed(self, speed: float) -> None:
        self.speed = speed

    def check_obstacle(self) -> bool:
        return self.obstacle_detected

### FILE: tests/unit/perception_helpers.py
from __future__ import annotations

from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget


def camera_target(pipeline, target: str) -> PerceptionTarget:
    semantic_target = PerceptionTarget.from_value(target)
    if semantic_target is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported test target: {target}")
    return semantic_target

### FILE: tests/unit/test_app_named_face_follow.py
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np

from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent
from irobot.app.interfaces.command_service import CommandService
from irobot.app.interfaces.language_controller import LanguageController
from irobot.app.interfaces.plan_guards import follow_request_refusal, requests_human_follow


class _Camera:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[tuple[str | None, object]] = []

    def set_face_target(self, name, embedding) -> None:
        self.calls.append((name, embedding))


class _Gallery:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._embeddings = {"Mathieu": np.ones(4, dtype=np.float32), "Moi": np.zeros(4, dtype=np.float32)}

    def resolve(self, name: str) -> str | None:
        return {"mathieu": "Mathieu", "moi": "Moi"}.get(name.lower())

    def get(self, name: str):
        canonical = self.resolve(name)
        return None if canonical is None else self._embeddings[canonical]


def test_named_follow_is_accepted_by_guards() -> None:
    assert requests_human_follow("suis Mathieu")
    assert follow_request_refusal("suis Mathieu") is None


def test_named_follow_does_not_swallow_objects_or_articles() -> None:
    assert follow_request_refusal("suis le chat") == "unsupported_follow_target"
    assert follow_request_refusal("suis un chat") == "unsupported_follow_target"


def test_select_face_target_uses_gallery_for_named_person() -> None:
    camera = _Camera()
    controller = LanguageController(camera=camera, gallery=_Gallery())
    intent = SimpleNamespace(name="follow_person", parameters={"person": "mathieu"})

    controller._select_face_target(intent)

    assert camera.calls[0][0] == "Mathieu"
    assert np.array_equal(camera.calls[0][1], np.ones(4, dtype=np.float32))


def test_select_face_target_resets_to_any_face_without_name() -> None:
    camera = _Camera()
    controller = LanguageController(camera=camera, gallery=_Gallery())
    controller._select_face_target(SimpleNamespace(name="follow_person", parameters={}))
    controller._select_face_target(SimpleNamespace(name="move_forward", parameters={}))

    assert camera.calls == [(None, None), (None, None)]


def test_local_parser_extracts_person_name() -> None:
    from irobot.app.interfaces.intent.text_intent_parser import TextIntentParser

    intent = TextIntentParser().parse("suis Mathieu")

    assert intent.name == "follow_person"
    assert intent.parameters == {"person": "Mathieu"}
    assert TextIntentParser().parse("suis le chat").name != "follow_person"


def test_search_and_approach_selects_named_face_target() -> None:
    camera = _Camera()
    controller = LanguageController(camera=camera, gallery=_Gallery())
    controller._select_face_target(
        SimpleNamespace(name="search_and_approach_object", parameters={"person": "Mathieu"}),
    )
    controller._select_face_target(SimpleNamespace(name="search_and_approach_object", parameters={}))

    assert camera.calls[0][0] == "Mathieu"
    assert np.array_equal(camera.calls[0][1], np.ones(4, dtype=np.float32))
    assert camera.calls[1] == (None, None)


def test_plan_refusal_flags_unknown_person_only() -> None:
    service = CommandService(use_llm=False, gallery=_Gallery())

    def refusal(name: str):
        intent = Intent(
            name="search_and_approach_object", target="face", parameters={"person": name}, raw_text="approche toi",
        )
        return service._plan_refusal([intent], {"source": "local"})

    assert refusal("Inconnu") == "personne inconnue"
    assert refusal("mathieu") is None

### FILE: tests/unit/test_architecture.py
import ast
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

SRC = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "src" / "irobot"
FORBIDDEN = {"common": ("irobot.app", "irobot.tools"), "app": ("irobot.tools",), "tools": ("irobot.app",)}


def _imports(path: Path):
    for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            yield from (alias.name for alias in node.names)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom) and node.module:
            yield node.module


@pytest.mark.parametrize("package", FORBIDDEN)
def test_layer_dependencies(package):
    offenders = [
        f"{path.relative_to(SRC)} -> {name}"
        for path in (SRC / package).rglob("*.py")
        for name in _imports(path)
        if any(name == f or name.startswith(f + ".") for f in FORBIDDEN[package])
    ]
    assert not offenders, offenders

### FILE: tests/unit/test_camera_aruco.py
import queue
import math
import threading
import time
from types import SimpleNamespace

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import MarkerIdentity, RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.hardware.camera import camera_live_perception_pipeline

from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import ArucoFrameDetection, ObservationDisposition
from irobot.common.interfaces.robot_controller import RobotController
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot
from tests.unit.perception_helpers import camera_target


def _frame(center_x=240, width=80, image_width=480, marker_id=1):
    dictionary = cv2.aruco.getPredefinedDictionary(cv2.aruco.DICT_4X4_50)
    marker = cv2.aruco.generateImageMarker(dictionary, marker_id, width)
    grayscale = np.full((640, image_width), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    left = center_x - width // 2
    grayscale[280:280 + width, left:left + width] = marker
    return cv2.cvtColor(grayscale, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2RGB)


def _runtime_settings(**kwargs):
    return RuntimeSettings(
        camera_focal_px=600.0,
        camera_capture_size_px=(640, 480),
        camera_oriented_frame_size_px=(480, 640),
        camera_clahe_enabled=False,
        camera_exposure_value=0.0,
        **kwargs,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def pipeline(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings", _runtime_settings)
    return camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(
        autostart=False,
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("center_x", "position"),
    [(179, "left"), (181, "center"), (240, "center"), (300, "center"), (301, "right")],
)
def test_marker_position_and_distance(pipeline, center_x, position):
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, "marker")
    pipeline.detect(target=strategy)
    detection = pipeline._process_frame(_frame(center_x), target=strategy)
    assert detection.label == "marker_1"
    assert detection.position == position
    assert detection.confidence == 1.0
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(
        8 / 79 * math.sqrt(600**2 + (center_x - 240.5)**2 + 0.5**2),
    )
    pipeline._last_detection = detection
    assert pipeline.detect(target=camera_target(pipeline, "tag")).distance_cm == detection.distance_cm


def test_position_scales_to_image_width(pipeline):
    assert pipeline._process_frame(
        _frame(center_x=440, image_width=640), target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")
    ).position == "right"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("center_x", "position"),
    [(179, "left"), (180, "center"), (300, "center"), (301, "right")],
)
def test_exact_thresholds_and_grayscale_input(pipeline, monkeypatch, center_x, position):
    frame = _frame()

    def detect_markers(grayscale):
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(grayscale, cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY))
        corners = np.asarray([[
                (center_x - 40, 280), (center_x + 40, 280),
                (center_x + 40, 360), (center_x - 40, 360),
            ]], dtype=np.float32)
        return corners, np.asarray([[1]], dtype=np.int32), None

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        pipeline._detector._marker_detector,
        "_detector",
        SimpleNamespace(detectMarkers=detect_markers),
    )
    detection = pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker"))
    assert detection.position == position
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(
        8 / 80 * math.hypot(600, center_x - 240),
    )


def test_configured_identity(monkeypatch):
    identity = MarkerIdentity("aruco", "DICT_4X4_50", "2")
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: _runtime_settings(human_marker_identity=identity),
    )
    pipeline = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(
        autostart=False,
    )
    marker_detector = pipeline._detector._marker_detector
    assert marker_detector.identity == identity
    assert pipeline._process_frame(_frame(marker_id=1), target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")) is None
    assert pipeline._process_frame(
        _frame(marker_id=2), target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")
    ).label == "marker_1"


def test_detect_returns_complete_marker_result(pipeline):
    detector = pipeline._detector._marker_detector

    assert detector.focal_px == pipeline.focal_px == 600.0
    detected = detector.detect(_frame())
    assert detected.disposition is ObservationDisposition.VALID
    assert detected.corners_px is not None
    assert detected.distance_cm is not None
    assert detected.bbox_px is not None
    object_detection = detected.as_object_detection()
    assert object_detection is not None
    assert object_detection.distance_cm == detected.distance_cm

    absent = detector.detect(np.full((640, 480, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8))
    assert absent.disposition is ObservationDisposition.ABSENT
    assert absent.corners_px is None
    assert absent.distance_cm is None
    assert absent.as_object_detection() is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["marker", "tag"])
def test_requested_target_changes_camera_processing(pipeline, target):
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, target)
    assert pipeline.detect(target=strategy) is None
    detection = pipeline._process_frame(_frame(), target=strategy)
    pipeline._last_detection = detection
    assert pipeline.detect(target=strategy).label == "marker_1"
    red_strategy = camera_target(pipeline, "red")
    assert pipeline.detect(target=red_strategy) is None
    assert pipeline._process_frame(_frame(), target=red_strategy) is None


def test_marker_and_color_targets_use_only_the_requested_detector(pipeline):
    frame = _frame()
    cv2.circle(frame, (400, 150), 25, (255, 0, 0), -1)
    assert pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")).label == "marker_1"
    assert pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(pipeline, "red")).label == "red_ball"


def test_missing_strategy_is_not_silently_defaulted(pipeline):
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        pipeline.detect()
    assert pipeline._requested_target is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("disposition", [
    ObservationDisposition.ABSENT, ObservationDisposition.REJECTED,
    ObservationDisposition.AMBIGUOUS, ObservationDisposition.FAULT,
])
def test_nonvalid_marker_is_not_returned(pipeline, monkeypatch, disposition):
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        pipeline._detector._marker_detector, "detect",
        lambda image: ArucoFrameDetection(disposition),
    )
    assert pipeline._process_frame(_frame(), target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")) is None


def test_degenerate_marker_is_logged(pipeline, monkeypatch, caplog):
    marker_detector = pipeline._detector._marker_detector
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        marker_detector,
        "_detector",
        SimpleNamespace(detectMarkers=lambda image: (
            [[(240.0, 300.0)] * 4], [[marker_detector.expected_id]], None,
        )),
    )
    assert pipeline._process_frame(_frame(), target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")) is None
    assert "Pose ArUco inexploitable" in caplog.text


@pytest.fixture
def live_pipeline(monkeypatch):
    frames = queue.Queue()
    first_capture = threading.Event()
    second_capture = threading.Event()
    wait_started = threading.Event()

    class Camera:
        captures = 0

        def __init__(self, index):
            pass

        def create_preview_configuration(self, **kwargs):
            return kwargs

        def configure(self, config):
            pass

        def start(self):
            pass

        def capture_array(self):
            self.captures += 1
            (first_capture if self.captures == 1 else second_capture).set()
            frame = frames.get(timeout=3)
            if isinstance(frame, Exception):
                raise frame
            return cv2.rotate(frame, cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE)

        def stop(self):
            pass

        def close(self):
            pass

    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "Picamera2", Camera)
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings", _runtime_settings)
    pipeline = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(poll_interval_s=0)
    original_wait = pipeline._frame_condition.wait_for

    def wait_for(predicate, timeout=None):
        wait_started.set()
        return original_wait(predicate, timeout)

    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline._frame_condition, "wait_for", wait_for)
    try:
        assert first_capture.wait(2)
        yield pipeline, frames, second_capture, wait_started
    finally:
        pipeline._stop_event.set()
        frames.put(_frame())
        pipeline.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["marker", "tag"])
def test_live_target_switch_waits_for_matching_frame(live_pipeline, target):
    pipeline, frames, second_capture, wait_started = live_pipeline
    results = []
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, target)
    reader = threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(pipeline.detect(target=strategy)))
    reader.start()
    try:
        assert wait_started.wait(2)
        frames.put(_frame())
        assert second_capture.wait(2)
        assert reader.is_alive()
        assert results == []
        frames.put(_frame())
        reader.join(2)
        assert not reader.is_alive()
        assert results[0].label == "marker_1"
        assert results[0].distance_cm == pytest.approx(
            8 / 79 * math.sqrt(600**2 + 0.5**2 + 0.5**2),
        )
    finally:
        with pipeline._frame_condition:
            pipeline._stop_event.set()
            pipeline._frame_condition.notify_all()
        reader.join(2)


def test_target_frame_wait_is_bounded_and_logged(live_pipeline, caplog, monkeypatch):
    pipeline, _, _, _ = live_pipeline
    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline, "TARGET_FRAME_TIMEOUT_S", 0.03)
    started = time.monotonic()
    assert pipeline.detect(target=camera_target(pipeline, "marker")) is None
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
    assert 0.025 <= elapsed < 0.5
    assert "Délai dépassé" in caplog.text


@pytest.mark.parametrize("present", [False, True])
def test_matching_frame_is_cached_including_absence(live_pipeline, present, monkeypatch):
    pipeline, frames, second_capture, wait_started = live_pipeline
    results = []
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, "marker")
    reader = threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(pipeline.detect(target=strategy)))
    reader.start()
    try:
        assert wait_started.wait(2)
        frames.put(_frame())
        assert second_capture.wait(2)
        frames.put(_frame() if present else np.full((640, 480, 3), 255, np.uint8))
        reader.join(2)
        assert not reader.is_alive()
        monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline, "TARGET_FRAME_TIMEOUT_S", 0)
        cached = pipeline.detect(target=camera_target(pipeline, "tag"))
        assert (cached is not None) == present
        assert (results[0] is not None) == present
        assert pipeline._frame_revision == pipeline._target_revision
        if present:
            assert results[0].cached is False
            assert cached.cached is True
            with pipeline._frame_condition:
                previous = pipeline._last_detection
                assert previous.cached is False
                frames.put(_frame())
                assert pipeline._frame_condition.wait_for(
                    lambda: pipeline._last_detection is not previous, timeout=2,
                )
            fresh = pipeline.detect(target=strategy)
            assert fresh is not None
            assert fresh.cached is False
            assert fresh.bbox_px == results[0].bbox_px
            assert pipeline.detect(target=strategy).cached is True
    finally:
        with pipeline._frame_condition:
            pipeline._stop_event.set()
            pipeline._frame_condition.notify_all()
        reader.join(2)


def test_sighting_is_not_lost_when_next_frame_is_empty(live_pipeline, monkeypatch):
    pipeline, frames, second_capture, wait_started = live_pipeline
    results = []
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, "marker")
    reader = threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(pipeline.detect(target=strategy)))
    reader.start()
    try:
        assert wait_started.wait(2)
        frames.put(_frame())
        assert second_capture.wait(2)
        frames.put(_frame())
        reader.join(2)
        assert not reader.is_alive()
        monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline, "TARGET_FRAME_TIMEOUT_S", 0)
        for image in (_frame(), np.full((640, 480, 3), 255, np.uint8)):
            with pipeline._frame_condition:
                sequence = pipeline._frame_sequence
                frames.put(image)
                assert pipeline._frame_condition.wait_for(
                    lambda: pipeline._frame_sequence > sequence, timeout=2,
                )
        assert pipeline._last_detection is None
        sighting = pipeline.detect(target=strategy)
        assert sighting is not None
        assert sighting.cached is False
        assert pipeline.detect(target=strategy) is None
    finally:
        with pipeline._frame_condition:
            pipeline._stop_event.set()
            pipeline._frame_condition.notify_all()
        reader.join(2)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", [False, True])
def test_target_frame_wait_is_released_on_close_or_failure(live_pipeline, failure, caplog):
    pipeline, frames, _, wait_started = live_pipeline
    results = []
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, "marker")
    reader = threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(pipeline.detect(target=strategy)))
    reader.start()
    try:
        assert wait_started.wait(2)
        if failure:
            frames.put(RuntimeError("capture interrompue"))
        else:
            with pytest.raises(TimeoutError):
                pipeline.close(timeout_s=0)
        reader.join(0.5)
        assert not reader.is_alive()
        assert results == [None]
        if failure:
            assert "capture interrompue" in caplog.text
    finally:
        with pipeline._frame_condition:
            pipeline._stop_event.set()
            pipeline._frame_condition.notify_all()
        reader.join(2)

### FILE: tests/unit/test_camera_color_format.py
import importlib
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.tools import calibrate_distance
from irobot.common.hardware.camera import camera_live_perception_pipeline
from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings, load_runtime_settings

from irobot.common.hardware.camera.frame_enhancer import FrameEnhancer
from tests.unit.perception_helpers import camera_target

SETTINGS = load_runtime_settings()
CAPTURE_W, CAPTURE_H = SETTINGS.camera_capture_size_px
ORIENTED_W, ORIENTED_H = SETTINGS.camera_oriented_frame_size_px

CAPTURE_SIZE_PX = [CAPTURE_W, CAPTURE_H]
ORIENTED_FRAME_SIZE_PX = [ORIENTED_W, ORIENTED_H]
EXPECTED_MAIN_CONFIG = {"size": (CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[0], CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[1]), "format": "BGR888"}

CAMERA_MODEL = "ov5647"
EXPOSURE_CONTROL_RANGE = (-8.0, 8.0, 0.0)
EXPOSURE_VALUE = 1.0
CLAHE_ENABLED = True
EXPOSURE_ERROR_MESSAGE = "exposure rejected"

MAX_CAPTURES = 3
CAPTURE_ERROR_MESSAGE = "La calibration ne détecte pas la cible synthétique rouge."
RED_LEVEL_DEFAULT = 255
RED_LEVEL_ENHANCER = 78
DISC_CENTER_XY = (CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[0] // 2, CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[1] // 2)
DISC_RADIUS_PX = 25
# Après cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE, x devient la ligne et y est inversé en colonne (hauteur brute - 1 - y).
DISC_CENTER_ROTATED_RC = (DISC_CENTER_XY[0], CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[1] - 1 - DISC_CENTER_XY[1])

COLOR = "red"
BALL_DIAMETER_CM = "5"
KNOWN_DISTANCE_CM = "70"
SAMPLES = "1"
EXPECTED_BALL_DIAMETER_PX = 2 * DISC_RADIUS_PX
DIAMETER_REL_TOLERANCE = 0.05
EXPECTED_LABEL = "red_ball"

PREVIEW_ONLY_ARG = "--preview-only"
PREVIEW_MESSAGE = "Aperçu caméra uniquement"
FOCAL_OUTPUT = "focal_px ="
DISPLAY_VALUE = ":test"
QUIT_KEY = ord("q")

@pytest.fixture
def camera_factory():
    class Camera:
        camera_controls = {"ExposureValue": EXPOSURE_CONTROL_RANGE}

        @staticmethod
        def global_camera_info():
            return [{"Model": CAMERA_MODEL}]

        def __init__(self, index):
            self.config = None
            self.stopped = False
            self.closed = False
            self.captures = 0
            self.on_capture = lambda: None
            self.controls = {}
            self.red_level = RED_LEVEL_DEFAULT
            self.calls = []

        def create_preview_configuration(self, **kwargs):
            return kwargs

        def configure(self, config):
            self.config = config
            self.calls.append("configure")

        def start(self):
            self.calls.append("start")

        def set_controls(self, controls):
            self.controls.update(controls)
            self.calls.append("exposure")

        def capture_array(self):
            self.captures += 1
            if self.captures > MAX_CAPTURES:
                raise RuntimeError(CAPTURE_ERROR_MESSAGE)
            frame = np.zeros((ORIENTED_FRAME_SIZE_PX[0], ORIENTED_FRAME_SIZE_PX[1], 3), dtype=np.uint8)
            # Ordre mémoire Picamera2 : BGR888 -> RGB, RGB888 -> BGR.
            red = (
                (self.red_level, 0, 0) if self.config["main"]["format"] == "BGR888"
                else (0, 0, self.red_level)
            )
            cv2.circle(frame, DISC_CENTER_XY, DISC_RADIUS_PX, red, -1)
            self.on_capture()
            return frame

        def stop(self):
            self.stopped = True

        def close(self):
            self.closed = True

    return Camera


def test_calibration_detects_red_from_picamera_memory_layout(monkeypatch, camera_factory, capsys):
    camera = camera_factory(0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(calibrate_distance, "Picamera2", lambda index: camera)

    calibrate_distance.main([
        "--color", COLOR,
        "--ball-diameter-cm", BALL_DIAMETER_CM,
        "--known-distance-cm", KNOWN_DISTANCE_CM,
        "--samples", SAMPLES,
    ])

    assert camera.config["main"] == EXPECTED_MAIN_CONFIG
    assert camera.controls == {"ExposureValue": EXPOSURE_VALUE}
    assert FOCAL_OUTPUT in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert camera.stopped and camera.closed


def test_runtime_detects_red_from_picamera_memory_layout(monkeypatch, camera_factory):
    camera = camera_factory(0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "Picamera2", lambda index: camera)
    pipeline = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    strategy = camera_target(pipeline, COLOR)
    pipeline.detect(target=strategy)
    camera.on_capture = pipeline._stop_event.set

    pipeline._capture_loop()
    detection = pipeline.detect(target=strategy)

    assert camera.config["main"] == EXPECTED_MAIN_CONFIG
    assert camera.controls == {"ExposureValue": EXPOSURE_VALUE}
    assert detection is not None
    assert detection.label == EXPECTED_LABEL
    assert detection.diameter_px == pytest.approx(EXPECTED_BALL_DIAMETER_PX, rel=DIAMETER_REL_TOLERANCE)
    assert detection.distance_cm is not None
    assert camera.stopped and camera.closed


def _entrypoint(monkeypatch, name, camera):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DISPLAY", DISPLAY_VALUE)
    class Factory:
        global_camera_info = staticmethod(lambda: [{"Model": CAMERA_MODEL}])

        def __new__(cls, index):
            return camera

    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "picamera2", SimpleNamespace(Picamera2=Factory))
    module_name = (
        "irobot.tools.vision_diagnostic"
        if name == "vision_diagnostic"
        else f"irobot.tools.{name}"
    )
    module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    if hasattr(module, "Picamera2"):
        monkeypatch.setattr(module, "Picamera2", Factory)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: RuntimeSettings(camera_clahe_enabled=CLAHE_ENABLED, camera_exposure_value=EXPOSURE_VALUE),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "imshow", lambda *args: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "waitKey", lambda *args: QUIT_KEY)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "destroyAllWindows", lambda: None)
    return module


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["calibrate_distance", "vision_diagnostic"])
def test_camera_tools_apply_shared_rgb_enhancer(monkeypatch, camera_factory, name, capsys):
    camera = camera_factory(0)
    camera.red_level = RED_LEVEL_ENHANCER
    module = _entrypoint(monkeypatch, name, camera)
    calls = []
    original_apply = FrameEnhancer.apply

    def apply(enhancer, frame):
        calls.append(frame.copy())
        return original_apply(enhancer, frame)

    monkeypatch.setattr(FrameEnhancer, "apply", apply)
    if name == "calibrate_distance":
        module.main(["--color", COLOR, "--known-distance-cm", KNOWN_DISTANCE_CM, "--samples", SAMPLES])
        assert FOCAL_OUTPUT in capsys.readouterr().out
    else:
        module.main([PREVIEW_ONLY_ARG])
        assert PREVIEW_MESSAGE in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert calls[0].shape == (CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[0], CAPTURE_SIZE_PX[1], 3)
    assert calls[0][DISC_CENTER_ROTATED_RC].tolist() == [RED_LEVEL_ENHANCER, 0, 0]
    assert camera.calls == ["configure", "exposure", "start"]
    assert camera.stopped and camera.closed


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["calibrate_distance", "vision_diagnostic"])
def test_exposure_failure_releases_camera(monkeypatch, camera_factory, name):
    camera = camera_factory(0)
    module = _entrypoint(monkeypatch, name, camera)

    def fail(controls):
        raise RuntimeError(EXPOSURE_ERROR_MESSAGE)

    monkeypatch.setattr(camera, "set_controls", fail)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=EXPOSURE_ERROR_MESSAGE):
        if name == "calibrate_distance":
            module.main([])
        else:
            module.main([PREVIEW_ONLY_ARG])
    assert camera.calls == ["configure"]
    assert camera.captures == 0
    assert camera.closed


def test_exposure_failure_is_logged_and_releases_runtime_camera(monkeypatch, camera_factory, caplog):
    camera = camera_factory(0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "Picamera2", lambda index: camera)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: RuntimeSettings(camera_exposure_value=EXPOSURE_VALUE),
    )

    def fail(controls):
        raise RuntimeError(EXPOSURE_ERROR_MESSAGE)

    monkeypatch.setattr(camera, "set_controls", fail)
    pipeline = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    pipeline._capture_loop()
    assert pipeline.detect(target=camera_target(pipeline, COLOR)) is None
    assert camera.closed
    assert camera.captures == 0
    assert EXPOSURE_ERROR_MESSAGE in caplog.text

### FILE: tests/unit/test_camera_pipeline_shutdown.py
import threading

import pytest

from irobot.common.hardware.camera import camera_live_perception_pipeline as camera_module
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot


def test_close_waits_for_camera_initialization_and_releases_resources(monkeypatch):
    initializing = threading.Event()
    release = threading.Event()
    calls = []

    class Camera:
        camera_controls = {"ExposureValue": (-8.0, 8.0, 0.0)}

        def __init__(self, index):
            pass

        def create_preview_configuration(self, **kwargs):
            return {}

        def configure(self, config):
            initializing.set()
            if not release.wait(5):
                raise TimeoutError("test camera initialization")

        def start(self):
            calls.append("start")

        def set_controls(self, controls):
            assert controls == {"ExposureValue": 1.0}

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def close(self):
            calls.append("close")

    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_module, "Picamera2", Camera)
    pipeline = camera_module.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline()
    try:
        assert initializing.wait(2)
        assert pipeline._thread.daemon is False
        with pytest.raises(TimeoutError, match="thread caméra"):
            pipeline.close(timeout_s=0)
        assert pipeline._thread.is_alive()
    finally:
        release.set()
        pipeline.close()

    assert not pipeline._thread.is_alive()
    assert calls == ["start", "stop", "close"]
    pipeline.close()
    assert calls == ["start", "stop", "close"]


def test_capture_failure_is_logged_and_camera_is_released(monkeypatch, caplog):
    calls = []

    class Camera:
        def __init__(self, index):
            pass

        def create_preview_configuration(self, **kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("configuration invalide")

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def close(self):
            calls.append("close")

    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_module, "Picamera2", Camera)
    pipeline = camera_module.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline()
    pipeline.close()

    assert not pipeline._thread.is_alive()
    assert calls == ["stop", "close"]
    assert "configuration invalide" in caplog.text


def test_runtime_close_stops_robot_and_leaves_hardware_to_the_entry_point(tmp_path):
    class Perception:
        def close(self):
            raise AssertionError("la caméra est fermée par app.main()")

    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, perception=Perception(), use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    runtime.close()

    assert robot.commands[-1] == "stop"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_continuous_tracking.py
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent
from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.follow_object import FollowObjectSkill
from irobot.common.domain.skills.base_skill import SkillResult
from irobot.common.interfaces.robot_controller import RobotController
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime

from tests.unit.test_follow_object import World, run


class ContinuousWorld(World):
    def __init__(self, *, stop_at=650.0, hidden=(1.0, 100.0)):
        super().__init__(distance_cm=200.0, hidden=hidden)
        self.stop_at = stop_at
        self.targets = []

    def emergency_stop_requested(self):
        return self.now >= self.stop_at or self.emergency

    def detect(self, *, target):
        self.targets.append(target)
        return super().detect(target=target)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("name", "target"),
    [("follow_person", "marker"), ("follow_red_ball", "red_ball"), ("follow_object", "yellow")],
)
def test_continuous_tracking_survives_old_limits_until_stop(tmp_path, monkeypatch, name, target):
    world = ContinuousWorld()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(
        robot=RobotController(world, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep),
        perception=world,
        use_llm=False,
        trace_dir=str(tmp_path),
    )
    runtime.command_service.settings = RuntimeSettings(
        min_stand_off_cm=10.0, stand_off_default_cm=20.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=50.0,
    )
    runtime.command_service.controller.planner.registry.register(
        FollowObjectSkill(settings=runtime.command_service.settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep)
    )
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.time_fn = world.time
    trace_path = tmp_path / "continuous.jsonl"

    planned = runtime.command_service.planner.plan_intents(
        [Intent(name=name, target=target)]
    )
    steps, status = runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.execute(
        planned, runtime.command_service.controller.handle_planned_step,
        stop_requested=runtime.command_service._emergency_stop_requested,
        on_event=lambda event: runtime.command_service._trace_mission_event(trace_path, event),
    )

    assert status == "aborted"
    assert steps[0]["reason"] == "emergency stop"
    assert steps[0]["attempt"] == 1
    assert steps[0]["plan"] == ["follow_object"]
    assert world.now >= 650
    assert set(world.targets) == {PerceptionTarget.from_value(target)}
    assert "right" in world.commands
    assert "forward" in world.commands
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"
    events = [json.loads(line) for line in trace_path.read_text().splitlines()]
    assert len([event for event in events if event["event"] == "step_result"]) == 1
    assert events[0]["results"][0]["data"]["emergency_stop"] is True


def test_permanently_missing_target_keeps_searching_until_stop():
    world = ContinuousWorld(stop_at=100, hidden=(0, 1000))
    result, _ = run(world)

    assert result.data["emergency_stop"] is True
    assert world.now >= 100
    assert "right" in world.commands
    assert "forward" not in world.commands
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_continuous_follow_resumes_after_holding_distance():
    class RecedingWorld(ContinuousWorld):
        def detect(self, *, target):
            if self.now >= 1 and not getattr(self, "moved", False):
                self.distance_cm = 200
                self.moved = True
            return super().detect(target=target)

    world = RecedingWorld(stop_at=3, hidden=(0, 0))
    world.distance_cm = 100
    result, _ = run(world)

    assert world.commands[0] == "stop"
    assert "forward" in world.commands
    assert result.data["emergency_stop"] is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["follow_person", "follow_red_ball", "follow_object"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("skill_status", ["failed", "in_progress"])
def test_tracking_failure_is_not_retried(tmp_path, monkeypatch, name, skill_status):
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=World(), use_llm=False)
    calls = []

    def execute(step):
        calls.append(step.intent)
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult(status=skill_status, message="camera error")]}

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service.controller, "handle_planned_step", execute)
    planned = runtime.command_service.planner.plan_intents([Intent(name=name)])[0]
    steps, _ = runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.execute(
        [planned], execute, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )
    result = steps[0]

    assert result["status"] == "failed"
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert result["results"][0].message == "camera error"


def test_continuous_tracking_requires_live_perception():
    world = World()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=world, use_llm=False)
    controller = runtime.command_service.controller
    step = controller.planner.plan_intents([Intent(name="follow_person", target="marker")])[0]
    result = controller.handle_planned_step(step)

    assert result["results"][0].status == "failed"
    assert "perception" in result["results"][0].data["error"]
    assert set(world.commands) == {"stop"}


def test_continuous_tracking_stops_on_obstacle():
    world = ContinuousWorld()
    world.obstacle_detected = True
    result, _ = run(world)

    assert result.data["obstacle_detected"] is True
    assert world.now == 0
    assert set(world.commands) == {"stop"}


def test_continuous_tracking_stops_on_camera_error():
    world = ContinuousWorld()
    world.detect_error = RuntimeError("camera down")
    result, _ = run(world)

    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert result.data["error"] == "camera down"
    assert set(world.commands) == {"stop"}

### FILE: tests/unit/test_directional_calibration.py
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.domain.skills import SkillContext
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.follow_object import FollowObjectSkill
from irobot.common.domain.skills.primitives.move_backward import MoveBackwardSkill
from irobot.common.domain.skills.primitives.move_forward import MoveForwardSkill
from irobot.common.domain.skills.primitives.turn_left import TurnLeftSkill
from irobot.common.domain.skills.primitives.turn_right import TurnRightSkill


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("skill", "parameters", "expected"),
    [
        (MoveForwardSkill(), {"distance": 20}, 20 / 55),
        (MoveBackwardSkill(), {"distance": 10}, 10 / 25),
        (MoveBackwardSkill(), {"distance": 0.1, "unit": "m"}, 10 / 25),
        (MoveBackwardSkill(), {"duration": 1}, 1),
        (TurnLeftSkill(), {"angle": 45}, 0.25),
        (TurnRightSkill(), {"angle": 90}, 0.5),
    ],
)
def test_directional_motion_duration(skill, parameters, expected):
    skill.settings = RuntimeSettings(cm_per_second=55, backward_cm_per_second=25, deg_per_second=180)
    context = SkillContext(state={"active_intent_parameters": parameters})

    assert skill._resolve_motion_parameters(context)["duration"] == pytest.approx(expected)


def test_missing_backward_calibration_preserves_shared_speed():
    skill = MoveBackwardSkill()
    skill.settings = RuntimeSettings(cm_per_second=50, backward_cm_per_second=None)
    context = SkillContext(state={"active_intent_parameters": {"distance": 10}})

    assert skill._resolve_motion_parameters(context)["duration"] == pytest.approx(0.2)


def test_follow_uses_directional_speed_and_preserves_pulse_limits():
    skill = FollowObjectSkill(settings=RuntimeSettings(cm_per_second=55, backward_cm_per_second=25))

    assert skill._step(3, 0.05, 0.25) == pytest.approx(3 / 55)
    assert skill._step(3, 0.05, 0.15, backward=True) == pytest.approx(0.12)
    assert skill._step(100, 0.05, 0.15, backward=True) == 0.15

### FILE: tests/unit/test_distance_estimator.py
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config import settings
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import color_detector
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.camera_live_perception_pipeline import (
    CameraLivePerceptionPipeline,
    ExponentialSmoother,
)
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import ColorDetector, Detector
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.distance_estimator import estimate_distance_cm
from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget
from irobot.common.interfaces.perception_contracts import (
    ObjectDetection,
)
from irobot.tools.calibrate_distance import focal_from_known_distance
from tests.unit.perception_helpers import camera_target


def test_estimate_distance_known_values():
    assert estimate_distance_cm(39.0, 6.5, 600.0) == pytest.approx(100.0)
    assert estimate_distance_cm(78.0, 6.5, 600.0) == pytest.approx(50.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("diameter", [0, -5.0, None, float("nan"), float("inf"), "abc"])
def test_estimate_distance_invalid_diameter(diameter):
    assert estimate_distance_cm(diameter, 6.5, 600.0) is None


def test_estimate_distance_invalid_calibration():
    assert estimate_distance_cm(40.0, 0.0, 600.0) is None
    assert estimate_distance_cm(40.0, 6.5, -1.0) is None


def test_focal_from_known_distance_roundtrip():
    focal = focal_from_known_distance(39.0, 6.5, 100.0)
    assert focal == pytest.approx(600.0)
    assert estimate_distance_cm(39.0, 6.5, focal) == pytest.approx(100.0)
    assert focal_from_known_distance(0.0, 6.5, 100.0) is None


def test_smoother_exponential_and_reset():
    smoother = ExponentialSmoother(alpha=0.4)
    assert smoother.update(100.0) == 100.0
    assert smoother.update(200.0) == pytest.approx(140.0)
    assert smoother.update(None) is None
    assert smoother.update(50.0) == 50.0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ExponentialSmoother(alpha=0.0)


def _pipeline(**kwargs) -> CameraLivePerceptionPipeline:
    return CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False, ball_diameter_cm=6.5, focal_px=600.0, **kwargs)


def _ball(position: str = "center", diameter_px: float = 39.0) -> ObjectDetection:
    distance = estimate_distance_cm(diameter_px, 6.5, 600.0)
    return ObjectDetection(
        label="yellow_ball", position=position, confidence=0.95, diameter_px=diameter_px,
        distance_cm=distance, distance_visual_cm=distance,
    )


def _publish(pipe: CameraLivePerceptionPipeline, detection: ObjectDetection) -> None:
    pipe._last_detection = detection


def test_color_detector_returns_visual_distance_and_common_contract():
    detector = ColorDetector("yellow", ball_diameter_cm=6.5, focal_px=600.0)
    detection = detector.detect(_synthetic_frame(40))
    assert isinstance(detector, Detector)
    assert detection is not None
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(600.0 * 6.5 / detection.diameter_px)
    assert detection.distance_visual_cm == detection.distance_cm
    assert detection.bbox_px is not None


def test_pipeline_smooths_color_detector_distance_and_resets_on_lost_target():
    pipe = _pipeline()
    strategy = camera_target(pipe, "yellow")
    first = pipe._process_frame(_synthetic_frame(40), target=strategy)
    second = pipe._process_frame(_synthetic_frame(80), target=strategy)
    assert first.distance_cm == pytest.approx(600.0 * 6.5 / first.diameter_px)
    assert second.distance_cm == pytest.approx(
        0.4 * (600.0 * 6.5 / second.diameter_px)
        + 0.6 * (600.0 * 6.5 / first.diameter_px),
    )
    assert pipe._process_frame(np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8), target=strategy) is None
    third = pipe._process_frame(_synthetic_frame(40), target=strategy)
    assert third.distance_cm == pytest.approx(600.0 * 6.5 / third.diameter_px)


def test_pipeline_smooths_distance_for_any_strategy_and_resets_on_lost_detection():
    class ScriptedDetector:
        def __init__(self):
            second_detection = ObjectDetection(distance_cm=200.0, distance_visual_cm=200.0)
            self.second_detection = second_detection
            self.original_detections = [
                ObjectDetection(distance_cm=100.0, distance_visual_cm=100.0),
                second_detection,
                None,
                ObjectDetection(distance_cm=50.0, distance_visual_cm=50.0),
            ]

        def detect(self, frame_rgb, target):
            return self.original_detections.pop(0)

        def reset(self, target):
            pass

    detector = ScriptedDetector()
    pipe = _pipeline(detector=detector)
    frame = np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8)
    target = PerceptionTarget.FACE

    first = pipe._process_frame(frame, target=target)
    second = pipe._process_frame(frame, target=target)
    assert first.distance_cm == 100.0
    assert second.distance_cm == pytest.approx(140.0)
    assert second.distance_visual_cm == pytest.approx(140.0)
    assert detector.second_detection.distance_cm == 200.0
    assert pipe._process_frame(frame, target=target) is None
    after_loss = pipe._process_frame(frame, target=target)
    assert after_loss.distance_cm == 50.0


def test_detect_returns_visual_distance_without_mutating_published_detection():
    pipe = _pipeline()
    strategy = camera_target(pipe, "yellow")
    pipe.detect(target=strategy)
    _publish(pipe, _ball())
    assert pipe.detect(target=strategy).distance_cm == pytest.approx(100.0)
    assert pipe._last_detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(100.0)


def _synthetic_frame(radius: int, rotate: bool = False):
    frame = np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8)
    # jaune en RGB
    import cv2

    cv2.circle(frame, (320, 240), radius, (255, 220, 0), -1)
    return cv2.rotate(frame, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE) if rotate else frame


def test_detector_diameter_is_rotation_invariant_and_selects_largest_ball():
    frame = _synthetic_frame(40)
    expected = 80.0
    for rotated in (frame, _synthetic_frame(40, rotate=True)):
        cands = color_detector.detect_colored_balls(rotated, "yellow", is_rgb=True)
        assert len(cands) == 1
        assert cands[0]["diameter_px"] == pytest.approx(expected, rel=0.05)
        selected = color_detector.select_largest_ball(cands)
        assert selected == cands[0]
        assert selected["diameter_px"] == cands[0]["diameter_px"]


def test_ball_selection_returns_largest_ball_or_none():
    candidates = [
        {"bbox": (40, 40, 10, 10), "area": 400, "diameter_px": 23},
        {"bbox": (70, 70, 15, 15), "area": 500, "diameter_px": 25},
    ]
    assert color_detector.select_largest_ball(candidates) is candidates[1]
    assert color_detector.select_largest_ball(candidates[:1]) is candidates[0]
    assert color_detector.select_largest_ball([]) is None


def test_pipeline_process_frame_end_to_end():
    pipe = _pipeline()
    strategy = camera_target(pipe, "yellow")
    detection = pipe._process_frame(_synthetic_frame(40), target=strategy)
    assert detection is not None and detection.label == "yellow_ball"
    assert detection.diameter_px == pytest.approx(80.0, rel=0.05)
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(600.0 * 6.5 / detection.diameter_px)
    assert pipe._process_frame(np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8), target=strategy) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("center_x", "position"), [
    (159, "left"), (160, "center"), (320, "center"), (321, "right"),
])
def test_pipeline_preserves_horizontal_position_thresholds(monkeypatch, center_x, position):
    pipe = _pipeline()
    candidate = {
        "bbox": (center_x - 10, 100, 20, 20),
        "area": 300.0, "diameter_px": 20.0,
    }
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        color_detector, "detect_colored_balls", lambda *args, **kwargs: [candidate],
    )
    detection = pipe._process_frame(
        np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8), target=camera_target(pipe, "red")
    )
    assert detection is not None
    assert detection.position == position


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", [None, "", "blue", "object"])
def test_unknown_target_is_rejected_before_selecting_a_strategy(target):
    assert PerceptionTarget.from_value(target) is None


def test_color_target_does_not_try_other_color(monkeypatch):
    pipe = _pipeline()
    red_target = camera_target(pipe, "red")
    color_detector = pipe._detector._color_detectors[red_target]
    monkeypatch.setattr(color_detector, "detect", lambda image: None)
    yellow_target = camera_target(pipe, "yellow")
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        pipe._detector._color_detectors[yellow_target],
        "detect",
        lambda image: pytest.fail("Red strategy must not try yellow detection"),
    )

    assert pipe._process_frame(
        np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8),
        target=red_target,
    ) is None


def test_camera_section_in_toml(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    cfg = tmp_path / "c.toml"
    cfg.write_text("[camera]\nball_diameter_cm = 7.0\nfocal_px = 550.0\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)
    loaded = settings.load_runtime_settings()
    assert loaded.camera_ball_diameter_cm == 7.0
    assert loaded.camera_focal_px == 550.0

    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: Path("/tmp/nonexistent.toml"))
    default = settings.load_runtime_settings()
    assert (default.camera_ball_diameter_cm, default.camera_focal_px) == (6.2, 1394.2)

### FILE: tests/unit/test_emergency_stop.py
from __future__ import annotations

import threading
import time

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget
from irobot.common.domain.skills import SkillContext
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.follow_object import FollowObjectSkill
from irobot.common.interfaces.robot_controller import RobotController
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot
from tests.unit.test_follow_object import World



def test_controller_event_and_underlying_detector():
    fake = FakeRobot()
    controller = RobotController(fake)
    assert controller.emergency_stop_requested() is False

    controller.request_emergency_stop()
    assert controller.emergency_stop_requested() is True
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"

    controller.clear_emergency_stop()
    assert controller.emergency_stop_requested() is False

    fake.stop_requested = True
    assert controller.emergency_stop_requested() is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize("params", [{}, {"duration_s": 300}])
def test_stop_from_other_thread_interrupts_follow_object(params):
    world = World(distance_cm=100.0)
    controller = RobotController(world)
    started = threading.Event()

    def sleep(seconds: float) -> None:
        world.now += seconds
        started.set()
        time.sleep(0.0005)

    context = SkillContext(
        robot=controller,
        perception=world,
        perception_target=PerceptionTarget.RED_BALL,
        state={"active_intent_parameters": params},
    )
    skill = FollowObjectSkill(settings=RuntimeSettings(), time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=sleep)

    def stopper():
        started.wait(5)
        controller.request_emergency_stop()

    thread = threading.Thread(target=stopper)
    thread.start()
    result = skill.execute(context)
    thread.join()

    assert result.data["emergency_stop"] is True
    assert world.now < 300
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"


class BlockingRuntime(VoiceRuntime):
    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(use_llm=False, wake_word="", **kwargs)
        self.texts: list[str] = []
        self.entered = threading.Event()
        self.flag_at_start: bool | None = None

    def handle_text_as_plan(self, text):
        self.texts.append(text)
        self.flag_at_start = self.robot.emergency_stop_requested()
        self.entered.set()
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
        while not self.robot.emergency_stop_requested() and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            time.sleep(0.001)
        return {"status": "done"}


def test_new_mission_clears_flag_and_stop_phrase_aborts():
    controller = RobotController(FakeRobot())
    controller.request_emergency_stop()
    runtime = BlockingRuntime(robot=controller)

    runtime.handle_transcript("suis la balle")
    assert runtime.entered.wait(2)
    assert runtime.flag_at_start is False

    runtime.handle_transcript("arrête-toi")
    runtime._worker.join(2)
    assert not runtime.mission_running()
    assert controller.emergency_stop_requested() is True
    assert runtime.texts == ["suis la balle"]


def test_non_stop_phrase_ignored_during_mission(capsys):
    controller = RobotController(FakeRobot())
    runtime = BlockingRuntime(robot=controller)

    runtime.handle_transcript("suis la balle")
    assert runtime.entered.wait(2)
    runtime.handle_transcript("avance de deux mètres")

    assert runtime.texts == ["suis la balle"]
    assert controller.emergency_stop_requested() is False
    assert "ignorée" in capsys.readouterr().out
    runtime.close()


def test_close_stops_robot_and_joins_worker():
    fake = FakeRobot()
    runtime = BlockingRuntime(robot=RobotController(fake))
    runtime.handle_transcript("suis la balle")
    assert runtime.entered.wait(2)

    runtime.close()

    assert not runtime.mission_running()
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_sync_path_runs_inline():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=RobotController(FakeRobot()), use_llm=False, threaded=False, wake_word="")
    result = runtime.handle_transcript("stop maintenant")
    assert result is None
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "aborted"
    assert runtime.robot.emergency_stop_requested()
    assert not runtime.mission_running()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", [
    "STOP !", "arrête-toi", "halte maintenant", "pause",
    "avance puis stop et recule", "suis un chat mais arrête",
])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("threaded", [False, True])
def test_stop_is_immediate_even_without_a_mission(tmp_path, monkeypatch, text, threaded):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=fake, trace_dir=str(tmp_path), threaded=threaded, wake_word="")
    calls = []
    original_request = runtime._request_emergency_stop

    def request():
        calls.append("request")
        original_request()

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, "_request_emergency_stop", request)
    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service.parser, "parse", lambda _: pytest.fail("parser called"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service, "_ask_plan", lambda _: pytest.fail("LLM called"))
    assert runtime.handle_transcript(text) is None
    assert calls == ["request"]
    assert fake.commands[0] == "stop"
    assert runtime.command_service._emergency_stop_requested()
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "aborted"
    assert runtime._worker is None


def test_stop_during_llm_planning_prevents_all_motion(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent

    fake = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=RobotController(fake), trace_dir=str(tmp_path), wake_word="")
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()

    def plan(_):
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(2)
        return [Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"duration": 1})], {"source": "llm_plan"}

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service, "_ask_plan", plan)
    try:
        runtime.handle_transcript("avance")
        assert entered.wait(2)
        start = time.monotonic()
        runtime.handle_transcript("stop puis recule")
        assert time.monotonic() - start < 0.1
        assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"
    finally:
        release.set()
        runtime.close()
    assert runtime.last_result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert runtime.last_result["steps"] == []
    assert "forward" not in fake.commands
    assert not runtime.mission_running()


def test_stop_does_not_wait_for_blocked_ultrasound(tmp_path):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    controller = RobotController(fake)
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=controller, use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path), wake_word="")
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()

    def obstacle():
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(2)
        return False

    fake.check_obstacle = obstacle
    try:
        runtime.handle_transcript("avance pendant cinq secondes puis recule")
        assert entered.wait(2)
        start = time.monotonic()
        runtime.handle_transcript("halte")
        assert time.monotonic() - start < 0.1
        assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"
    finally:
        release.set()
        runtime.close()
    assert not runtime.mission_running()
    assert runtime.last_result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert "forward" not in fake.commands
    assert "backward" not in fake.commands


def test_motion_cannot_restart_after_stop_during_initial_sensor_read():
    fake = FakeRobot()
    controller = RobotController(fake)

    def obstacle():
        controller.request_emergency_stop()
        return False

    fake.check_obstacle = obstacle
    assert controller.forward_for(5) == "emergency_stop"
    assert "forward" not in fake.commands
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_stop_cancels_timed_motion_and_remaining_steps(tmp_path):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    now = 0.0

    def sleep(seconds):
        nonlocal now
        now += seconds
        runtime.handle_transcript("pause")

    controller = RobotController(fake, time_fn=lambda: now, sleep_fn=sleep)
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(
        robot=controller, use_llm=False, threaded=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path), wake_word="",
    )
    result = runtime.handle_transcript("avance pendant cinq secondes puis recule")
    assert result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert result["mission"]["phase"] == "aborted"
    assert len(result["steps"]) == 1
    assert result["steps"][0]["attempt"] == 1
    assert now <= controller.CONTROL_LOOP_PERIOD_S
    assert fake.commands.count("forward") == 1
    assert "backward" not in fake.commands
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("duration", [-1, float("inf"), float("nan"), True])
def test_invalid_duration_stops_and_raises(duration):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duration"):
        RobotController(fake).forward_for(duration)
    assert fake.commands == ["stop"]


def test_stop_failure_still_requests_cancellation(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=fake, trace_dir=str(tmp_path), wake_word="")
    calls = []

    def stop():
        calls.append("stop")
        raise OSError("driver stop failed")

    def request():
        calls.append("request")
        runtime._emergency_event.set()

    monkeypatch.setattr(fake, "stop", stop)
