    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime, "_request_emergency_stop", request)
    with pytest.raises(OSError, match="driver stop failed"):
        runtime.handle_transcript("stop")
    assert calls == ["stop", "request"]
    assert runtime.command_service._emergency_stop_requested()
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "aborted"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["error", "unavailable"])
def test_ultrasound_failure_is_logged_and_prevents_motion(caplog, mode):
    fake = FakeRobot()

    def obstacle():
        if mode == "error":
            raise OSError("sensor failed")
        return None

    fake.check_obstacle = obstacle
    assert RobotController(fake).forward_for(5) == "obstacle"
    assert fake.commands == ["stop"]
    assert caplog.records


def test_wake_word_gates_commands_but_not_stop(tmp_path):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=fake, use_llm=False, threaded=False, wake_word="robot", trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    assert runtime.handle_transcript("avance") is None
    assert fake.commands == []

    assert runtime.handle_transcript("robot") is None
    assert runtime.handle_transcript("avance") is not None
    assert fake.commands

    fake.commands.clear()
    runtime.handle_transcript("stop")
    assert "stop" in fake.commands

### FILE: tests/unit/test_face_detector.py
import logging
from dataclasses import replace
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget
from irobot.common.domain.skills.base_skill import SkillContext
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.follow_object import FollowObjectSkill
from irobot.common.hardware.camera import camera_live_perception_pipeline
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import FaceDetector
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.frame_enhancer import FrameEnhancer
from irobot.common.interfaces.perception_contracts import (
    ObjectDetection,
    ObjectPerceptionPipeline,
)
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot
from tests.unit.perception_helpers import camera_target


def _faces(*boxes):
    return np.array([(*box[:4], *([0.0] * 10), box[4]) for box in boxes], dtype=np.float32)


@pytest.fixture
def yunet(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    model = tmp_path / "model.onnx"
    model.touch()
    settings = RuntimeSettings(
        camera_face_model_path=str(model),
        camera_face_grace_frames=2,
        camera_focal_px=600.0,
        camera_capture_size_px=(640, 480),
        camera_oriented_frame_size_px=(480, 640),
        camera_face_width_cm=15.0,
        camera_face_score_threshold=0.45,
        camera_clahe_enabled=False,
        camera_exposure_value=0.0,
    )

    class Net:
        faces = None
        inputs = []
        sizes = []
        creates = []

        def setInputSize(self, size):
            self.sizes.append(size)

        def detect(self, frame):
            self.inputs.append(frame.copy())
            if isinstance(self.faces, Exception):
                raise self.faces
            return 1, self.faces

    net = Net()

    def create(*args, **kwargs):
        net.creates.append((args, kwargs))
        return net

    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "FaceDetectorYN", SimpleNamespace(create=create))
    return settings, net


def test_yunet_configuration_and_rgb_input(yunet):
    settings, net = yunet
    detector = FaceDetector(settings)
    frame = np.full((640, 480, 3), (120, 10, 5), np.uint8)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None
    assert net.creates == [(
        (settings.camera_face_model_path, "", (300, 300)),
        {"score_threshold": 0.45, "nms_threshold": 0.3, "top_k": 5000},
    )]
    assert net.sizes == [(480, 640)]
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(net.inputs[0], cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))


def test_largest_eligible_face_and_distance(yunet):
    settings, net = yunet
    net.faces = _faces(
        (10, 20, 50, 80, 0.99),
        (180, 20, 100, 110, 0.75),
        (10, 20, 300, 300, 0.2),
    )
    detection = FaceDetector(settings).detect(np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8))
    assert detection.label == "face"
    assert detection.position == "center"
    assert detection.confidence == pytest.approx(0.75)
    assert detection.diameter_px == 100
    assert detection.distance_cm == 90
    assert detection.distance_visual_cm == 90


@pytest.mark.parametrize("center,position", [
    (159, "left"), (160, "center"), (320, "center"), (321, "right"),
])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("scale", [1, 2])
def test_position_thresholds_scale_with_frame_width(yunet, center, position, scale):
    settings, net = yunet
    net.faces = _faces(((center - 40) * scale, 20, 80 * scale, 80, 0.9))
    detection = FaceDetector(settings, focal_px=600 * scale).detect(
        np.zeros((640, 480 * scale, 3), np.uint8),
    )
    assert detection.position == position
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(112.5)


def test_custom_face_width_focal_and_score_threshold(yunet):
    settings, net = yunet
    settings = replace(settings, camera_face_width_cm=12, camera_face_score_threshold=0.8)
    net.faces = _faces((10, 20, 60, 80, 0.8), (150, 20, 100, 100, 0.79))
    detection = FaceDetector(settings, focal_px=500).detect(np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8))
    assert detection.diameter_px == 60
    assert detection.distance_cm == 100


def test_grace_expires_after_exactly_n_missing_frames(yunet):
    settings, net = yunet
    detector = FaceDetector(settings)
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    first = detector.detect(frame)
    assert detector.bounding_box == (180, 20, 100, 100)
    assert detector.is_cached is False
    net.faces = None
    assert detector.detect(frame) == replace(first, cached=True)
    assert detector.bounding_box == (180, 20, 100, 100)
    assert detector.is_cached is True
    assert detector.detect(frame) == replace(first, cached=True)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None
    assert detector.bounding_box is None
    assert detector.is_cached is False
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("grace_frames", [0, 2])
def test_face_loss_logged_once_after_grace_and_again_after_reacquisition(yunet, caplog, grace_frames):
    settings, net = yunet
    detector = FaceDetector(replace(settings, camera_face_grace_frames=grace_frames))
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    caplog.set_level(logging.INFO, logger=FaceDetector.__module__)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None
    assert caplog.records == []

    for _ in range(2):
        net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
        assert detector.detect(frame) is not None
        caplog.clear()
        net.faces = None
        for _ in range(grace_frames):
            assert detector.detect(frame).cached is True
        assert caplog.records == []
        assert detector.detect(frame) is None
        assert detector.detect(frame) is None
        assert [(record.levelno, record.getMessage()) for record in caplog.records] == [
            (logging.INFO, f"Visage perdu : délai de grâce épuisé ({grace_frames} images)."),
        ]


def test_low_scores_and_empty_arrays_consume_grace(yunet):
    settings, net = yunet
    detector = FaceDetector(settings)
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    first = detector.detect(frame)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.3))
    assert detector.detect(frame) == replace(first, cached=True)
    net.faces = np.empty((0, 15), np.float32)
    assert detector.detect(frame) == replace(first, cached=True)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None


def test_reacquisition_restarts_grace_and_results_cannot_mutate_memory(yunet):
    settings, net = yunet
    detector = FaceDetector(settings)
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    first = detector.detect(frame)
    first.distance_cm = -1
    net.faces = None
    assert detector.detect(frame).distance_cm == 90
    net.faces = _faces((10, 20, 50, 80, 0.95))
    second = detector.detect(frame)
    assert second.position == "left"
    assert second.distance_cm == 180
    net.faces = None
    assert detector.detect(frame) == replace(second, cached=True)
    assert detector.detect(frame) == replace(second, cached=True)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action", ["reset", "resize", "disabled"])
def test_grace_reset_resize_and_disabled(yunet, action):
    settings, net = yunet
    if action == "disabled":
        settings = replace(settings, camera_face_grace_frames=0)
    detector = FaceDetector(settings)
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    assert detector.detect(frame) is not None
    net.faces = None
    if action == "reset":
        detector.reset()
    if action == "resize":
        frame = np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8)
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad_faces", [
    np.zeros((1, 14), np.float32),
    _faces((10, 20, 0, 80, 0.9)),
    _faces((10, 20, 80, -1, 0.9)),
    _faces((10, 20, 80, 80, float("nan"))),
    _faces((10, 20, 80, 80, 1.1)),
    cv2.error("synthetic inference failure"),
])
def test_faults_clear_memory_and_are_explicit(yunet, bad_faces, caplog):
    settings, net = yunet
    detector = FaceDetector(settings)
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    assert detector.detect(frame) is not None
    net.faces = bad_faces
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, cv2.error)):
        detector.detect(frame)
    assert "mémoire du visage effacée" in caplog.text
    net.faces = None
    assert detector.detect(frame) is None


def test_missing_model_fails_explicitly(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="YuNet introuvable"):
        FaceDetector(RuntimeSettings(camera_face_model_path=str(tmp_path / "missing.onnx")))


@pytest.fixture
def pipeline(yunet, monkeypatch):
    settings, net = yunet
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: replace(settings, camera_clahe_enabled=True),
    )
    return camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False), net


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["face", "human"])
def test_pipeline_face_routes_enhanced_frame_without_aruco_or_colors(pipeline, monkeypatch, target):
    camera, net = pipeline
    frame = np.full((640, 480, 3), (90, 10, 5), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    marker_strategy = camera_target(camera, "marker")
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        camera._detector._marker_detector, "detect",
        lambda image: pytest.fail("No ArUco for faces"),
    )
    for color in ("red", "yellow"):
        color_strategy = camera_target(camera, color)
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            camera._detector._color_detectors[color_strategy], "detect",
            lambda image: pytest.fail("No color detector for faces"),
        )
    strategy = camera_target(camera, target)
    assert camera.detect(target=strategy) is None
    detection = camera._process_frame(frame, target=strategy)
    camera._last_detection = detection
    assert camera.detect(target=strategy).label == "face"
    expected = FrameEnhancer(camera._settings).apply(frame)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(net.inputs[-1], cv2.cvtColor(expected, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
    assert len(net.creates) == 1
    assert camera._process_frame(frame, target=strategy) is not None
    assert len(net.creates) == 1


def test_face_aliases_do_not_change_request_revision(pipeline):
    camera, net = pipeline
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    face_strategy = camera_target(camera, "human")
    camera.detect(target=face_strategy)
    revision = camera._target_revision
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    camera._last_detection = camera._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(camera, "face"))
    assert camera.detect(target=camera_target(camera, "face")).label == "face"
    assert camera._target_revision == revision
    net.faces = None
    assert camera.detect(target=face_strategy) is not None
    assert camera._process_frame(frame, target=face_strategy).label == "face"


def test_changing_to_balls_clears_face_memory(pipeline):
    camera, net = pipeline
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    assert camera._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(camera, "face")) is not None
    camera._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(camera, "red"))
    net.faces = None
    assert camera._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(camera, "face")) is None

    for target in ("red", "yellow", "marker", "tag"):
        camera._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(camera, target))
    assert net.creates == []


def test_inflight_frame_and_rapid_target_changes_do_not_restore_old_face(pipeline, monkeypatch):
    camera, net = pipeline
    camera.detect(target=camera_target(camera, "face"))
    captures = 0
    closed = []

    class Camera:
        def create_preview_configuration(self, **kwargs):
            return kwargs

        def configure(self, config):
            pass

        def start(self):
            pass

        def capture_array(self):
            nonlocal captures
            captures += 1
            if captures == 1:
                net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
            else:
                net.faces = None
            if captures == 2:
                assert camera._last_detection.label == "face"
                assert camera.detect(target=camera_target(camera, "red")) is None
                assert camera.detect(target=camera_target(camera, "face")) is None
            if captures == 3:
                camera._stop_event.set()
            return np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8)

        def stop(self):
            closed.append("stop")

        def close(self):
            closed.append("close")

    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "Picamera2", lambda index: Camera())
    camera._capture_loop()
    assert captures == 3
    assert len(net.inputs) == 3
    assert camera._target_revision == 3
    assert camera._frame_revision == 3
    assert camera._last_detection is None
    assert closed == ["stop", "close"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["face", "human"])
def test_follow_skill_aligns_approaches_and_holds_face_from_perception_mock(target):
    clock = [0.0]

    def sleep(seconds):
        clock[0] += seconds

    robot = FakeRobot()
    detections = iter((
        ObjectDetection(label="face", position="left", distance_cm=200, confidence=0.9),
        ObjectDetection(label="face", position="center", distance_cm=200, confidence=0.9),
        ObjectDetection(label="face", position="center", distance_cm=100, confidence=0.9),
    ))
    last_detection = ObjectDetection(label="face", position="center", distance_cm=100, confidence=0.9)
    perception = Mock(spec=ObjectPerceptionPipeline)
    perception.detect.side_effect = lambda **kwargs: next(detections, last_detection)
    context = SkillContext(
        robot=robot,
        perception=perception,
        perception_target=PerceptionTarget.FACE,
        state={"active_intent_parameters": {"target_object": target, "duration_s": 1}},
    )
    skill = FollowObjectSkill(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(stand_off_default_cm=100.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=15.0),
        time_fn=lambda: clock[0], sleep_fn=sleep,
    )
    result = skill.execute(context)
    assert result.status == "success"
    assert robot.commands.index("left") < robot.commands.index("forward")
    assert "backward" not in robot.commands
    assert result.data["final_distance_cm"] == 100
    assert context.state["follow_target_state"]["phase"] == "holding"
    assert robot.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_follow_face_waits_for_new_frame_before_repeating_motion(pipeline):
    camera, net = pipeline
    target = camera_target(camera, "face")
    camera.detect(target=target)
    frame = np.zeros((1280, 960, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((258, 258, 78, 93, 0.9))
    camera._last_detection = camera._process_frame(frame, target=target)
    camera._frame_sequence += 1
    assert camera._last_detection.position == "left"
    clock = [0.0]
    published_center = False

    def sleep(seconds):
        nonlocal published_center
        clock[0] += seconds
        if clock[0] >= 0.3 and not published_center:
            net.faces = _faces((440, 258, 78, 93, 0.9))
            camera._last_detection = camera._process_frame(frame, target=target)
            camera._frame_sequence += 1
            published_center = True

    robot = FakeRobot()
    context = SkillContext(
        robot=robot,
        perception=camera,
        perception_target=target,
        state={"active_intent_parameters": {"target_object": "face", "duration_s": 0.8}},
    )
    skill = FollowObjectSkill(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(stand_off_default_cm=100.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=15.0),
        time_fn=lambda: clock[0], sleep_fn=sleep,
    )
    result = skill.execute(context)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert robot.commands.count("left") == 1
    assert robot.commands.count("forward") == 1
    assert robot.commands.index("left") < robot.commands.index("stop") < robot.commands.index("forward")
    assert robot.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_new_frame_with_memorized_face_remains_cached(pipeline):
    camera, net = pipeline
    target = camera_target(camera, "face")
    camera.detect(target=target)
    frame = np.zeros((640, 480, 3), np.uint8)
    net.faces = _faces((180, 20, 100, 100, 0.9))
    camera._process_frame(frame, target=target)
    net.faces = None
    camera._last_detection = camera._process_frame(frame, target=target)
    camera._frame_sequence += 1

    assert camera.detect(target=target).cached is True

### FILE: tests/unit/test_face_gallery.py
from types import SimpleNamespace

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import FaceDetector
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import face_gallery
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.face_gallery import FaceGallery, normalize_name


def test_resolve_ignores_case_and_accents():
    gallery = FaceGallery({"Mathieu": np.zeros(1), "Élodie": np.zeros(1)})
    assert gallery.resolve("mathieu") == "Mathieu"
    assert gallery.resolve("elodie") == "Élodie"
    assert gallery.resolve("Bob") is None
    assert gallery.get("Bob") is None
    assert gallery.names() == ["Mathieu", "Élodie"]
    assert normalize_name(" ÉLodie ") == "elodie"


def test_load_keeps_only_single_face_photos(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    for name in ("Mathieu.jpg", "QUENTIN.PNG", "Deux.jpeg", "Aucun.jpg", "notes.txt"):
        (tmp_path / name).write_bytes(b"x")
    (tmp_path / "Moi.jpg").write_bytes(b"x")
    model = tmp_path / "m.onnx"
    model.touch()
    settings = RuntimeSettings(
        camera_face_gallery_dir=str(tmp_path), camera_face_model_path=str(model),
        camera_face_recognition_model_path=str(model),
    )
    counts = {"Deux": 2, "Aucun": 0}

    class Yunet:
        current = ""

        def setInputSize(self, size):
            pass

        def detect(self, image):
            count = counts.get(self.current, 1)
            return 1, (np.zeros((count, 15), np.float32) if count else None)

    yunet = Yunet()
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "FaceDetectorYN", SimpleNamespace(create=lambda *a, **k: yunet))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "imread", lambda path: (setattr(yunet, "current", path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1].split(".")[0]),
                                                      np.zeros((10, 10, 3), np.uint8))[1])
    monkeypatch.setattr(face_gallery, "FaceEmbedder", lambda s: SimpleNamespace(
        embed=lambda image, face: np.ones(3, np.float32)))
    gallery = FaceGallery.load(settings)
    assert gallery.names() == ["Mathieu", "Moi", "QUENTIN"]


def test_load_without_directory_gives_empty_gallery(tmp_path):
    assert FaceGallery.load(RuntimeSettings(camera_face_gallery_dir=str(tmp_path / "absent"))).names() == []


class _Embedder:
    """Embedding = 3e valeur de la ligne YuNet (id de personne)."""

    def embed(self, frame, face):
        return np.array([face[4]], np.float32)

    def similarity(self, first, second):
        return 1.0 if first[0] == second[0] else 0.0

    def is_match(self, score):
        return score >= 0.363


@pytest.fixture
def detector(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    model = tmp_path / "m.onnx"
    model.touch()
    settings = RuntimeSettings(
        camera_face_model_path=str(model), camera_face_grace_frames=1, camera_face_recognition_every_n=3,
        camera_clahe_enabled=False, camera_exposure_value=0.0,
    )
    net = SimpleNamespace(faces=None, setInputSize=lambda size: None)
    net.detect = lambda frame: (1, net.faces)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "FaceDetectorYN", SimpleNamespace(create=lambda *a, **k: net))
    monkeypatch.setattr("irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.face_detector.FaceEmbedder", lambda s: _Embedder())
    return FaceDetector(settings), net


def _row(x, person, width=100, score=0.9):
    row = np.zeros(15, np.float32)
    row[:4] = (x, 50, width, width)
    row[4] = person
    row[-1] = score
    return row


FRAME = np.zeros((480, 640, 3), np.uint8)


def test_identify_selects_best_match_and_rejects_unknown():
    gallery = FaceGallery(
        {"Mathieu": np.array([1], np.float32), "Quentin": np.array([2], np.float32)},
        _Embedder(),
    )
    assert gallery.identify(FRAME, _row(10, 2)) == "Quentin"
    assert gallery.identify(FRAME, _row(10, 3)) is None
    assert FaceGallery({}).identify(FRAME, _row(10, 1)) is None
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="sans modèle"):
        FaceGallery({"Mathieu": np.array([1], np.float32)}).identify(FRAME, _row(10, 1))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("scores,expected", [
    ((0.4, 0.6), "Quentin"),
    ((0.362, 0.1), None),
    ((0.363, 0.1), "Mathieu"),
])
def test_identify_uses_best_score_and_inclusive_threshold(scores, expected):
    calls = []
    embedder = SimpleNamespace(
        embed=lambda frame, face: calls.append("embed") or np.zeros(1, np.float32),
        similarity=lambda reference, embedding: scores[int(reference[0])],
        is_match=lambda score: score >= 0.363,
    )
    gallery = FaceGallery(
        {"Mathieu": np.array([0], np.float32), "Quentin": np.array([1], np.float32)},
        embedder,
    )
    assert gallery.identify(FRAME, _row(10, 1)) == expected
    assert calls == ["embed"]


def test_gallery_identifies_selected_face_and_preserves_only_cached_identity(detector):
    face_detector, net = detector
    gallery = FaceGallery(
        {"Mathieu": np.array([1], np.float32), "Quentin": np.array([2], np.float32)},
        _Embedder(),
    )
    face_detector = FaceDetector(face_detector._settings, gallery=gallery)
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1, width=200), _row(300, 2, width=80)])
    first = face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    assert first.identity == "Mathieu"
    assert first.diameter_px == 200
    net.faces = None
    cached = face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    assert cached.cached and cached.identity == "Mathieu"
    assert face_detector.detect(FRAME) is None
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 3, width=200), _row(300, 2, width=80)])
    assert face_detector.detect(FRAME).identity is None
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1, width=80), _row(300, 2, width=200)])
    assert face_detector.detect(FRAME).identity == "Quentin"


def test_target_picks_the_right_person_not_the_largest(detector):
    face_detector, net = detector
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1, width=200), _row(300, 2, width=80)])
    face_detector.set_target("Quentin", np.array([2], np.float32))
    detection = face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    assert detection.identity == "Quentin"
    assert detection.diameter_px == 80


def test_target_absent_never_falls_back_to_another_face(detector):
    face_detector, net = detector
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1)])
    face_detector.set_target("Quentin", np.array([2], np.float32))
    assert face_detector.detect(FRAME) is None


def test_target_is_tracked_by_iou_between_recognitions(detector):
    face_detector, net = detector
    face_detector.set_target("Quentin", np.array([2], np.float32))
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1, width=200), _row(300, 2, width=80)])
    face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    embeds = []
    real = face_detector._embedder.embed
    face_detector._embedder.embed = lambda frame, face: embeds.append(1) or real(frame, face)
    # Les ids changent mais la boîte de la cible bouge peu : pas de nouvelle reconnaissance.
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1, width=200), _row(305, 9, width=80)])
    assert face_detector.detect(FRAME).diameter_px == 80
    assert embeds == []
    face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    face_detector.detect(FRAME)  # N=3 : reconnaissance, la cible n'est plus là
    assert embeds


def test_clearing_target_restores_largest_face(detector):
    face_detector, net = detector
    net.faces = np.array([_row(10, 1, width=200), _row(300, 2, width=80)])
    face_detector.set_target("Quentin", np.array([2], np.float32))
    face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    face_detector.set_target(None)
    detection = face_detector.detect(FRAME)
    assert detection.identity is None
    assert detection.diameter_px == 200


def test_target_requires_name_and_embedding(detector):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        detector[0].set_target("Quentin")

### FILE: tests/unit/test_follow_object.py
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings, load_runtime_settings
from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget
from irobot.common.domain.orchestrator.registry import SkillRegistry
from irobot.common.domain.skills import SkillContext
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.follow_object import FollowObjectSkill
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot



class World(FakeRobot):
    """Robot + perception simulés avec horloge : la balle bouge selon les impulsions."""

    def __init__(self, distance_cm=100.0, drift_cm_s=0.0, hidden=None, bearing="center", offset_x=0.0, cached=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.now = 0.0
        self.distance_cm = distance_cm
        self.drift_cm_s = drift_cm_s
        self.hidden = hidden or (0.0, 0.0)
        self.bearing = bearing
        self.offset_x = offset_x
        self.cached = cached
        self.emergency = False
        self.detect_error: Exception | None = None

    def time(self) -> float:
        return self.now

    def sleep(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        self.now += seconds
        self.distance_cm += self.drift_cm_s * seconds

    def forward_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("forward")
        self.distance_cm -= 25.0 * duration
        self.sleep(duration)

    def backward_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("backward")
        self.distance_cm += 25.0 * duration
        self.sleep(duration)

    def left_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("left")
        self.bearing = "center"
        self.sleep(duration)

    def right_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("right")
        self.bearing = "center"
        self.sleep(duration)

    def emergency_stop_requested(self) -> bool:
        return self.emergency

    def detect(self, *, target):
        if self.detect_error:
            raise self.detect_error
        if self.hidden[0] <= self.now < self.hidden[1]:
            return None
        return SimpleNamespace(
            label=target.value, position=self.bearing, distance_cm=self.distance_cm,
            offset_x=self.offset_x, cached=self.cached,
        )


def run(world: World, recovery_attempts: int = 1, **params):
    context = SkillContext(
        robot=world,
        perception=world,
        perception_target=PerceptionTarget.RED_BALL,
        state={"active_intent_parameters": params},
    )
    skill = FollowObjectSkill(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(
            stand_off_default_cm=100.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=15.0, target_recovery_attempts=recovery_attempts,
            camera_focal_px=TOML_FOCAL_PX,
        ),
        time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep,
    )
    return skill.execute(context), context


MOTIONS = {"forward", "backward", "left", "right"}
TOML_FOCAL_PX = load_runtime_settings().camera_focal_px


def test_registered_in_default_registry():
    assert "follow_object" in SkillRegistry.default()


def test_holds_distance_in_dead_zone_without_moving():
    world = World(distance_cm=110.0)
    result, context = run(world, duration_s=5)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert not MOTIONS & set(world.commands)
    assert result.data["final_distance_cm"] == 110.0
    assert result.data["reacquisitions"] == 0
    assert result.data["iterations"] > 0
    assert context.state["follow_target_state"]["phase"] == "holding"
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_approaches_when_too_far():
    world = World(distance_cm=200.0)
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=30)

    assert "forward" in world.commands
    assert "backward" not in world.commands
    assert 85.0 <= result.data["final_distance_cm"] <= 115.0


def test_backs_up_when_too_close():
    world = World(distance_cm=50.0)
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=30)

    assert "backward" in world.commands
    assert "forward" not in world.commands
    assert 85.0 <= result.data["final_distance_cm"] <= 115.0


def test_follows_receding_ball():
    world = World(distance_cm=100.0, drift_cm_s=5.0)
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=20)

    assert "forward" in world.commands
    assert result.data["final_distance_cm"] <= 120.0


def test_aligns_before_regulating_distance():
    world = World(distance_cm=200.0, bearing="left")
    run(world, duration_s=1)

    assert world.commands[0] == "left"
    assert "forward" in world.commands


def test_target_lost_briefly_stops_without_search():
    world = World(distance_cm=100.0, hidden=(1.0, 2.0))
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=6, max_target_loss_s=3.0)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert not {"left", "right"} & set(world.commands)
    assert result.data["reacquisitions"] == 0


def test_target_lost_then_reacquired():
    world = World(distance_cm=100.0, hidden=(1.0, 8.0))
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=20, max_target_loss_s=1.0, search_timeout_s=30)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert "right" in world.commands
    assert result.data["reacquisitions"] == 1


def test_target_permanently_lost_fails():
    world = World(distance_cm=100.0, hidden=(0.0, 1e9))
    result, context = run(world, duration_s=600, max_target_loss_s=1.0, search_timeout_s=5)

    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert result.data["target_lost"] is True
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"
    assert context.state["follow_target_state"]["phase"] == "lost_target"

    world.obstacle_detected = True
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=10)

    assert result.status == "success"

    world.emergency = True
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=10)

    assert result.data["obstacle_detected"] is True
    assert result.data["emergency_stop"] is True
    assert not MOTIONS & set(world.commands)


def test_ends_at_duration():
    world = World(distance_cm=100.0)
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=3)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert result.data["duration_s"] == 3
    assert 3.0 <= world.now < 3.1

STAND_OFF_SETTINGS = RuntimeSettings(stand_off_default_cm=80.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=10.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("intent, distance_cm, expected_motion", [
    ("follow_object", 85.0, None),
    ("follow_object", 90.0, "forward"),
    ("follow_object", 70.0, "backward"),
    ("follow_person", 90.0, "forward"),
    ("follow_red_ball", 90.0, "forward"),
])
def test_stand_off_defaults_from_settings(intent, distance_cm, expected_motion):
    world = World(distance_cm=distance_cm)
    context = SkillContext(
        robot=world,
        perception=world,
        perception_target=PerceptionTarget.RED_BALL,
        state={"active_intent_name": intent, "active_intent_parameters": {"duration_s": 1}},
    )
    FollowObjectSkill(settings=STAND_OFF_SETTINGS, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep).execute(context)

    if expected_motion is None:
        assert not MOTIONS & set(world.commands)
    else:
        assert world.commands[0] == expected_motion


def test_explicit_stand_off_overrides_default_and_tolerance():
    skill = FollowObjectSkill(settings=RuntimeSettings(stand_off_default_cm=60.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=5.0))

    assert skill._resolve_stand_off({"stand_off_cm": 100}) == (100.0, 5.0)
    assert skill._resolve_stand_off({"stand_off_cm": 100, "stand_off_tolerance_cm": 3}) == (100.0, 3.0)


def _record_pulses(monkeypatch, skill_cls):
    pulses = []
    original = skill_cls._pulse_motion

    def spy(self, context, position, duration):
        pulses.append((position, duration))
        return original(self, context, position, duration)

    monkeypatch.setattr(skill_cls, "_pulse_motion", spy)
    return pulses


def _align_skill(**settings_kwargs):
    return FollowObjectSkill(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(camera_focal_px=TOML_FOCAL_PX, **settings_kwargs),
        time_fn=lambda: 0.0, sleep_fn=lambda seconds: None,
    )


def test_align_step_s_is_proportional_clamped_and_falls_back():
    skill = _align_skill()
    assert skill._align_step_s(1.0, 0.08) == pytest.approx(0.048, abs=0.002)
    assert skill._align_step_s(0.8, 0.08) == pytest.approx(0.039, abs=0.002)
    assert skill._align_step_s(1.0, 0.08) > skill._align_step_s(0.8, 0.08)
    assert skill._align_step_s(0.001, 0.08) == pytest.approx(0.03)
    assert _align_skill(align_gain=1.0, align_step_max_seconds=0.05)._align_step_s(1.0, 0.08) == pytest.approx(0.05)
    assert skill._align_step_s(None, 0.08) == 0.08


def test_align_pulse_duration_scales_with_offset(monkeypatch):
    pulses = _record_pulses(monkeypatch, FollowObjectSkill)
    helper = _align_skill()
    durations = []
    for offset in (0.7, 0.95):
        pulses.clear()
        run(World(distance_cm=100.0, bearing="left", offset_x=offset), duration_s=1)
        expected = helper._align_step_s(offset, FollowObjectSkill.ALIGN_STEP_S)
        assert pulses[0] == ("left", pytest.approx(expected))
        durations.append(pulses[0][1])
    assert durations[0] < durations[1]


class OvershootWorld(World):
    """La cible sort du champ juste après le premier alignement à gauche ; elle revient après un `right`."""

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(**kwargs)
        self.lost = False

    def left_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        super().left_for(duration)
        self.lost = True

    def right_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        super().right_for(duration)
        self.lost = False

    def detect(self, *, target):
        return None if self.lost else super().detect(target=target)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("attempts,expected_prefix,status", [
    (1, ["left", "right", "forward"], "success"),
    (0, ["left", "left"], "failed"),
])
def test_overshoot_recovery_reverses_only_within_attempts(attempts, expected_prefix, status):
    world = OvershootWorld(distance_cm=200.0, bearing="left", offset_x=0.9)
    result, _ = run(world, duration_s=30, max_target_loss_s=3.0, search_timeout_s=5, recovery_attempts=attempts)

    moves = [command for command in world.commands if command != "stop"]
    assert moves[:len(expected_prefix)] == expected_prefix
    assert result.status == status
    if attempts == 0:
        assert "right" not in world.commands


class CachedAfterAlignWorld(World):
    """Après un alignement, la cible reste visible mais ne provient que de la mémoire."""

    def left_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        super().left_for(duration)
        self.cached = True


def test_no_overshoot_recovery_without_recent_alignment_or_with_cached_detection():
    silent_loss = World(distance_cm=200.0, hidden=(0.0, 1e9))
    result, _ = run(silent_loss, duration_s=600, max_target_loss_s=1.0, search_timeout_s=3)
    assert "left" not in silent_loss.commands and result.status == "failed"

    cached = CachedAfterAlignWorld(distance_cm=200.0, bearing="left", offset_x=0.9)
    result, _ = run(cached, duration_s=600, max_target_loss_s=1.0, search_timeout_s=3, recovery_attempts=1)
    assert "right" not in cached.commands and result.status == "failed"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_follow_object_wiring.py
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent
from irobot.app.domain.orchestrator.planner import SkillPlanner

from irobot.app.domain.intent_validator import IntentValidator

    TextIntentParser,
    _extract_duration,
    _extract_stand_off_cm,
)

from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot


SENTENCE = "cherche la balle jaune, approche toi à 1m de la balle jaune puis suit la pendant 5 min"


def _plan(text: str) -> list[Intent]:
    return TextIntentParser().parse_plan(text)


def test_parse_plan_full_sentence():
    plan = _plan(SENTENCE)

    assert [i.name for i in plan] == ["search_and_approach_object", "follow_object"]
    search, follow = plan
    assert search.target == "yellow"
    assert search.parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 100.0
    assert follow.target == "yellow"
    assert follow.parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 100.0
    assert follow.parameters["duration_s"] == 300.0
    assert follow.parameters["target_object"] == "yellow"


def test_parse_plan_variants_words_and_red():
    plan = _plan("cherche la balle rouge, approche-toi à 2 mètres, ensuite suis-la pendant cinq minutes")

    assert [i.name for i in plan] == ["search_and_approach_object", "follow_object"]
    assert plan[0].target == "red"
    assert plan[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 200.0
    assert plan[1].target == "red"
    assert plan[1].parameters["duration_s"] == 300.0
    assert plan[1].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 200.0


def test_parse_plan_spelled_distance_and_cm():
    plan = _plan("cherche la balle jaune puis approche toi à un mètre de la balle puis suivre la 2 minutes")
    assert plan[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 100.0

    plan = _plan("approche toi à 50 cm de la balle jaune et puis suis la pendant 30 secondes")
    assert plan[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 50.0
    assert plan[1].parameters["duration_s"] == 30.0
    assert plan[1].target == "yellow"


def test_parse_plan_single_segment_matches_parse():
    parser = TextIntentParser()
    assert [i.name for i in parser.parse_plan("avance")] == [parser.parse("avance").name]
    assert [i.name for i in parser.parse_plan("cherche la balle rouge")] == ["follow_red_ball"]

    assert search.parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 100.0

    )

    assert _plan("suis moi")[0].parameters == {}
    assert _plan("suis mathieu")[0].parameters == {"person": "Mathieu"}
    assert _plan("suis la balle jaune pendant 5 minutes")[0].parameters == {
        "target_object": "yellow", "duration_s": 300.0,
    }
    assert _plan("suis le chien pendant 5 minutes")[0].name == "unknown"
    assert _plan("cherche mathieu puis suis mathieu pendant 5 minutes")[1].parameters == {
        "person": "Mathieu", "duration_s": 300.0,
    }


def test_pronoun_follow_keeps_colour_target_when_no_name():
    _, follow = _plan(
        "cherche la balle jaune approche toi à un mètre de la balle jaune puis suis la pendant cinq minutes"
    )
    assert follow.name == "follow_object"
    assert follow.target == "yellow"
    assert "person" not in follow.parameters

    assert _plan("cherche mathieu puis suis la balle jaune")[-1].name == "follow_object"
    assert _plan("cherche mathieu puis suis la balle jaune")[-1].target == "yellow"

    last = _plan("cherche la balle jaune puis cherche mathieu puis suis le")[-1]
    assert last.name == "follow_person"
    assert last.parameters == {"person": "Mathieu"}
    assert [s.name for s in SkillPlanner().plan("follow_object")] == ["follow_object"]


def test_normalize_synonyms_and_targets():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    names = ["follow", "track", "track_object", "follow_target", "suivre", "suit"]
    out = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.normalize([Intent(name=n, target="balle jaune") for n in names])
    assert all(i.name == "follow_object" and i.target == "yellow" for i in out)
    assert all(i.parameters["target_object"] == "yellow" for i in out)

    out = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.normalize(
        [
            Intent(name="search_object", target="Rouge"),
            Intent(name="follow_object", target="red_ball"),
            Intent(name="follow_red_ball", target="red_ball"),
            Intent(name="follow_object", parameters={"target_object": "jaune"}),
        ]
    )
    assert [(i.name, i.target) for i in out] == [
        ("search_and_approach_object", "red"),
        ("follow_object", "red"),
        ("follow_red_ball", "red_ball"),
        ("follow_object", "yellow"),
    ]


def test_validate_rejects_follow_object_with_unknown_target():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    plan = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.normalize([Intent(name="follow", target="blue")])
    validated, errors = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.validate(plan)
    assert validated == []
    assert errors[0]["reason"] == "intent not allowed"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["follow_object", "search_and_approach_object"])
def test_sanitize_bounds_parameters(name):
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    high, _ = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.validate(
        [Intent(name=name, target="red", parameters={"stand_off_cm": 9999, "duration_s": 99999, "search_timeout_s": 999})]
    )
    assert high[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == settings.max_stand_off_cm
    assert high[0].parameters["duration_s"] == settings.max_follow_duration_sec
    assert high[0].parameters["search_timeout_s"] == 120.0

    low, _ = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.validate(
        [Intent(name=name, target="red", parameters={"stand_off_cm": 1, "duration_s": -5, "search_timeout_s": 0})]
    )
    assert low[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == settings.min_stand_off_cm
    assert low[0].parameters["duration_s"] == 1.0
    assert low[0].parameters["search_timeout_s"] == 1.0

    ok, _ = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.validate(
        [Intent(name=name, target="red", parameters={"stand_off_cm": "100", "duration_s": 300})]
    )
    assert ok[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 100.0
    assert ok[0].parameters["duration_s"] == 300.0


def test_sanitize_non_numeric_falls_back_to_defaults():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    bad = {"stand_off_cm": "près", "duration_s": None, "search_timeout_s": float("nan")}
    out, _ = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.validate([Intent(name="follow_object", target="yellow", parameters=bad)])

    assert out[0].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == runtime.command_service.settings.stand_off_default_cm
    assert out[0].parameters["duration_s"] == 60.0
    assert out[0].parameters["search_timeout_s"] == runtime.command_service.settings.search_timeout_seconds


def test_sanitize_leaves_absent_keys_absent():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    out, _ = runtime.command_service.plan_validator.validate([Intent(name="follow_object", target="yellow")])
    assert out[0].parameters == {}


class World(FakeRobot):
    """Robot + perception simulés avec horloge virtuelle."""

    def __init__(self, distance_cm: float):
        super().__init__()
        self.now = 0.0
        self.distance_cm = distance_cm
        self.targets: list[PerceptionTarget] = []

    def time(self) -> float:
        return self.now

    def sleep(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        self.now += seconds

    def forward_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("forward")
        self.distance_cm -= 25.0 * duration
        self.sleep(duration)

    def backward_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("backward")
        self.distance_cm += 25.0 * duration
        self.sleep(duration)

    def left_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("left")
        self.sleep(duration)

    def right_for(self, duration: float) -> None:
        self.commands.append("right")
        self.sleep(duration)

    def detect(self, *, target):
        self.targets.append(target)
        return SimpleNamespace(
            label=target.value, position="center", distance_cm=self.distance_cm
        )


def test_full_plan_runs_through_voice_runtime(tmp_path):
    world = World(distance_cm=180.0)
    settings = RuntimeSettings()
    registry = SkillRegistry.default()
    registry.register(ApproachTargetSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep))
    registry.register(FollowObjectSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep))
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(
        robot=world,
        perception=world,
                registry=registry,
        use_llm=False,
        trace_dir=str(tmp_path),
    )

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan(SENTENCE)

    assert result["status"] == "accepted", result
    steps = result["steps"]
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in steps] == ["search_and_approach_object", "follow_object"]
    assert "approach_target" in steps[0]["plan"]
    assert steps[1]["plan"] == ["follow_object"]
    assert steps[1]["intent"].parameters["duration_s"] == 300.0
    assert set(world.targets) == {PerceptionTarget.YELLOW_BALL}
    # Le suivi consomme bien les 5 minutes sur l'horloge virtuelle, sans attente réelle.
    assert world.now >= 300.0
    assert "forward" in world.commands
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_frame_enhancer.py
from types import SimpleNamespace

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.hardware.camera import camera_live_perception_pipeline, frame_enhancer
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.frame_enhancer import FrameEnhancer, configure_exposure
from tests.unit.perception_helpers import camera_target


def _settings(**kwargs):
    return RuntimeSettings(camera_clahe_enabled=True, **kwargs)


def test_disabled_enhancer_returns_original_frame():
    frame = np.full((40, 60, 3), 30, np.uint8)
    assert FrameEnhancer(RuntimeSettings(camera_clahe_enabled=False)).apply(frame) is frame


def test_clahe_changes_only_lab_luminance_and_preserves_rgb(monkeypatch):
    frame = np.zeros((64, 64, 3), np.uint8)
    frame[:, :32] = (90, 10, 5)
    frame[:, 32:] = (90, 85, 5)
    original = frame.copy()
    lab = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2LAB)
    expected = lab.copy()
    expected[:, :, 0] = cv2.createCLAHE(3.0, (8, 8)).apply(lab[:, :, 0])
    real_cvt_color = cv2.cvtColor
    converted = []

    def convert(image, code):
        if code == cv2.COLOR_LAB2RGB:
            converted.append(image.copy())
        return real_cvt_color(image, code)

    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "cvtColor", convert)
    result = FrameEnhancer(_settings()).apply(frame)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(converted[0][:, :, 1:], lab[:, :, 1:])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, real_cvt_color(expected, cv2.COLOR_LAB2RGB))
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(frame, original)
    assert result.dtype == np.uint8
    assert result.shape == frame.shape
    assert result[32, 16, 0] > result[32, 16, 2]
    assert result[32, 48, 0] > result[32, 48, 2]


def test_clahe_reused_across_frames(monkeypatch):
    real_create = cv2.createCLAHE
    calls = []

    def create(**kwargs):
        calls.append(kwargs)
        return real_create(**kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "createCLAHE", create)
    enhancer = FrameEnhancer(_settings(camera_clahe_clip_limit=2.0))
    for _ in range(3):
        enhancer.apply(np.zeros((64, 64, 3), np.uint8))
    assert calls == [{"clipLimit": 2.0, "tileGridSize": (8, 8)}]


def test_dark_foreground_luminance_improves():
    gradient = np.tile(np.linspace(20, 60, 640, dtype=np.uint8), (480, 1))
    frame = np.repeat(gradient[:, :, None], 3, axis=2)
    frame[:, 480:] = 240
    result = FrameEnhancer(_settings()).apply(frame)
    before = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2LAB)[:, :400, 0].mean()
    after = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_RGB2LAB)[:, :400, 0].mean()
    assert after > before + 5


@pytest.mark.parametrize("color", ["red", "yellow"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("level", [78, 255])
def test_runtime_detects_backlit_and_normal_balls(monkeypatch, color, level):
    frame = np.full((480, 640, 3), 240, np.uint8)
    cv2.circle(frame, (320, 240), 25, (level, 0, 0) if color == "red" else (level, level, 0), -1)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: RuntimeSettings(camera_clahe_enabled=False, camera_exposure_value=0.0),
    )
    uncorrected = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    baseline = uncorrected._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(uncorrected, color))
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings", _settings)
    corrected = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    detection = corrected._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(corrected, color))
    assert detection is not None
    assert detection.label == f"{color}_ball"
    assert detection.diameter_px == pytest.approx(49, abs=1)
    if baseline is not None:
        assert detection.position == baseline.position
        assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(baseline.distance_cm)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("foreground,background", [(0, 255), (15, 90)])
def test_runtime_detects_same_aruco_id_after_enhancement(monkeypatch, foreground, background):
    marker = cv2.aruco.generateImageMarker(
        cv2.aruco.getPredefinedDictionary(cv2.aruco.DICT_4X4_50), 1, 80,
    )
    marker = np.where(marker == 0, foreground, background).astype(np.uint8)
    image = np.full((640, 480), background, np.uint8)
    image[280:360, 200:280] = marker
    frame = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2RGB)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: RuntimeSettings(camera_clahe_enabled=False, camera_exposure_value=0.0),
    )
    baseline_pipeline = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    baseline = baseline_pipeline._process_frame(
        frame, target=camera_target(baseline_pipeline, "marker")
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings", _settings)
    corrected = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    detection = corrected._process_frame(frame, target=camera_target(corrected, "marker"))
    assert detection is not None and baseline is not None
    assert detection.label == baseline.label == "marker_1"
    assert detection.position == baseline.position
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(baseline.distance_cm, rel=0.01)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("level", [0, 30, 128, 255])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["red", "yellow", "marker"])
def test_uniform_frames_do_not_create_detections(monkeypatch, level, target):
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings", _settings)
    pipeline = camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(autostart=False)
    assert pipeline._process_frame(
        np.full((480, 640, 3), level, np.uint8),
        target=camera_target(pipeline, target),
    ) is None


def test_exposure_applied_to_supported_camera():
    calls = []
    camera = SimpleNamespace(camera_controls={"ExposureValue": (-8, 8, 0)}, set_controls=calls.append)
    configure_exposure(camera, _settings(camera_exposure_value=1.0))
    assert calls == [{"ExposureValue": 1.0}]


def test_zero_exposure_leaves_camera_untouched():
    configure_exposure(SimpleNamespace(), RuntimeSettings(camera_exposure_value=0.0))


def test_unsupported_exposure_is_logged(caplog):
    camera = SimpleNamespace(camera_controls={}, set_controls=lambda controls: pytest.fail("unsupported"))
    configure_exposure(camera, _settings(camera_exposure_value=1.0))
    assert "ExposureValue non pris en charge" in caplog.text


def test_exposure_failure_is_not_silenced():
    def fail(controls):
        raise RuntimeError("exposure rejected")

    camera = SimpleNamespace(camera_controls={"ExposureValue": (-8, 8, 0)}, set_controls=fail)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="exposure rejected"):
        configure_exposure(camera, _settings(camera_exposure_value=1.0))

### FILE: tests/unit/test_hardware_distance_script.py
from pathlib import Path
import runpy
import sys
from types import ModuleType


def test_distance_script_initializes_gpio_with_supported_arguments(monkeypatch, capsys):
    devices = []

    class OutputDevice:
        def __init__(self, pin):
            self.pin = pin
            self.closed = False
            devices.append(self)

        def close(self):
            self.closed = True

    class InputDevice(OutputDevice):
        def __init__(self, pin, *, pull_up):
            super().__init__(pin)
            self.pull_up = pull_up

    gpiozero = ModuleType("gpiozero")
    gpiozero.DigitalInputDevice = InputDevice
    gpiozero.DigitalOutputDevice = OutputDevice
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "gpiozero", gpiozero)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        sys,
        "argv",
        [
            "test_hardware_distance.py",
            "--trigger-pin", "17",
            "--echo-pin", "27",
            "--duration", "0",
        ],
    )
    script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "hardware" / "test_hardware_distance.py"

    runpy.run_path(str(script), run_name="__main__")

    assert [device.pin for device in devices] == [17, 27]
    assert devices[1].pull_up is False
    assert all(device.closed for device in devices)
    assert "Aucune distance" in capsys.readouterr().out

### FILE: tests/unit/test_intent_validator.py
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent
from irobot.app.domain.intent_validator import IntentValidator

@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", sorted(IntentValidator.ALLOWED_INTENTS))
def test_domain_accepts_known_intents_without_text(name):
    assert IntentValidator().validate(Intent(name=name))


def test_domain_rejects_unknown_intent():
    assert not IntentValidator().validate(Intent(name="draw_heart"))


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "name,target,expected",
    [
        ("follow_object", "red", True),
        ("follow_object", "yellow", True),
        ("follow_object", "blue", False),
        ("follow_object", "red_ball", False),
        ("follow_red_ball", "red_ball", True),
        ("follow_red_ball", "yellow", False),
    ],
)
def test_domain_checks_structured_targets(name, target, expected):
    assert IntentValidator().validate(Intent(name=name, target=target)) is expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["red", "yellow", "red_ball", "yellow_ball", "marker", None])
def test_domain_accepts_normalized_tracking_parameter(target):
    intent = Intent(name="follow_object", parameters={"target_object": target})
    assert IntentValidator().validate(intent)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["blue", "balle jaune", [], {}, 42])
def test_domain_rejects_invalid_tracking_parameter(target):
    intent = Intent(name="follow_object", parameters={"target_object": target})
    assert not IntentValidator().validate(intent)


def test_lexical_refusal_stays_outside_domain():
    intent = Intent(name="follow_object", target="red", raw_text="suis un chat")
    assert IntentValidator().validate(intent)
    validated, errors = IntentPlanValidator(RuntimeSettings()).validate([intent])
    assert not validated
    assert errors[0]["reason"] == "intent not allowed"
    controller = LanguageController(robot=FakeRobot())
    result = controller.handle_planned_step(controller.planner.plan_intents([intent])[0])
    assert result["status"] == "rejected"
    assert result["reason"] == "unsupported_follow_target"


def test_language_alias_is_normalized_before_business_validation():
    validator = IntentPlanValidator(RuntimeSettings())
    normalized = validator.normalize([
        Intent(name="follow", target="balle jaune", parameters={"target_object": "jaune"}),
    ])
    validated, errors = validator.validate(normalized)
    assert not errors
    assert validated[0].target == "yellow"
    assert validated[0].parameters["target_object"] == "yellow"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_language_controller.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from irobot.app.domain.intent_validator import IntentValidator
from irobot.app.interfaces.intent.text_intent_parser import TextIntentParser
from irobot.app.interfaces.command_service import CommandService
from irobot.app.domain.orchestrator.planner import SkillPlanner
from irobot.app.interfaces.language_controller import LanguageController
from irobot.common.interfaces.perception_contracts import ObjectDetection
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot



def test_intent_parser_follow_red_ball():
    intent = TextIntentParser().parse("va vers la balle rouge")

    assert intent.name == "follow_red_ball"
    assert intent.target == "red_ball"
    assert intent.confidence > 0.5


def test_intent_parser_and_planner_follow_person():
    parser = TextIntentParser()
    validator = IntentValidator()
    for phrase in ("suis-moi", "suis moi", "suivez-moi"):
        intent = parser.parse(phrase)
        assert intent.name == "follow_person"
        assert intent.target == "face"
        assert intent.confidence == 1.0
        assert intent.raw_text == phrase
        assert validator.validate(intent)

    controller = LanguageController(robot=FakeRobot())
    assert [skill.name for skill in controller.planner.plan("follow_person")] == ["follow_object"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("phrase", [
    "approche toi de moi", "approche-toi de moi", "Approche-toi de moi !",
    "rapproche-toi de moi", "rejoins-moi",
])
def test_intent_parser_approach_me_targets_face(phrase):
    intent = TextIntentParser().parse(phrase)

    assert intent.name == "search_and_approach_object"
    assert intent.target == "face"
    assert intent.raw_text == phrase
    assert IntentValidator().validate(intent)


def test_intent_parser_stop():

    assert intent.name == "stop"


def test_intent_validator_accepts_known_intent():

    assert IntentValidator().validate(intent) is True


def test_intent_parser_recognizes_spin_square_and_return():
    spin = TextIntentParser().parse("fais un tour sur toi même")
    square = TextIntentParser().parse("forme un carré de 40 cm")
    back = TextIntentParser().parse("reviens au point de départ")

    assert spin.name == "spin_360"
    assert square.name == "move_square"
    assert square.parameters["side"] == 40
    assert back.name == "return_to_start"


def test_intent_parser_recognizes_search_and_approach_object():

    assert intent.name == "search_and_approach_object"
    assert intent.parameters["search"] is True
    assert intent.parameters["approach"] is True

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"

    result = service.handle_text("avance")
