def test_skill_planner_compiles_compound_intents_to_typed_skill_steps():
    planner = SkillPlanner()
    parsed = TextIntentParser().parse("forme un carré de 30 cm")
    expanded = planner.expand_intents([parsed])
    steps = planner.plan_intents(expanded)

    assert len(steps) == 8
    assert [step.intent.name for step in steps] == [
        "move_forward", "turn_right", "move_forward", "turn_right",
        "move_forward", "turn_right", "move_forward", "turn_right",
    ]
    assert all([skill.name for skill in step.skills] == [step.intent.name] for step in steps)


def test_language_controller_prioritizes_obstacle_stop():
    robot = FakeRobot()
    robot.obstacle_detected = True
    controller = LanguageController(robot=robot)

    intent = TextIntentParser().parse("avance")
    result = controller.handle_planned_step(controller.planner.plan_intents([intent])[0])

    assert result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert result["reason"] == "obstacle prioritized"
    assert result["results"][0].data["motion"] == "stop"


def test_language_controller_injects_perception_detection():
    class FakePerception:
        def detect(self, *, target):
            return ObjectDetection(
                label=target.value, position="center", distance_cm=15.0, confidence=0.9
            )

    controller = LanguageController(
        robot=FakeRobot(),
        perception=FakePerception(),
            )
    intent = TextIntentParser().parse("cherche la balle rouge puis approche toi")
    result = controller.handle_planned_step(controller.planner.plan_intents([intent])[0])

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"


def test_parse_plan_extracts_person_for_approach_and_follow() -> None:
    parser = TextIntentParser()

    named = parser.parse_plan("approche toi de mathieu")[0]
    assert (named.name, named.target, named.parameters) == (
        "search_and_approach_object", "face", {"person": "Mathieu"},
    )
    for text in ("approche toi de moi", "approche toi de la balle jaune"):
        assert "person" not in parser.parse_plan(text)[0].parameters
    follow = parser.parse_plan("suis mathieu")[0]
    assert (follow.name, follow.parameters) == ("follow_person", {"person": "Mathieu"})


def test_parse_plan_splits_on_et_only_before_action_verb() -> None:
    parser = TextIntentParser()

    plan = parser.parse_plan("suis mathieu et avance")
    assert [(i.name, i.parameters) for i in plan] == [
        ("follow_person", {"person": "Mathieu"}), ("move_forward", {"duration": 1.0}),
    ]
    assert [i.name for i in parser.parse_plan("avance, recule et tourne à droite")] == [
        "move_forward", "move_backward", "turn_right",
    ]


def test_parse_plan_keeps_et_inside_non_action_phrases() -> None:
    parser = TextIntentParser()

    assert [i.name for i in parser.parse_plan("tourne à gauche et à droite")] == ["turn_left"]
    duration = parser.parse_plan("avance pendant une minute et trente secondes")
    assert [(i.name, i.parameters) for i in duration] == [("move_forward", {"duration": 90.0})]
    mixed = parser.parse_plan("cherche mathieu et suis le pendant 5 minutes")
    assert [(i.name, i.parameters.get("person"), i.parameters.get("duration_s")) for i in mixed] == [
        ("search_and_approach_object", "Mathieu", None), ("follow_person", "Mathieu", 300.0),
    ]
### FILE: tests/unit/test_mission_orchestrator.py
import ast
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent
from irobot.app.domain.orchestrator.mission_orchestrator import MissionOrchestrator
from irobot.app.domain.orchestrator.planner import SkillPlanStep
from irobot.common.domain.skills.base_skill import SkillResult


def plan(*names):
    return [SkillPlanStep(Intent(name=name), ()) for name in names]


def test_sequence_and_success_without_voice_or_hardware():
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator()
    calls = []
    events = []

    def execute(step):
        assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "executing"
        calls.append(step.intent.name)
        return {"intent": step.intent, "status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult()]}

    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward", "turn_left"), execute,
        stop_requested=lambda: False, on_event=events.append,
    )

    assert calls == ["move_forward", "turn_left"]
    assert status == "accepted"
    assert [step["attempt"] for step in steps] == [1, 1]
    assert [event["event"] for event in events] == ["step_result", "step_result"]
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.as_dict()["phase"] == "success"
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.metadata == {"steps_total": 2, "step_index": 1}


def test_retry_recovers_before_next_step():
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator()
    calls = []

    def execute(step):
        calls.append(step.intent.name)
        status = "failed" if len(calls) == 1 else "success"
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult(status=status)]}

    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward", "stop"), execute, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )

    assert status == "accepted"
    assert calls == ["move_forward", "move_forward", "stop"]
    assert [step["attempt"] for step in steps] == [2, 1]


def test_exhausted_retries_fail_and_skip_remaining_steps():
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator(settings=RuntimeSettings(max_step_retries=1))
    calls = []

    def execute(step):
        calls.append(step.intent.name)
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult(status="failed")]}

    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward", "stop"), execute, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )

    assert calls == ["move_forward", "move_forward"]
    assert status == "incomplete"
    assert steps[0]["reason"] == "max retries reached"
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "failed"
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.metadata["failed_at"] == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("reason", ["emergency_stop", "obstacle_detected"])
def test_safety_stop_during_step_is_not_retried(reason):
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator()
    events = []

    def execute(step):
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult(data={reason: True})]}

    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward", "turn_left"), execute,
        stop_requested=lambda: False, on_event=events.append,
    )

    assert status == "aborted"
    assert len(steps) == 1
    assert steps[0]["attempt"] == 1
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "aborted"
    assert events[-1]["event"] == ("emergency_stop" if reason == "emergency_stop" else "obstacle_stop")


def test_stop_before_execution_skips_executor():
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator()
    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward"), lambda _: pytest.fail("Executor must not run"),
        stop_requested=lambda: True,
    )
    assert status == "aborted"
    assert steps[0]["reason"] == "emergency stop"
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "aborted"


def test_step_timeout_uses_injected_clock_and_stops_sequence():
    clock = [0.0]
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator(time_fn=lambda: clock[0])

    def execute(step):
        clock[0] += 7
        return {"status": "accepted"}

    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward", "turn_left"), execute, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )
    assert status == "aborted"
    assert len(steps) == 1
    assert steps[0]["step_duration_seconds"] == 7
    assert steps[0]["reason"] == "step timeout"


def test_mission_timeout_includes_time_spent_retrying():
    clock = [0.0]
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(step_timeout_seconds=6, mission_timeout_seconds=0),
        time_fn=lambda: clock[0],
    )
    calls = []
    events = []

    def execute(step):
        calls.append(step.intent.name)
        clock[0] += 5
        status = "failed" if len(calls) < 3 else "success"
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult(status=status)]}

    steps, status = orchestrator.execute(
        plan("move_forward", "turn_left"), execute,
        stop_requested=lambda: False, on_event=events.append,
    )
    assert calls == ["move_forward"] * 3
    assert status == "aborted"
    assert steps[-1]["reason"] == "mission timeout"
    assert events[-1]["event"] == "mission_timeout"
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.metadata["aborted_at"] == 1


def test_execution_error_is_propagated_and_marks_mission_failed():
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator()

    def execute(step):
        raise OSError("motor disconnected")

    with pytest.raises(OSError, match="motor disconnected"):
        orchestrator.execute(plan("move_forward"), execute, stop_requested=lambda: False)
    assert orchestrator.state_machine.state.phase == "failed"
    assert "motor disconnected" in orchestrator.state_machine.state.last_error


def test_empty_plan_is_an_explicit_error():
    orchestrator = MissionOrchestrator()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least one"):
        orchestrator.execute([], lambda _: pytest.fail("Executor must not run"), stop_requested=lambda: False)


def test_domain_has_no_interface_or_hardware_imports():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "src" / "irobot"
    forbidden = (
        "irobot.common.interfaces",
        "irobot.app.interfaces",
        "irobot.common.hardware",
    )
    for domain in (root / "common" / "domain", root / "app" / "domain"):
        for path in domain.rglob("*.py"):
            for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))):
                if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
                    assert not (node.module or "").startswith(forbidden), path
                elif isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                    assert all(not alias.name.startswith(forbidden) for alias in node.names), path

### FILE: tests/unit/test_numbers.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from irobot.common.domain.numbers import to_float


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value, expected", [("1.5", 1.5), (3, 3.0), (2.5, 2.5)])
def test_to_float_converts(value, expected):
    assert to_float(value) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [None, "abc", [], {}])
def test_to_float_invalid_returns_default(value):
    assert to_float(value) is None
    assert to_float(value, 7.0) == 7.0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", ["nan", "inf", float("-inf")])
def test_to_float_finiteness_only_when_required(value):
    assert to_float(value, 1.0) != 1.0
    assert to_float(value, 1.0, require_finite=True) == 1.0
    assert to_float(value, require_finite=True) is None

### FILE: tests/unit/test_plan_guards.py
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from irobot.app.interfaces import plan_guards as pg
from irobot.common.interfaces.text_normalizer import preprocess


def _load_eval_script():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "scenarios" / "eval_prompt_plan.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("eval_prompt_plan_guards", path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def step(intent, target=None, **params):
    return {"intent": intent, "target": target, "parameters": params}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["stop", "arrête toi", "halte", "pause", "Arrête-toi !", "stop stop robot"])
def test_pure_stop_phrases(text):
    assert pg.is_pure_stop(text)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "text", ["", "avance puis arrête", "arrête toi et recule", "stop maintenant", "toi", "robot"]
)
def test_non_pure_stop_phrases(text):
    assert not pg.is_pure_stop(text)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("text", "steps"),
    [
        ("chante une chanson", [step("spin_360")]),
        ("ouvre la porte du garage", [step("move_forward", distance=100)]),
        ("fais un salto arrière", [step("avoid_obstacle")]),
    ],
)
def test_ungrounded_intent_rejects_plan(text, steps):
    kept, reason, _ = pg.grounding_check(preprocess(text), steps)

    assert kept == []
    assert "non justifié" in reason


def test_invented_number_without_digits_is_dropped_then_default_duration():
    kept, reason, notes = pg.grounding_check("avance", [step("move_forward", distance=1)])

    assert reason is None
    assert kept[0]["parameters"] == {"duration": 1}
    assert notes == ["move_forward.distance=1 absent de la phrase : supprimé"]


def test_wrong_number_with_digits_rejects_plan():
    kept, reason, _ = pg.grounding_check(
        preprocess("avance de cinquante centimètres"), [step("move_forward", distance=500)]
    )

    assert kept == []
    assert "500" in reason


def test_parasite_stop_is_removed():
    text = preprocess("avance de vingt centimètres puis recule de dix centimètres")
    steps = [step("move_forward", distance=20), step("stop"), step("move_backward", distance=10)]

    kept, reason, notes = pg.grounding_check(text, steps)

    assert reason is None
    assert [s["intent"] for s in kept] == ["move_forward", "move_backward"]
    assert "stop parasite supprimé" in notes


def test_unhandled_parameter_is_removed():
    kept, reason, notes = pg.grounding_check(
        "reviens au point de départ", [step("return_to_start", duration=1)]
    )

    assert reason is None
    assert kept == [step("return_to_start")]
    assert notes == ["return_to_start.duration inutile : supprimé"]


def test_turn_without_angle_gets_default_and_unit_is_kept():
    kept, reason, _ = pg.grounding_check("tourne à gauche", [step("turn_left")])
    assert reason is None
    assert kept[0]["parameters"] == {"angle": 90}

    kept, reason, _ = pg.grounding_check(
        preprocess("recule de deux mètres"), [step("move_backward", distance=200, unit="centimètres")]
    )
    assert reason is None
    assert kept[0]["parameters"] == {"distance": 200, "unit": "centimètres"}


def test_use_case_is_grounded():
    text = preprocess(
        "cherche la balle jaune approche toi à un mètre de la balle jaune puis suis la pendant cinq minutes"
    )
    steps = [
        step("search_and_approach_object", "yellow", stand_off_cm=100),
        step("follow_object", "yellow", stand_off_cm=100, duration_s=300),
    ]

    assert pg.grounding_check(text, steps) == (steps, None, [])


def test_build_formats_chain():
    formats = pg.build_formats(False, True)

    assert formats[0] == {
        "type": "json_schema",
        "json_schema": {"name": "plan", "strict": True, "schema": pg.PLAN_SCHEMA},
    }
    assert formats[1:] == [{"type": "json_object", "schema": pg.PLAN_SCHEMA}, {"type": "json_object"}, None]
    assert pg.build_formats(False, False) == [{"type": "json_object"}, None]
    assert pg.build_formats(True, True) == [None]


def test_guards_match_eval_script():
    script = _load_eval_script()
    for name in ("STOP_WORDS", "INTENT_LEXICON", "NUMERIC_KEYS", "ALLOWED_PARAMS", "PLAN_SCHEMA"):
        assert getattr(pg, name) == getattr(script, name), name
    for flags in ((False, True), (False, False), (True, False)):
        assert pg.build_formats(*flags) == script.build_formats(*flags)

    texts = [case[1] for case in script.CASES] + ["stop", "pause", "Arrête-toi !", "avance puis arrête"]
    for text in texts:
        sent = preprocess(text)
        assert pg.fold(sent) == script.fold(sent), text
        assert pg.is_pure_stop(sent) == script.is_pure_stop(sent), text

    candidates = [
        [step("spin_360")],
        [step("move_forward", distance=100)],
        [step("move_forward", distance=1), step("stop")],
        [step("turn_right", angle=90, speed=2)],
        [step("return_to_start", duration=1)],
        [step("follow_object", "yellow", stand_off_cm=100, duration_s=300)],
        [step("execute_path", steps=[])],
        ["pas un objet"],
    ]
    for text in texts:
        sent = preprocess(text)
        for steps in candidates:
            assert pg.grounding_check(sent, steps) == script.grounding_check(sent, steps), (text, steps)


def test_person_param_and_named_follow_guards() -> None:
    assert "person" in pg.ALLOWED_PARAMS["search_and_approach_object"]
    assert pg.named_follow_target("suis mathieu") == "Mathieu"
    assert pg.named_follow_target("suis la balle jaune") is None
    assert pg.follow_request_refusal("suis mathieu") is None
    assert pg.follow_request_refusal("suis le chien") == "unsupported_follow_target"


def test_follow_person_keeps_grounded_duration() -> None:
    steps = [step("follow_person", "face", person="Mathieu", duration_s=300)]

    kept, reason, _ = pg.grounding_check(preprocess("suis mathieu pendant cinq minutes"), steps)

    assert reason is None
    assert kept == steps


def test_follow_person_rejects_ungrounded_stand_off() -> None:
    steps = [step("follow_person", "face", person="Mathieu", duration_s=300, stand_off_cm=100)]

    kept, reason, _ = pg.grounding_check(preprocess("suis mathieu pendant cinq minutes"), steps)

    assert kept == []
    assert reason is not None

### FILE: tests/unit/test_plan_guards_grounding.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from irobot.app.interfaces import plan_guards as pg


def step(intent, target=None, **params):
    return {"intent": intent, "target": target, "parameters": params}


def P(**params):
    return {"parameters": params}


CASES = [
    # intent non justifié
    ("chante une chanson", [step("spin_360")], None, "intent 'spin_360' non justifié par la phrase", []),
    # stop parasite supprimé, le reste conservé
    ("avance de 2 metres", [step("stop"), step("move_forward", distance=2)],
     [step("move_forward", distance=2)], None, ["stop parasite supprimé"]),
    # paramètre inutile
    ("avance de 2 metres", [step("move_forward", distance=2, speed=5)],
     [step("move_forward", distance=2)], None, ["move_forward.speed inutile : supprimé"]),
    # nombre absent, phrase sans chiffre : supprimé puis défaut ajouté
    ("avance", [step("move_forward", distance=3)],
     [step("move_forward", duration=1)], None, ["move_forward.distance=3 absent de la phrase : supprimé"]),
    # nombre absent, phrase avec chiffres : rejet
    ("avance de 2 metres", [step("move_forward", distance=5)],
     None, "move_forward.distance=5 ne correspond à aucun nombre de la phrase", []),
    # duration 1 tolérée, rien à ajouter
    ("avance", [step("move_forward", duration=1)], [step("move_forward", duration=1)], None, []),
    # angle 90 toléré
    ("tourne a gauche", [step("turn_left", angle=90)], [step("turn_left", angle=90)], None, []),
    # duration 1 non tolérée pour turn_left
    ("tourne a gauche", [step("turn_left", duration=1)], [step("turn_left", angle=90)], None,
     ["turn_left.duration=1 absent de la phrase : supprimé"]),
    # durée par défaut ajoutée
    ("recule", [step("move_backward")], [step("move_backward", duration=1)], None, []),
    # angle par défaut ajouté
    ("tourne a droite", [step("turn_right")], [step("turn_right", angle=90)], None, []),
    # parameters absent : défaut ajouté
    ("avance", [{"intent": "move_forward"}], [{"intent": "move_forward", "parameters": {"duration": 1}}], None, []),
    # étape non dict
    ("avance", ["avance"], None, "étape invalide", []),
    # marqueur
    ("suis moi", [step("follow_person", target="yellow")], None,
     "moi désigne le visage : target doit être face", []),
    # refus de suivi
    ("suis le chat", [step("follow_object")], None, "unsupported_follow_target", []),
    # intent sans règle : aucun contrôle
    ("avance", [step("execute_path", foo=1)], [step("execute_path", foo=1)], None, []),
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("text", "steps", "kept", "reason", "notes"), CASES)
def test_grounding_check_behavior(text, steps, kept, reason, notes):
    got_kept, got_reason, got_notes = pg.grounding_check(text, steps)

    assert got_reason == reason
    assert got_kept == ([] if kept is None else kept)
    assert got_notes == notes


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("value", "expected"),
    [
        ({"intent": "follow_object", "target": "chat"}, True),
        ({"intent": "follow_object", "parameters": "x"}, True),
        ({"intent": "follow_object", "parameters": {"target_object": "chat"}}, True),
        ({"intent": "follow_object", "target": "red"}, False),
        ({"intent": "move_forward", "target": "chat"}, False),
        ([{"steps": [{"name": "follow_person", "target": "chat"}]}], True),
        ("texte", False),
    ],
)
def test_contains_unsupported_follow_step(value, expected):
    assert pg.contains_unsupported_follow_step(value) is expected


def test_steps_are_reordered_to_follow_sentence_verbs():
    text = "recule, tourne a droite puis avance"
    steps = [step("turn_right", angle=90), step("move_backward", duration=1), step("move_forward", duration=1)]
    kept, reason, notes = pg.grounding_check(text, steps)
    assert reason is None
    assert [s["intent"] for s in kept] == ["move_backward", "turn_right", "move_forward"]
    assert "étapes réordonnées selon l'ordre des verbes de la phrase" in notes


def test_ordered_steps_are_left_unchanged():
    text = "recule, tourne a droite puis avance"
    steps = [step("move_backward", duration=1), step("turn_right", angle=90), step("move_forward", duration=1)]
    kept, reason, notes = pg.grounding_check(text, steps)
    assert reason is None
    assert [s["intent"] for s in kept] == ["move_backward", "turn_right", "move_forward"]
    assert not any("réordonnées" in n for n in notes)

### FILE: tests/unit/test_primitive_safety_stop.py
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from irobot.common.interfaces.robot_controller import RobotController
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("phrase", "motion", "reason"),
    [
        ("avance pendant cinq secondes", "forward", "obstacle"),
        ("tourne à gauche pendant cinq secondes", "left", "obstacle"),
        ("tourne à droite pendant cinq secondes", "right", "obstacle"),
        ("avance pendant cinq secondes", "forward", "emergency_stop"),
        ("recule pendant cinq secondes", "backward", "emergency_stop"),
    ],
)
def test_safety_stop_during_primitive_aborts_plan_without_retry(tmp_path, phrase, motion, reason):
    now = 0.0
    fake = FakeRobot()

    def sleep(seconds):
        nonlocal now
        now += seconds
        if reason == "emergency_stop":
            controller.request_emergency_stop()

    controller = RobotController(fake, time_fn=lambda: now, sleep_fn=sleep)
    fake.check_obstacle = lambda: reason == "obstacle" and now >= 0.02 and fake.commands[-1] != "stop"
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=controller, use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan(phrase + " puis recule de dix centimètres")

    assert result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert result["mission"]["phase"] == "aborted"
    assert len(result["steps"]) == 1
    step = result["steps"][0]
    assert step["attempt"] == 1
    assert step["results"][0].data["motion"] == "stop"
    flag = "obstacle_detected" if reason == "obstacle" else "emergency_stop"
    assert step["results"][0].data[flag] is True
    assert fake.commands.count(motion) == 1
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"
    assert fake.check_obstacle() is False
    assert now < 5
    events = [json.loads(line)["event"] for line in Path(result["trace_path"]).read_text().splitlines()]
    assert ("obstacle_stop" if reason == "obstacle" else "emergency_stop") in events


def test_initial_obstacle_is_traced_and_prevents_motion(tmp_path):
    fake = FakeRobot()
    fake.obstacle_detected = True
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=RobotController(fake), use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance pendant cinq secondes")

    assert result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert "forward" not in fake.commands
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"
    assert '"event": "obstacle_stop"' in Path(result["trace_path"]).read_text()


def test_uninterrupted_primitive_runs_full_duration(tmp_path):
    now = 0.0
    fake = FakeRobot()

    def sleep(seconds):
        nonlocal now
        now += seconds

    controller = RobotController(fake, time_fn=lambda: now, sleep_fn=sleep)
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=controller, use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance pendant cinq secondes")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    assert now == pytest.approx(5)
    assert fake.commands.count("forward") == 1
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_motion_exception_still_stops_motor():
    fake = FakeRobot()

    def sleep(seconds):
        raise RuntimeError("interruption du temporisateur")

    controller = RobotController(fake, sleep_fn=sleep)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="temporisateur"):
        controller.forward_for(5)
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_robot_controller_structure.py
"""Controller extraction limits and public compatibility, without hardware."""

import ast
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import unittest

from irobot.common.interfaces import RobotController


INTERFACES = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "src/irobot/common/interfaces"
CONTROLLER_FILES = (
    "robot_controller.py",
)


class RobotControllerStructureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_modules_and_functions_respect_line_limits(self):
        for name in CONTROLLER_FILES:
            source = (INTERFACES / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            with self.subTest(module=name):
                self.assertLessEqual(len(source.splitlines()), 500)
            for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(source)):
                if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                    start = min([node.lineno] + [item.lineno for item in node.decorator_list])
                    with self.subTest(module=name, function=node.name):
                        self.assertLessEqual(node.end_lineno - start + 1, 50)

    def test_controller_logic_never_imports_concrete_hardware(self):
        forbidden = ("irobot.common.hardware", "gpiozero", "RPi", "cv2", "picamera", "vosk")
        for name in CONTROLLER_FILES:
            tree = ast.parse((INTERFACES / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            for node in ast.walk(tree):
                imports = []
                if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
                    imports = [alias.name for alias in node.names]
                elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
                    imports = [node.module or ""]
                if imports:
                    with self.subTest(module=name, line=node.lineno):
                        self.assertFalse(any(module.startswith(forbidden) for module in imports))

    def test_controller_delegates_to_injected_driver(self):
        calls = []
        controller = RobotController(SimpleNamespace(
            speed=0.5, stop=lambda: calls.append("stop"),
            forward=lambda: calls.append("forward"),
            custom=lambda: calls.append("custom"),
        ))
        self.assertEqual(controller.speed, 0.5)
        controller.forward()
        controller.custom()
        controller.stop()
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["forward", "custom", "stop"])

    def test_timed_motion_keeps_clock_period_and_final_stop(self):
        calls = []
        now = 0.0

        def sleep(duration):
            nonlocal now
            calls.append(("sleep", duration))
            now += duration

        driver = SimpleNamespace(
            forward=lambda: calls.append("forward"),
            stop=lambda: calls.append("stop"),
            check_obstacle=lambda: False,
        )
        controller = RobotController(driver, time_fn=lambda: now, sleep_fn=sleep)
        self.assertIsNone(controller.forward_for(0.05))
        self.assertEqual(calls[0], "forward")
        self.assertEqual(calls[-1], "stop")
        waits = [duration for kind, duration in calls[1:-1]]
        self.assertEqual(len(waits), 3)
        self.assertTrue(all(0 < duration <= controller.CONTROL_LOOP_PERIOD_S for duration in waits))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(now, 0.05)
        calls.clear()

        def fail_sleep(_):
            raise OSError("sleep failed")

        controller.sleep_fn = fail_sleep
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(OSError, "sleep failed"):
            controller.forward_for(0.05)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["forward", "stop"])

### FILE: tests/unit/test_robot_test_cli.py
import argparse
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from irobot.tools.robot_test import cli
from irobot.tools.robot_test.command_parser import (
    Command,
    parse_command_chain,
    parse_voice_command,
)

from irobot.common.interfaces.wake_word import split_wake_word


def test_single_command_with_spoken_duration():
    assert parse_voice_command("suis moi pendant dix secondes") == Command("follow", "person:Moi", 10.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("avance") == Command("move", "forward", 1.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("cherche la balle rouge") == Command("search", "red_ball", 15.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("cherche Quentin") == Command("search", "person:Quentin", 15.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("approche toi de Quentin") == Command("approach", "person:Quentin", 15.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["", "bonjour", "cherche", "suis"])
def test_incomplete_phrases_are_rejected(text):
    assert parse_voice_command(text) is None


def test_chain_is_split_on_puis():
    assert parse_command_chain("avance deux secondes puis tourne a gauche puis approche la balle jaune") == [
        Command("move", "forward", 2.0),
        Command("move", "left", 1.0),
        Command("approach", "yellow_ball", 15.0),
    ]


def test_chain_is_all_or_nothing():
    assert parse_command_chain("avance puis blabla") is None
    assert parse_command_chain("avance puis") is None


def test_command_validates_vocabulary():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Command("move", "human")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Command("follow", "forward")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Command("search", "person:")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Command("dance", "forward")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        Command("move", "forward", 0)


def test_wake_word_split():
    assert split_wake_word("Robot avance puis recule") == "avance puis recule"
    assert split_wake_word("robot") == ""
    assert split_wake_word("avance") is None
    assert split_wake_word("ok jarvis recule", "jarvis") == "recule"


def _args(**kwargs):
    return argparse.Namespace(wake_word="robot", input_device="auto", **kwargs)


def _patch_audio(monkeypatch, transcripts):
    import irobot.common.hardware.audio.audio_utils as audio_utils
    import irobot.common.hardware.audio.voice_input as voice_input

    monkeypatch.setattr(audio_utils, "resolve_input_device", lambda device: device)
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_input, "transcribe_loop", lambda device: (text for text in transcripts))


def test_listen_loop_requires_wake_word_then_runs_chain(monkeypatch):
    executed = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "_run_commands", lambda registry, hardware, commands: executed.append(commands))
    _patch_audio(
        monkeypatch,
        [
            "avance",  # sans mot d'activation : ignoré
            "robot",  # armement
            "recule puis tourne a droite",  # commande sur la phrase suivante
            "robot suis moi dix secondes",  # mot d'activation et commande ensemble
            "robot blabla",  # incompris : rien n'est exécuté
        ],
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="Flux audio terminé"):
        cli._listen_and_run(None, {}, _args())
    assert executed == [
        [Command("move", "backward"), Command("move", "right")],
        [Command("follow", "person:Moi", 10.0)],
    ]


def test_run_commands_stops_at_first_failure(monkeypatch):
    calls = []

    def runner(label, status):
        def run(registry, hardware, command):
            calls.append(label)
            return SimpleNamespace(status=status, message="", data={})

        return run

    monkeypatch.setitem(cli.RUNNERS, "move", runner("move", "failed"))
    monkeypatch.setitem(cli.RUNNERS, "follow", runner("follow", "success"))
    commands = [Command("move", "forward"), Command("follow", "human")]
    released = []
    camera = SimpleNamespace(release_target=lambda: released.append(True))
    assert cli._run_commands(None, {"camera": camera}, commands) is False
    assert calls == ["move"]
    assert released == [True]


def test_cli_builds_a_validated_command():
    _, commands = cli._parse_args(["--behavior", "follow", "--target", "human", "--duration", "200"])
    assert commands == [Command("follow", "human", 200.0)]
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        cli._parse_args(["--behavior", "move", "--target", "marker"])


def test_cli_text_builds_a_chain_with_optional_wake_word():
    expected = [Command("move", "forward", 2.0), Command("follow", "person:Moi", 15.0)]
    for text in ("avance deux secondes puis suis moi", "robot avance deux secondes puis suis moi"):
        assert cli._parse_args(["--text", text])[1] == expected


def test_cli_text_builds_named_search_and_approach_commands():
    assert cli._parse_args(["--text", "cherche Quentin"])[1] == [
        Command("search", "person:Quentin", 15.0),
    ]
    assert cli._parse_args(["--text", "approche toi de Quentin"])[1] == [
        Command("approach", "person:Quentin", 15.0),
    ]


def test_cli_text_rejects_unknown_or_conflicting_input():
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        cli._parse_args(["--text", "blabla"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        cli._parse_args(["--text", "avance", "--audio"])


def test_follow_named_person():
    assert parse_voice_command("suis Mathieu") == Command("follow", "person:Mathieu", 15.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("suis Quentin pendant 5 secondes") == Command("follow", "person:Quentin", 5.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("suis la balle rouge") == Command("follow", "red_ball", 15.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("suis un humain") == Command("follow", "human", 15.0)
    assert parse_voice_command("suis la balle") is None
    assert parse_voice_command("approche moi") == Command("approach", "human", 15.0)


def test_cli_accepts_person_target():
    _, commands = cli._parse_args(["--behavior", "follow", "--target", "person:Mathieu"])
    assert commands == [Command("follow", "person:Mathieu", 15.0)]
    for behavior in ("search", "approach", "follow"):
        _, commands = cli._parse_args(["--behavior", behavior, "--target", "person:Bob"])
        assert commands == [Command(behavior, "person:Bob", 15.0)]
    for argv in (
        ["--behavior", "follow", "--target", "person:"],
        ["--behavior", "search", "--target", "person:"],
        ["--behavior", "move", "--target", "person:Bob"],
    ):
        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
            cli._parse_args(argv)


def test_follow_unknown_person_fails_without_starting_the_skill():
    gallery = SimpleNamespace(resolve=lambda name: None)
    hardware = {"camera": SimpleNamespace(set_face_target=lambda *a: pytest.fail("camera touched")),
                "gallery": gallery}
    result = cli._run_follow(None, hardware, Command("follow", "person:Bob"))
    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert "Personne inconnue : Bob" in result.message


def test_approach_named_person_targets_gallery_face_and_clears_it(monkeypatch):
    embedding = object()
    camera = SimpleNamespace(calls=[], set_face_target=lambda *args: camera.calls.append(args))
    gallery = SimpleNamespace(resolve=lambda name: "Quentin", get=lambda name: embedding)
    hardware = {"robot": None, "sensor": None, "camera": camera, "gallery": gallery}
    captured = {}

    class _Executor:
        def __init__(self, context):
            captured["context"] = context

        def run(self, skill):
            captured["skill"] = skill
            return SimpleNamespace(status="success", message="", data={})

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "SkillExecutor", _Executor)
    registry = SimpleNamespace(get=lambda name: name)

    result = cli._run_approach(registry, hardware, Command("approach", "person:quentin"))

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert camera.calls == [("Quentin", embedding), (None,)]
    assert captured["context"].perception_target.value == "face"
    assert captured["context"].state.active_intent_parameters["target_object"] == "human"
    assert captured["skill"] == "approach_target"


def test_approach_unknown_person_fails_without_selecting_face():
    gallery = SimpleNamespace(resolve=lambda name: None)
    camera = SimpleNamespace(set_face_target=lambda *args: pytest.fail("camera touched"))

    result = cli._run_approach(None, {"camera": camera, "gallery": gallery}, Command("approach", "person:Bob"))

    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert "Personne inconnue : Bob" in result.message

### FILE: tests/unit/test_runtime_factory_gallery.py
import pytest

from irobot.common.hardware.runtime_factory import load_face_gallery


def test_load_face_gallery_returns_none_when_unavailable(monkeypatch, capsys):
    pytest.importorskip("cv2")
    from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.face_gallery import FaceGallery

    def _missing(settings):
        raise FileNotFoundError("assets/face introuvable")

    monkeypatch.setattr(FaceGallery, "load", staticmethod(_missing))

    assert load_face_gallery(object()) is None
    assert "[RUNTIME] galerie de visages indisponible" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_load_face_gallery_returns_gallery(monkeypatch):
    pytest.importorskip("cv2")
    from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.face_gallery import FaceGallery

    gallery = object()
    monkeypatch.setattr(FaceGallery, "load", staticmethod(lambda settings: gallery))

    assert load_face_gallery(object()) is gallery

### FILE: tests/unit/test_runtime_settings.py
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from irobot.common.config import settings
from irobot.common.config.llm_settings import LLMSettings as SeparateLLMSettings
from irobot.common.config.llm_settings import load_llm_settings as load_separate_llm_settings


def test_runtime_recovery_attempts_default_and_loaded(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    assert settings.RuntimeSettings().target_recovery_attempts == 1
    cfg = tmp_path / "runtime_settings.toml"
    cfg.write_text("[runtime]\ntarget_recovery_attempts = 3\n")
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)
    assert settings.load_runtime_settings().target_recovery_attempts == 3
    for value in ("-1", "1.5"):
        cfg.write_text(f"[runtime]\ntarget_recovery_attempts = {value}\n")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="target_recovery_attempts"):
            settings.load_runtime_settings()


def test_target_loss_and_align_settle_settings(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    defaults = settings.RuntimeSettings()
    assert (defaults.target_loss_wait_seconds, defaults.align_settle_seconds,
            defaults.target_recovery_window_seconds) == (2.0, 0.4, 1.5)
    cfg = tmp_path / "runtime_settings.toml"
    cfg.write_text(
        "[runtime]\ntarget_loss_wait_seconds = 0.5\nalign_settle_seconds = 0.2\ntarget_recovery_window_seconds = 1.0\n",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)
    loaded = settings.load_runtime_settings()
    assert (loaded.target_loss_wait_seconds, loaded.align_settle_seconds,
            loaded.target_recovery_window_seconds) == (0.5, 0.2, 1.0)
    invalid = (
        "target_loss_wait_seconds = 0",
        "target_recovery_window_seconds = 0",
        "align_settle_seconds = -0.1",
        "align_settle_seconds = 1.0\ntarget_recovery_window_seconds = 0.5",
    )
    for lines in invalid:
        cfg.write_text(f"[runtime]\n{lines}\n")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            settings.load_runtime_settings()


def test_align_step_settings_defaults_load_and_validation(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    defaults = settings.RuntimeSettings()
    assert (defaults.align_gain, defaults.align_step_min_seconds, defaults.align_step_max_seconds) == (0.6, 0.03, 0.25)
    cfg = tmp_path / "runtime_settings.toml"
    cfg.write_text("[runtime]\nalign_gain = 0.5\nalign_step_min_seconds = 0.02\nalign_step_max_seconds = 0.2\n")
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)
    loaded = settings.load_runtime_settings()
    assert (loaded.align_gain, loaded.align_step_min_seconds, loaded.align_step_max_seconds) == (0.5, 0.02, 0.2)
    for lines in ("align_gain = 1.5", "align_gain = 0", "align_step_min_seconds = 0.3\nalign_step_max_seconds = 0.2"):
        cfg.write_text(f"[runtime]\n{lines}\n")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            settings.load_runtime_settings()


def test_load_runtime_settings_defaults_when_file_missing(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: Path("/tmp/nonexistent_runtime_settings.toml"))

    loaded = settings.load_runtime_settings()

    assert loaded == settings.RuntimeSettings()

    }
    assert {name: getattr(loaded, name) for name in expected} == expected
    assert Path(loaded.camera_face_model_path).resolve().is_file()


def test_runtime_loader_rejects_unknown_top_level_and_section_keys(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    cfg = tmp_path / "runtime_settings.toml"
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)

    cfg.write_text("[runtime]\ncm_per_sec = 33.0\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"Unknown key\(s\) in \[runtime\].*cm_per_sec"):
        settings.load_runtime_settings()

    cfg.write_text("[camera]\nultrasonic_fusion = false\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"Unknown key\(s\) in \[camera\].*ultrasonic_fusion"):
        settings.load_runtime_settings()

    cfg.write_text("unexpected = true\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Unknown top-level key.*unexpected"):
        settings.load_runtime_settings()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("toml", "error"),
    [
        ("[camera]\nclahe_enabled = 1\n", "camera_clahe_enabled"),
        ('[camera]\nfocal_px = "680"\n', "camera_focal_px"),
        ("[limits]\nmax_plan_steps = 1.5\n", "limits.max_plan_steps"),
        ("[runtime]\nobstacle_threshold_cm = -1.0\n", "obstacle_threshold_cm"),
        ("[human_follow]\nmax_age_seconds = true\n", "human_follow.max_age_seconds"),
        ("[camera]\nfocal_px = nan\n", "camera_focal_px"),
        ("[camera]\ncapture_size_px = [640, 0]\n", "camera.capture_size_px"),
        ("[camera]\noriented_frame_size_px = [480, true]\n", "camera.oriented_frame_size_px"),
        ("[camera]\nedge_offset_threshold = 1.5\n", "camera_edge_offset_threshold"),
        ("[camera]\nedge_offset_threshold = 0\n", "camera_edge_offset_threshold"),
    ],
)
def test_runtime_loader_rejects_wrong_toml_types(tmp_path, monkeypatch, toml, error):
    cfg = tmp_path / "runtime_settings.toml"
    cfg.write_text(toml, encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=error):
        settings.load_runtime_settings()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="camera must be a TOML table"):

def test_llm_settings_have_dedicated_module_and_compatibility_exports():

def test_load_runtime_settings_from_toml(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    cfg = tmp_path / "runtime_settings.toml"
    cfg.write_text(
        """
[runtime]
cm_per_second = 30.0
deg_per_second = 150.0
drive_speed = 0.7
obstacle_threshold_cm = 7.5
step_timeout_seconds = 3.5
mission_timeout_seconds = 20.0
max_step_retries = 1

[limits]
max_plan_steps = 10
max_distance_cm = 180.0
max_angle_deg = 120.0
max_duration_sec = 8.0
""".strip(),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, "_config_file", lambda: cfg)

    loaded = settings.load_runtime_settings()

    assert loaded.cm_per_second == 30.0
    assert loaded.backward_cm_per_second == 25.0
    assert loaded.deg_per_second == 150.0
    assert loaded.drive_speed == 0.7
    assert loaded.obstacle_threshold_cm == 7.5
    assert loaded.step_timeout_seconds == 3.5
    assert loaded.mission_timeout_seconds == 20.0
    assert loaded.max_step_retries == 1
    assert loaded.max_plan_steps == 10
    assert loaded.max_distance_cm == 180.0
    assert loaded.max_angle_deg == 120.0
    assert loaded.max_duration_sec == 8.0

### FILE: tests/unit/test_scenario_yellow_ball.py
"""Scénario bout en bout « cherche la balle jaune, approche-toi à 1 m puis suis-la 5 min ».

VoiceRuntime (sans LLM, synchrone) -> Intent -> validation -> Skills -> RobotController -> robot simulé.
Horloge entièrement simulée : aucune attente réelle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.common.domain.orchestrator.registry import SkillRegistry
from irobot.common.domain.skills import ApproachTargetSkill, FollowObjectSkill
from irobot.common.interfaces.robot_controller import RobotController
from irobot.app.interfaces.voice_runtime import VoiceRuntime
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot


SENTENCE = "cherche la balle jaune, approche-toi à 1 m puis suis-la pendant 5 min"

LINEAR_CM_S = RuntimeSettings().cm_per_second
TURN_DEG_S = 120.0
HALF_FOV_DEG = 25.0
CENTER_BAND_DEG = 8.0
STAND_OFF_CM = 100.0
TOLERANCE_CM = 15.0
FOLLOW_S = 300.0
MOTIONS = {"forward", "backward", "left", "right"}

APPEAR_AT_S = 4.0
INITIAL_DISTANCE_CM = 220.0
INITIAL_BEARING_DEG = 20.0
OUT_OF_FIELD_S = (160.0, 162.0)
# (début, fin, vitesse radiale cm/s : > 0 s'éloigne, vitesse latérale °/s)
BALL_MOTION = [
    (20.0, 80.0, 8.0, 0.0),
    (100.0, 150.0, -6.0, 0.0),
    (170.0, 230.0, 5.0, -0.3),
    (230.0, 290.0, -5.0, 0.3),
]


def _integral(t: float, column: int) -> float:
    return sum(seg[column] * max(0.0, min(t, seg[1]) - seg[0]) for seg in BALL_MOTION)


class SimWorld(FakeRobot):
    """Robot 2WD + balle jaune + caméra simulés ; le mouvement est intégré à chaque sleep()."""

    def __init__(self, *, ball_appears: bool = True, emergency_at: float | None = None, obstacle_at: float | None = None):
        super().__init__(time_fn=self.time)
        self.now = 0.0
        self.x_cm = 0.0
        self.heading_deg = 0.0
        self.ball_appears = ball_appears
        self.ball_anchor_deg: float | None = None
        self.emergency_at = emergency_at
        self.obstacle_at = obstacle_at
        self.controller: RobotController | None = None
        self.observations: list[tuple[float, str | None, float | None]] = []
        self.targets: set[str | None] = set()

    def time(self) -> float:
        return self.now

    def sleep(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        dt = max(0.0, float(seconds))
        motion = self.commands[-1] if self.commands else "stop"
        if motion == "forward":
            self.x_cm += LINEAR_CM_S * dt
        elif motion == "backward":
            self.x_cm -= LINEAR_CM_S * dt
        elif motion == "right":
            self.heading_deg += TURN_DEG_S * dt
        elif motion == "left":
            self.heading_deg -= TURN_DEG_S * dt
        self.now += dt
        if self.emergency_at is not None and self.now >= self.emergency_at and self.controller is not None:
            self.emergency_at = None
            # Équivaut au thread d'écoute qui entend « stop » pendant la mission.
            self.controller.request_emergency_stop()

    def check_obstacle(self) -> bool:
        return self.obstacle_at is not None and self.now >= self.obstacle_at

    def distance_cm(self) -> float:
        return INITIAL_DISTANCE_CM + _integral(self.now, 2) - self.x_cm

    def detect(self, *, target):
        target = target.value
        self.targets.add(target)
        if not self.ball_appears or self.now < APPEAR_AT_S:
            self.observations.append((self.now, None, None))
            return None
        if self.ball_anchor_deg is None:
            self.ball_anchor_deg = self.heading_deg + INITIAL_BEARING_DEG
        bearing = self.ball_anchor_deg + _integral(self.now, 3) - self.heading_deg
        if OUT_OF_FIELD_S[0] <= self.now < OUT_OF_FIELD_S[1]:
            bearing += 2 * HALF_FOV_DEG
        if abs(bearing) > HALF_FOV_DEG:
            self.observations.append((self.now, None, None))
            return None
        position = "right" if bearing > CENTER_BAND_DEG else "left" if bearing < -CENTER_BAND_DEG else "center"
        distance = self.distance_cm()
        self.observations.append((self.now, position, distance))
        return SimpleNamespace(
            label=f"{target}_ball", position=position, distance_cm=distance,
            offset_x=bearing / HALF_FOV_DEG,
        )


def _timed(skill, world: SimWorld, spans: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float, object]]]):
    execute = skill.execute

    def wrapper(context):
        started = world.time()
        result = execute(context)
        spans.setdefault(skill.name, []).append((started, world.time(), result))
        return result

    skill.execute = wrapper
    return skill


def run_scenario(tmp_path: Path, *, settings: RuntimeSettings | None = None, **world_kwargs):
    world = SimWorld(**world_kwargs)
    controller = RobotController(world, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep)
    world.controller = controller
    settings = settings or RuntimeSettings()
    spans: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float, object]]] = {}
    registry = SkillRegistry.default()
    registry.register(_timed(ApproachTargetSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep), world, spans))
    registry.register(_timed(FollowObjectSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep), world, spans))
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(
        robot=controller,
        perception=world,
                registry=registry,
        use_llm=False,
        threaded=False,
        trace_dir=str(tmp_path),
        wake_word="",
    )
    result = runtime.handle_transcript(SENTENCE)
    events = [json.loads(line) for line in Path(result["trace_path"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    return world, result, events, spans


def _event_names(events: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    return [event["event"] for event in events]


def _step_intents(events: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    return [event["intent"]["name"] for event in events if event["event"] == "step_result"]


def _assert_stopped_since(world: SimWorld, since_s: float) -> None:
    after = [cmd for t, cmd in world.timed_commands if t >= since_s]
    assert after and set(after) == {"stop"}, after
    first_stop_at = next(t for t, cmd in world.timed_commands if t >= since_s)
    assert first_stop_at <= since_s + 0.05
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_full_mission_search_approach_and_follow_five_minutes(tmp_path):
    world, result, events, spans = run_scenario(tmp_path, settings=RuntimeSettings(target_loss_wait_seconds=3.0))

    assert result["status"] == "accepted", result
    assert result["mission"]["phase"] == "success"
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in result["steps"]] == ["search_and_approach_object", "follow_object"]
    assert world.targets == {"yellow_ball"}

    # 1. Balle absente pendant 4 s, puis vue à droite à 220 cm.
    assert all(pos is None for t, pos, _ in world.observations if t < APPEAR_AT_S)
    first_seen = next(obs for obs in world.observations if obs[1] is not None)
    assert first_seen[0] >= APPEAR_AT_S
    assert first_seen[1] == "right"
    assert abs(first_seen[2] - INITIAL_DISTANCE_CM) < 1.0
    assert any(cmd == "right" for t, cmd in world.timed_commands if t < APPEAR_AT_S)

    # 2. Centrage puis approche jusqu'au stand-off.
    approach_end = spans["approach_target"][-1][1]
    approach_result = spans["approach_target"][-1][2]
    assert approach_result.status == "success"
    assert approach_result.data["close_enough"] is True
    assert abs(approach_result.data["distance_cm"] - STAND_OFF_CM) <= TOLERANCE_CM
    assert any(pos == "center" for t, pos, _ in world.observations if APPEAR_AT_S <= t <= approach_end)
    assert approach_end < BALL_MOTION[0][0]

    # 3. Suivi pendant 300 s simulées à 100 ± 15 cm, malgré éloignement, rapprochement et sortie du champ.
    follow_start, follow_end, follow_result = spans["follow_object"][0]
    assert follow_result.status == "success"
    assert follow_result.data["duration_s"] == FOLLOW_S
    assert FOLLOW_S <= follow_end - follow_start < FOLLOW_S + 0.5
    followed = [(t, pos, d) for t, pos, d in world.observations if follow_start <= t <= follow_end]
    distances = [d for _, pos, d in followed if pos is not None]
    assert len(distances) > 1000
    # Le robot ne réagit qu'après une période de boucle et une impulsion avant : la balle peut s'éloigner pendant ce délai.
    reaction_s = FollowObjectSkill.CONTROL_LOOP_PERIOD_S + FollowObjectSkill.FORWARD_MAX_S
    max_drift_cm_s = max(seg[2] for seg in BALL_MOTION)
    assert all(
        abs(d - STAND_OFF_CM) <= TOLERANCE_CM + max_drift_cm_s * reaction_s for d in distances
    ), (min(distances), max(distances))
    follow_cmds = {cmd for t, cmd in world.timed_commands if t >= follow_start}
    assert {"forward", "backward"} <= follow_cmds
    # Rotation seulement près du bord de l'image : au moins une correction latérale pendant le suivi.
    assert {"left", "right"} & follow_cmds
    lost = [t for t, pos, _ in followed if pos is None]
    assert lost and all(OUT_OF_FIELD_S[0] <= t < OUT_OF_FIELD_S[1] for t in lost)
    assert not [cmd for t, cmd in world.timed_commands if OUT_OF_FIELD_S[0] <= t < OUT_OF_FIELD_S[1] and cmd in MOTIONS]

    # 4. Fin de mission : robot arrêté, stop en dernier.
    _assert_stopped_since(world, follow_end)
    names = _event_names(events)
    assert names[:3] == ["input", "plan_raw", "plan_validated"]
    assert names[-1] == "mission_result"
    assert events[-1]["status"] == "accepted"
    assert "emergency_stop" not in names and "obstacle_stop" not in names
    assert _step_intents(events)[-1] == "follow_object"
    assert [e["status"] for e in events if e["event"] == "step_result"][-1] == "accepted"


def test_ball_never_found_ends_incomplete_without_following(tmp_path):
    world, result, events, spans = run_scenario(tmp_path, ball_appears=False)

    assert result["status"] == "incomplete", result
    assert result["mission"]["phase"] == "failed"
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in result["steps"]] == ["search_and_approach_object"]
    assert "follow_object" not in spans
    searches = spans["approach_target"]
    assert searches and all(r.status == "failed" and r.data["target_lost"] is True for _, _, r in searches)
    search_timeout = RuntimeSettings().search_timeout_seconds
    assert all(end - start >= search_timeout for start, end, _ in searches)
    assert all(pos is None for _, pos, _ in world.observations)

    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"
    _assert_stopped_since(world, searches[-1][1])
    names = _event_names(events)
    assert names[-1] == "mission_result" and events[-1]["status"] == "incomplete"
    assert set(_step_intents(events)) == {"search_and_approach_object"}
    assert "emergency_stop" not in names and "obstacle_stop" not in names


def test_emergency_stop_at_60s_interrupts_mission(tmp_path):
    world, result, events, spans = run_scenario(tmp_path, emergency_at=60.0)

    assert result["status"] == "aborted", result
    assert result["mission"]["phase"] == "aborted"
    assert result["mission"]["last_error"] == "emergency stop"
    follow_start, follow_end, follow_result = spans["follow_object"][0]
    assert follow_start < 60.0 <= follow_end < 60.5
    assert follow_result.data["emergency_stop"] is True
    assert result["steps"][-1]["reason"] == "emergency stop"

    _assert_stopped_since(world, 60.0)
    names = _event_names(events)
    assert "emergency_stop" in names
    assert names.index("emergency_stop") < names.index("mission_result")
    assert events[-1]["status"] == "aborted"
    assert len([e for e in events if e["event"] == "step_result" and e["intent"]["name"] == "follow_object"]) == 1


def test_obstacle_at_100s_stops_robot_and_aborts_mission(tmp_path):
    world, result, events, spans = run_scenario(tmp_path, obstacle_at=100.0)

    assert result["status"] == "aborted", result
    assert result["mission"]["last_error"] == "obstacle"
    follow_start, follow_end, follow_result = spans["follow_object"][0]
    assert follow_start < 100.0 <= follow_end < 100.5
    assert follow_result.data["obstacle_detected"] is True
    assert "emergency_stop" not in follow_result.data

    # Une impulsion de recul (sans contrôle d'obstacle avant) peut finir après 100 s : aucune commande avant n'est émise.
    assert not [cmd for t, cmd in world.timed_commands if t >= 100.0 and cmd in {"forward", "left", "right"}]
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"
    assert world.timed_commands[-1][0] >= 100.0
    names = _event_names(events)
    assert "obstacle_stop" in names and "emergency_stop" not in names
    assert names.index("obstacle_stop") < names.index("mission_result")
    assert events[-1]["status"] == "aborted"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_skills_behaviors.py
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings, load_runtime_settings
from irobot.common.interfaces import RobotController
from irobot.common.domain.skills import SkillContext
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.approach_target import ApproachTargetSkill
from irobot.common.domain.skills.behaviors.move_circle import MoveCircleSkill
from irobot.common.domain.perception import PerceptionTarget
from tests.unit.fake_robot import FakeRobot



TOML_FOCAL_PX = load_runtime_settings().camera_focal_px


def build_context(fake_robot: FakeRobot | None = None, **state):
    robot = fake_robot or FakeRobot()
    return SkillContext(robot=RobotController(robot), state=state)


def test_approach_target_skill_success_when_target_visible():

    assert result.status == "in_progress"
    assert result.data["target_position"] is None
    assert result.next_action == "detect_object"
    assert "right" in fake.commands


class FakeClock:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.now = 0.0

    def time(self) -> float:
        return self.now

    def sleep(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        self.now += max(0.0, seconds)


class ScriptedPerception:
    """Renvoie une détection par appel ; None = cible absente. Répète la dernière valeur.

    Une entrée est (position, distance) ou (position, distance, offset_x).
    """

    def __init__(self, script, clock: FakeClock | None = None, on_call=None) -> None:
        self.script = list(script)
        self.calls = 0

    def detect(self, *, target):
        item = self.script[min(self.calls, len(self.script) - 1)]
        self.calls += 1
        if item is None:
            return None
        position, distance, *rest = item
        offset_x = rest[0] if rest else None
        return SimpleNamespace(label=target.value, position=position, distance_cm=distance, offset_x=offset_x)


def live_run(script, clock=None, robot=None, recovery_attempts=1, **params):
    clock = clock or FakeClock()
    fake = robot or FakeRobot()
    context = SkillContext(
        robot=RobotController(fake),
        perception=ScriptedPerception(script),
        perception_target=PerceptionTarget.YELLOW_BALL,
        state={"active_intent_parameters": {"alignment_step_s": 0.0, "forward_step_s": 0.0, **params}},
    )
    settings = RuntimeSettings(
        min_stand_off_cm=10.0, stand_off_default_cm=20.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=50.0,
        target_recovery_attempts=recovery_attempts, camera_focal_px=TOML_FOCAL_PX,
    )
    skill = ApproachTargetSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=clock.time, sleep_fn=clock.sleep)
    return skill.execute(context), fake, context, clock


def test_approach_target_search_succeeds_after_n_pulses():
    script = [None] * 5 + [("center", 50.0)]
    result, fake, context, _ = live_run(script, max_target_loss_s=0.1, search_timeout_s=30.0, stand_off_cm=100)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert result.data["close_enough"] is True
    assert fake.commands.count("right") == 5


def test_approach_target_search_exhausted_fails_and_stops():
    result, fake, _, clock = live_run([None], search_timeout_s=2.0)

    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert result.data["target_lost"] is True
    assert fake.commands[-1] == "stop"
    assert clock.now >= 2.0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("distance_cm, reached", [(63.0, True), (65.0, False)])
def test_approach_target_reach_uses_stand_off_settings(distance_cm, reached):
    clock = FakeClock()
    context = SkillContext(
        robot=RobotController(FakeRobot()),
        perception=ScriptedPerception([("center", distance_cm)]),
        perception_target=PerceptionTarget.YELLOW_BALL,
        state={"active_intent_parameters": {
            "alignment_step_s": 0.0, "forward_step_s": 0.0, "search_timeout_s": 0.5,
        }},
    )
    settings = RuntimeSettings(stand_off_default_cm=60.0, stand_off_tolerance_percent=5.0)
    skill = ApproachTargetSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=clock.time, sleep_fn=clock.sleep)
    result = skill.execute(context)

    assert (result.status == "success") is reached


def test_approach_target_search_uses_search_timeout_not_target_loss_budget():
    # max_target_loss_s = 1 s ne doit plus interrompre une recherche de 5 s.
    script = [None] * 100 + [("center", 50.0)]
    result, _, _, clock = live_run(script, max_target_loss_s=1.0, search_timeout_s=60.0, stand_off_cm=100)

    assert result.status == "success"
    assert clock.now > 1.0


def test_approach_target_aligns_toward_target_side():
    for side in ("left", "right"):
        result, fake, _, _ = live_run([(side, None)] * 2 + [("center", 50.0)], stand_off_cm=100)
        assert result.status == "success"
        assert side in fake.commands
        opposite = "right" if side == "left" else "left"
        assert opposite not in fake.commands


def test_approach_target_align_pulse_is_proportional_unless_explicit(monkeypatch):
    pulses = []
    original = ApproachTargetSkill._pulse_motion

    def spy(self, context, position, duration):
        pulses.append((position, duration))
        return original(self, context, position, duration)

    monkeypatch.setattr(ApproachTargetSkill, "_pulse_motion", spy)
    script = [("left", 160.0, 0.8)]

    live_run(script, stand_off_cm=100, alignment_step_s=0.05)
    assert ("left", 0.05) in pulses

    pulses.clear()
    live_run(script, stand_off_cm=100, alignment_step_s=None)
    left_pulses = [duration for position, duration in pulses if position == "left"]
    expected = ApproachTargetSkill(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(camera_focal_px=TOML_FOCAL_PX), time_fn=lambda: 0.0, sleep_fn=lambda seconds: None,
    )._align_step_s(0.8, ApproachTargetSkill.DEFAULT_ALIGN_STEP_S)
    assert left_pulses[0] == pytest.approx(expected)
    assert left_pulses[0] != pytest.approx(0.05)


def test_approach_target_search_sweeps_toward_last_known_side():

def test_approach_target_uses_same_policy_for_any_target(live, target_object):

        "target_object": target_object,
        "stand_off_cm": 100,
        "stand_off_tolerance_cm": 10,
        "alignment_step_s": 0.0,
        "forward_step_s": 0.0,

        "search_timeout_s": 0.1,

            active_intent_parameters=params,
            last_object_position="center",
            last_object_distance_cm=None,
        )

    assert "forward" in fake.commands
    assert result.status != "failed"

### FILE: tests/unit/test_smart_car_sensor.py
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import importlib
import sys
import threading
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from irobot.common.hardware.smart_car import smart_car as sc
from irobot.common.hardware.smart_car import cli
from irobot.common.interfaces.robot_controller import RobotController


class _Pin:
    value = 0

    def __init__(self, *a, **k):
        pass

    def on(self):
        pass

    def off(self):
        pass

    def close(self):
        pass


class _Sensor:
    def __init__(self, values):
        self.values = list(values)
        self.calls = 0
        self.cleanup_calls = 0
        self.active = 0
        self.max_active = 0

    def read_distance_cm(self):
        self.calls += 1
        self.active += 1
        self.max_active = max(self.max_active, self.active)
        self.active -= 1
        return self.values.pop(0) if self.values else self.values_default

    values_default = 100.0

    def cleanup(self):
        self.cleanup_calls += 1


class _Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 0.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.now

    def sleep(self, seconds):
        self.now += seconds


def _car(monkeypatch, sensor, clock):
    monkeypatch.setattr(sc, "DigitalOutputDevice", _Pin)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sc, "PWMOutputDevice", _Pin)
    return sc.SmartCar2WD()


def _hc_sr04(monkeypatch, clock):
    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "gpiozero",
        SimpleNamespace(DigitalOutputDevice=_Pin, DigitalInputDevice=_Pin),
    )
    module = importlib.import_module("irobot.common.hardware.sensors.hc_sr04")
    # Le module peut déjà être importé avec le vrai gpiozero (ex. via cli.py sur Raspberry Pi).
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "DigitalOutputDevice", _Pin)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "DigitalInputDevice", _Pin)
    return module.HC_SR04(time_fn=clock, sleep_fn=clock.sleep)


def _mock_sensor_reads(sensor, clock, distances):
    pending = []
    sensor._trigger_pulse = lambda: None

    def wait_for_level(expected, timeout_s=0.05):
        if expected == 1:
            distance = next(distances)
            if distance is None:
                return False
            pending.append(distance)
            return True
        clock.now += pending.pop() * 2 / 34300
        return True

    sensor._wait_for_level = wait_for_level


def test_drive_speed_is_set_by_the_caller(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(sc, "DigitalOutputDevice", _Pin)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sc, "PWMOutputDevice", _Pin)
    car = sc.SmartCar2WD(drive_speed=0.7)
    assert car.speed == 0.7


def test_smart_car_cli_threshold_defaults_to_runtime_settings(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        cli, "load_runtime_settings",
        lambda: RuntimeSettings(obstacle_threshold_cm=9.0),
    )

    args = cli.build_parser().parse_args(["forward"])

    assert args.obstacle_threshold_cm == 9.0


def test_cleanup_releases_gpio_once(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(sc, "DigitalOutputDevice", _Pin)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sc, "PWMOutputDevice", _Pin)
    car = sc.SmartCar2WD()

    car.cleanup()
    car.cleanup()

    assert car._closed is True


def test_smart_car_cli_builds_and_cleans_injected_sensor(monkeypatch):
    sensor = _Sensor([])
    calls = []

    class Car:
        def __init__(self, *, drive_speed):
            self.speed = drive_speed

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def set_speed(self, speed):
            self.speed = speed

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("car cleanup")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "SmartCar2WD", Car)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "HC_SR04", lambda: sensor)
    args = argparse.Namespace(
        command="stop",
        duration=0,
        speed=0.5,
        obstacle_threshold_cm=20.0,
        obstacle_check_interval=0.2,
    )

    cli.run_command(args)

    assert sensor.cleanup_calls == 1
    assert calls == ["stop", "car cleanup"]


def test_smart_car_cli_routes_obstacle_stop_through_controller(monkeypatch, capsys):
    sensor = _Sensor([1.0])
    calls = []

    class Car:
        def __init__(self, *, drive_speed):
            self.speed = drive_speed

        def forward(self):
            calls.append("forward")

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def set_speed(self, speed):
            self.speed = speed

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("car cleanup")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "SmartCar2WD", Car)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "HC_SR04", lambda: sensor)
    args = argparse.Namespace(
        command="forward",
        duration=1,
        speed=0.5,
        obstacle_threshold_cm=20.0,
        obstacle_check_interval=0.2,
    )

    cli.run_command(args)

    assert calls == ["forward", "stop", "car cleanup"]
    assert sensor.cleanup_calls == 1
    assert "Obstacle détecté" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_sensor_cache_within_50ms_then_refresh(monkeypatch):
    clock = _Clock()
    sensor = _hc_sr04(monkeypatch, clock)
    _mock_sensor_reads(sensor, clock, iter([100.0, 80.0]))

    assert sensor.read_distance_cm() == 100.0
    clock.now += 0.049
    assert sensor.read_distance_cm() == 100.0
    clock.now += 0.002
    assert sensor.read_distance_cm() == pytest.approx(80.0)


def test_check_obstacle_retries_missing_sensor_reading(monkeypatch):
    sensor, clock = _Sensor([None, 3.0]), _Clock()
    controller = RobotController(
        _car(monkeypatch, None, clock),
        distance_sensor=sensor,
        obstacle_threshold_cm=5.0,
        sleep_fn=clock.sleep,
    )

    assert controller.check_obstacle() is True
    assert sensor.calls == 2


def test_no_sensor_obstacle_policy(monkeypatch):
    clock = _Clock()
    robot = _car(monkeypatch, None, clock)
    assert RobotController(robot).check_obstacle() is False
    assert RobotController(robot, require_obstacle_sensor=True).check_obstacle() is True


def test_sensor_concurrent_reads_are_serialized(monkeypatch):
    clock = _Clock()
    sensor = _hc_sr04(monkeypatch, clock)
    started = threading.Event()
    release = threading.Event()
    calls = []

    sensor._trigger_pulse = lambda: None

    def wait_for_level(expected, timeout_s=0.05):
        if expected == 1:
            calls.append(sensor._read_lock.locked())
            started.set()
            assert release.wait(2)
            return True
        clock.now += 42.0 * 2 / 34300
        return True

    sensor._wait_for_level = wait_for_level
    results = []
    threads = [threading.Thread(target=lambda: results.append(sensor.read_distance_cm())) for _ in range(4)]
    for t in threads:
        t.start()
    assert started.wait(1)
    release.set()
    for t in threads:
        t.join()
    assert calls == [True]
    assert results == [42.0] * 4


def test_sensor_does_not_cache_missing_measurements(monkeypatch):
    clock = _Clock()
    sensor = _hc_sr04(monkeypatch, clock)
    _mock_sensor_reads(sensor, clock, iter([None, 30.0]))

    assert sensor.read_distance_cm() is None
    clock.now += 0.001
    assert sensor.read_distance_cm() == 30.0

### FILE: tests/unit/test_standard_scenarios_script.py
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest


@pytest.mark.parametrize("runner_fails", [False, True])
def test_standard_scenarios_yellow_ball_report(tmp_path, runner_fails):
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    script = Path("scripts/scenarios/run_runtime_standard_scenarios_mac.sh")
    (tmp_path / script.parent).mkdir(parents=True)
    shutil.copy2(root / script, tmp_path / script)
    for folder in ("src", "tests"):
        (tmp_path / folder).symlink_to(root / folder, target_is_directory=True)

    bin_dir = tmp_path / "bin"
    bin_dir.mkdir()
    # Keep the four LLM cases offline; the fifth runs the real runtime.
    bash = bin_dir / "bash"
    bash.write_text(
        "#!/bin/bash\n"
        "printf '%s\\n' \"{'status': 'accepted'}\" 'TRACE_PATH=mock-llm.jsonl'\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    uv = bin_dir / "uv"
    uv.write_text(
        f"#!{sys.executable}\n"
        "import os, sys\n"
        + ("sys.exit(7)\n" if runner_fails else "os.execv(sys.executable, [sys.executable, *sys.argv[3:]])\n"),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    bash.chmod(0o755)
    uv.chmod(0o755)
    completed = subprocess.run(
        ["/bin/bash", str(tmp_path / script)],
        cwd=tmp_path,
        env={**os.environ, "PATH": f"{bin_dir}{os.pathsep}{os.environ['PATH']}"},
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=30,
    )

    reports = list((tmp_path / "target/logs/scenario_runs").glob("scenario_report_*.md"))
    assert len(reports) == 1
    report = reports[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if runner_fails:
        assert completed.returncode == 7
        assert "Yellow ball" not in report
        assert "Report generated:" not in completed.stdout
        return

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    rows = [line for line in report.splitlines() if line.startswith("| ")]
    assert len(rows) == 6
    assert "| Yellow ball (mock, 300 s simulated) |" in rows[-1]
    assert "| accepted |" in rows[-1]
    trace = Path(rows[-1].split("|")[-2].strip())
    events = [json.loads(line) for line in trace.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    steps = [event["intent"] for event in events if event["event"] == "step_result"]
    assert {step["name"] for step in steps} == {"search_and_approach_object", "follow_object"}
    follow = next(step for step in steps if step["name"] == "follow_object")
    assert follow["target"] == "yellow"
    assert follow["parameters"]["stand_off_cm"] == 100.0
    assert follow["parameters"]["duration_s"] == 300.0
    assert f"TRACE_PATH={trace}" in completed.stdout
