### FILE: tests/unit/test_vision_diagnostic.py
import math
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import MarkerIdentity, RuntimeSettings, load_runtime_settings
from irobot.common.hardware.camera import camera_live_perception_pipeline
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector.aruco_detector import estimate_marker_distance_cm
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.frame_enhancer import FrameEnhancer
from irobot.common.interfaces.perception_contracts import ObjectDetection
from irobot.tools.vision_diagnostic import detect_balls, format_object_status
from irobot.common.hardware.camera.detector import ArucoFrameDetection, ObservationDisposition
from irobot.tools.vision_diagnostic import (
    TEST_MARKER_IDENTITY,
    _draw_detection,
    format_detection_status,
    format_face_identity,
)
from tests.unit.perception_helpers import camera_target

SETTINGS = load_runtime_settings()
CAPTURE_W, CAPTURE_H = SETTINGS.camera_capture_size_px
ORIENTED_W, ORIENTED_H = SETTINGS.camera_oriented_frame_size_px


def _project_marker(
    translation,
    yaw=0,
    pitch=0,
    roll=0,
    size=SETTINGS.camera_oriented_frame_size_px,
    focal_px=SETTINGS.camera_focal_px,
):
    # Independently project the physical square through a pinhole camera.
    y, p, r = map(math.radians, (yaw, pitch, roll))
    rotate_y = np.array(((math.cos(y), 0, math.sin(y)), (0, 1, 0),
                         (-math.sin(y), 0, math.cos(y))))
    rotate_x = np.array(((1, 0, 0), (0, math.cos(p), -math.sin(p)),
                         (0, math.sin(p), math.cos(p))))
    rotate_z = np.array(((math.cos(r), -math.sin(r), 0),
                         (math.sin(r), math.cos(r), 0), (0, 0, 1)))
    square = np.array(((-4, -4, 0), (4, -4, 0), (4, 4, 0), (-4, 4, 0)))
    points = square @ (rotate_z @ rotate_y @ rotate_x).T + translation
    width, height = size
    image_points = points[:, :2] / points[:, 2, None]
    image_points *= (
        focal_px * width / ORIENTED_W,
        focal_px * height / ORIENTED_H,
    )
    image_points += (width / 2, height / 2)
    corners = tuple((float(x), float(y)) for x, y in image_points)
    return ArucoFrameDetection(
        ObservationDisposition.VALID, TEST_MARKER_IDENTITY,
        corners,
        distance_cm=estimate_marker_distance_cm(corners, focal_px, size),
    )


@pytest.fixture
def runtime_pipeline(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(camera_live_perception_pipeline, "load_runtime_settings", lambda: SETTINGS)
    return camera_live_perception_pipeline.CameraLivePerceptionPipeline(
        autostart=False,
    )


def _patch_marker_corners(monkeypatch, detector, corners):
    marker_id = detector.expected_id
    backend = SimpleNamespace(
        detectMarkers=lambda image: ([list(corners)], [[marker_id]], None),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(detector, "_detector", backend)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("x", [-20, 0, 20])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("yaw", [-45, 0, 45])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("pitch,roll", [(0, 0), (25, 30)])
def test_distance_accounts_for_position_and_orientation(x, yaw, pitch, roll):
    translation = (x, 0, 80)
    detection = _project_marker(translation, yaw, pitch, roll)
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(
        math.dist((0, 0, 0), translation), abs=0.01,
    )


def test_centered_frontal_distance_preserves_previous_result():
    detection = _project_marker((0, 0, 100))
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(100, abs=0.01)
    assert "distance=100.0 cm" in format_detection_status(detection)


def test_tilted_off_axis_distance_is_not_based_on_width_only():
    detection = _project_marker((20, 0, 80), yaw=-30)
    corners = detection.corners_px
    width = (math.dist(corners[0], corners[1]) + math.dist(corners[3], corners[2])) / 2
    expected = math.hypot(20, 80)
    assert abs(SETTINGS.camera_focal_px * 8 / width - expected) > 5
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(expected, abs=0.01)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("x", [-20, 20])
def test_84_cm_off_axis_with_subpixel_corner_noise(x):
    detection = _project_marker((x, 0, math.sqrt(84**2 - x**2)), yaw=-30)
    noise = ((0.1, -0.2), (-0.1, 0.2), (0.2, 0.1), (-0.2, -0.1))
    noisy_corners = tuple(
        (x + dx, y + dy) for (x, y), (dx, dy) in zip(detection.corners_px, noise)
    )
    noisy_detection = ArucoFrameDetection(
        ObservationDisposition.VALID,
        TEST_MARKER_IDENTITY,
        noisy_corners,
        distance_cm=estimate_marker_distance_cm(
            noisy_corners, SETTINGS.camera_focal_px,
        ),
    )
    assert noisy_detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(84, abs=1)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("size", [SETTINGS.camera_oriented_frame_size_px, (960, 1280)])
def test_distance_uses_actual_frame_dimensions(size):
    detection = _project_marker((20, 10, 80), yaw=30, size=size)
    assert detection.distance_cm == pytest.approx(
        math.sqrt(20**2 + 10**2 + 80**2), abs=0.01,
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("x,yaw", [(-20, -30), (0, 0), (20, 45)])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("size", [SETTINGS.camera_oriented_frame_size_px, (960, 1280)])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", ["marker", "tag"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("identifier", ["1", "2"])
def test_runtime_uses_pose_distance_for_configured_marker(
    runtime_pipeline, monkeypatch, x, yaw, size, target, identifier,
):
    projected = _project_marker((x, 10, 80), yaw=yaw, pitch=20, size=size)
    detection = ArucoFrameDetection(
        ObservationDisposition.VALID,
        MarkerIdentity("aruco", "DICT_4X4_50", identifier), projected.corners_px,
        distance_cm=projected.distance_cm,
    )
    strategy = camera_target(runtime_pipeline, "marker")
    marker_detector = runtime_pipeline._detector._marker_detector
    monkeypatch.setattr(marker_detector, "identity", detection.identity)
    monkeypatch.setattr(marker_detector, "expected_id", int(identifier))
    _patch_marker_corners(monkeypatch, marker_detector, projected.corners_px)
    frame = np.zeros((size[1], size[0], 3), dtype=np.uint8)
    strategy = camera_target(runtime_pipeline, target)
    runtime_pipeline.detect(target=strategy)
    result = runtime_pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=strategy)
    expected = math.sqrt(x**2 + 10**2 + 80**2)
    assert result is not None
    assert result.distance_cm == pytest.approx(expected, abs=0.01)
    assert result.distance_visual_cm == result.distance_cm
    if identifier == "1":
        assert result.distance_cm == projected.distance_cm
    runtime_pipeline._last_detection = result
    assert runtime_pipeline.detect(target=strategy).distance_cm == result.distance_cm


@pytest.mark.parametrize("disposition", [
    ObservationDisposition.ABSENT, ObservationDisposition.AMBIGUOUS,
    ObservationDisposition.REJECTED, ObservationDisposition.FAULT,
])
def test_non_detected_status_has_no_distance(disposition):
    detection = ArucoFrameDetection(disposition)
    assert detection.distance_cm is None
    assert "distance=" not in format_detection_status(detection)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("corners", [
    ((1, 1),) * 4,
    ((0, 0), (1, 1), (2, 2), (3, 3)),
    ((0, 0), (10, 10), (10, 0), (0, 10)),
    ((0, 0), (float("nan"), 0), (10, 10), (0, 10)),
    ((0, 0), (10, 0), (10, 10)),
])
def test_invalid_geometry_reports_unavailable(corners, runtime_pipeline, monkeypatch, caplog):
    strategy = camera_target(runtime_pipeline, "marker")
    marker_detector = runtime_pipeline._detector._marker_detector
    _patch_marker_corners(monkeypatch, marker_detector, corners)
    frame = np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8)
    detection = marker_detector.detect(frame)
    assert detection.distance_cm is None
    if detection.disposition is ObservationDisposition.VALID:
        assert "distance=indisponible" in format_detection_status(detection)
    else:
        assert detection.disposition is ObservationDisposition.REJECTED
        assert "rejected" in format_detection_status(detection)
    assert runtime_pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=strategy) is None
    assert "ArUco" in caplog.text


@pytest.mark.parametrize("success,translation", [
    (False, np.zeros((3, 1))),
    (True, np.array([[0], [0], [-80]])),
    (True, np.array([[0], [0], [float("nan")]])),
])
def test_failed_pose_does_not_produce_a_distance(
    monkeypatch, success, translation, runtime_pipeline, caplog,
):
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "solvePnP", lambda *args, **kwargs: (success, None, translation))
    projected = _project_marker((0, 0, 80))
    strategy = camera_target(runtime_pipeline, "marker")
    marker_detector = runtime_pipeline._detector._marker_detector
    _patch_marker_corners(monkeypatch, marker_detector, projected.corners_px)
    frame = np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8)
    detection = marker_detector.detect(frame)
    assert "distance=indisponible" in format_detection_status(detection)
    assert runtime_pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=strategy) is None
    assert "Pose ArUco inexploitable" in caplog.text


def test_opencv_pose_error_is_logged_without_width_fallback(
    monkeypatch, runtime_pipeline, caplog,
):
    def fail(*args, **kwargs):
        raise cv2.error("synthetic pose failure")

    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "solvePnP", fail)
    projected = _project_marker((0, 0, 80))
    strategy = camera_target(runtime_pipeline, "marker")
    marker_detector = runtime_pipeline._detector._marker_detector
    _patch_marker_corners(monkeypatch, marker_detector, projected.corners_px)
    frame = np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8)
    detection = marker_detector.detect(frame)
    assert "distance=indisponible" in format_detection_status(detection)
    assert runtime_pipeline._process_frame(frame, target=strategy) is None
    assert "synthetic pose failure" in caplog.text


def test_overlay_shows_current_pose_distance_and_failures():
    texts = []
    drawing_api = SimpleNamespace(
        line=lambda *args: None, circle=lambda *args: None,
        putText=lambda frame, text, *args: texts.append(text),
        FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX=0,
    )
    _draw_detection(object(), _project_marker((0, 0, 80)), drawing_api)
    _draw_detection(object(), _project_marker((20, 0, 80), yaw=30), drawing_api)
    invalid = ArucoFrameDetection(
        ObservationDisposition.VALID, TEST_MARKER_IDENTITY, ((1, 1),) * 4,
    )
    _draw_detection(object(), invalid, drawing_api)
    assert texts == [
        "ID 1 DETECTED ~80.0 cm", "ID 1 DETECTED ~82.5 cm",
        "ID 1 DETECTED - DISTANCE UNAVAILABLE",
    ]


def test_face_identity_status_distinguishes_unavailable_absent_unknown_and_named():
    face = ObjectDetection(label="face")
    assert format_face_identity(None, available=False) == "IDENTITY: unavailable"
    assert format_face_identity(None, available=True) == "IDENTITY: no face"
    assert format_face_identity(face, available=True) == "IDENTITY: unknown"
    face.identity = "Élodie"
    assert format_face_identity(face, available=True) == "IDENTITY: Élodie"


def test_face_identity_overlay_fits_frame_and_removes_accents(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.tools.vision_diagnostic import _draw_face_identity

    texts = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "putText", lambda frame, text, *args: texts.append(text))
    _draw_face_identity(np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8), "IDENTITY: Élodie" + "a" * 100, cv2)
    assert texts[0].startswith("IDENTITY: Elodie")
    assert cv2.getTextSize(texts[0], cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.55, 2)[0][0] <= ORIENTED_W - 12


@pytest.mark.parametrize("inference_error,identity,gallery_state", [
    (False, "Quentin", "loaded"), (False, None, "loaded"),
    (False, None, "empty"), (False, None, "error"), (True, None, "loaded"),
])
def test_main_configures_reference_resolution_and_rotates_without_camera(
    monkeypatch, capsys, inference_error, identity, gallery_state,
):
    from irobot.tools import vision_diagnostic

    calls = []
    texts = []
    boxes = []
    windows = []
    frame = np.zeros((CAPTURE_H, CAPTURE_W, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
    cv2.circle(frame, (CAPTURE_W // 4, CAPTURE_H // 2), 25, (255, 0, 0), -1)
    cv2.circle(frame, (3 * CAPTURE_W // 4, CAPTURE_H // 2), 25, (255, 255, 0), -1)

    class Camera:
        camera_controls = {"ExposureValue": (-8.0, 8.0, 0.0)}

        @staticmethod
        def global_camera_info():
            return [{"Model": "ov5647"}]

        def __init__(self, index):
            assert index == 0

        def create_preview_configuration(self, *, main):
            assert main == {"size": (CAPTURE_W, CAPTURE_H), "format": "BGR888"}
            return main

        def configure(self, config):
            calls.append("configure")

        def start(self):
            calls.append("start")

        def set_controls(self, controls):
            assert controls == {"ExposureValue": 1.0}
            calls.append("exposure")

        def capture_array(self):
            return frame

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def close(self):
            calls.append("camera_close")

    def detect(frame_rgb):
        assert frame_rgb.shape == (ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3)
        expected_rgb = FrameEnhancer(SETTINGS).apply(
            cv2.rotate(frame, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE),
        )
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(frame_rgb, expected_rgb)
        return _project_marker((0, 0, 80))

    monkeypatch.setenv("DISPLAY", ":test")
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "picamera2", SimpleNamespace(Picamera2=Camera))

    def create_marker_detector(identity, *, focal_px, oriented_frame_size_px):
        assert focal_px == SETTINGS.camera_focal_px
        assert oriented_frame_size_px == SETTINGS.camera_oriented_frame_size_px
        return SimpleNamespace(detect=detect)

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        vision_diagnostic, "OpenCVMarkerDetector",
        create_marker_detector,
    )
    face = ObjectDetection(label="face", position="left", distance_cm=90, confidence=0.9, identity=identity)

    def detect_face(frame_rgb):
        expected_rgb = FrameEnhancer(RuntimeSettings(camera_clahe_enabled=True)).apply(
            cv2.rotate(frame, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE),
        )
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(frame_rgb, expected_rgb)
        if inference_error:
            raise cv2.error("synthetic diagnostic inference failure")
        return face

    gallery = SimpleNamespace(names=lambda: ["Quentin"] if gallery_state == "loaded" else [])

    def load_gallery(settings):
        if gallery_state == "error":
            raise FileNotFoundError("modèle SFace absent")
        return gallery

    def create_face_detector(settings, *, gallery):
        assert gallery is (None if gallery_state != "loaded" else expected_gallery)
        return SimpleNamespace(
            detect=detect_face, is_cached=True, bounding_box=(20, 150, 100, 110),
        )

    expected_gallery = gallery
    monkeypatch.setattr(vision_diagnostic.FaceGallery, "load", load_gallery)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        vision_diagnostic, "FaceDetector",
        create_face_detector,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "imshow", lambda name, image: windows.append((name, image.copy())))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "putText", lambda image, text, *args: texts.append(text))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "rectangle", lambda image, start, end, color, thickness: boxes.append(
        (start, end, color),
    ))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "waitKey", lambda *args: ord("q"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "destroyAllWindows", lambda: calls.append("close"))
    if inference_error:
        with pytest.raises(cv2.error, match="diagnostic inference failure"):
            vision_diagnostic.main([])
        assert calls == ["configure", "exposure", "start", "stop", "camera_close", "close"]
        assert windows == []
        return
    assert vision_diagnostic.main([]) == 0
    assert calls == ["configure", "exposure", "start", "stop", "camera_close", "close"]
    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    output = captured.out
    expected_identity = identity or ("unknown" if gallery_state == "loaded" else "unavailable")
    assert "objectif non calibré" in output
    assert "distance=80.0 cm" in output
    assert "FACE: memorized; left; distance=90.0 cm" in output
    assert f"IDENTITY: {expected_identity}" in output
    if gallery_state != "loaded":
        assert "Reconnaissance des visages indisponible" in captured.err
    assert "BALL RED: detected" in output
    assert "BALL YELLOW: detected" in output
    assert len(texts) == 5
    assert texts[-1] == f"IDENTITY: {expected_identity}"
    assert len(boxes) == 3
    assert boxes[0][:2] == ((20, 150), (120, 260))
    assert windows[0][0] == "Vision - ArUco / Face / Red / Yellow"
    assert windows[0][1].shape == (ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3)
    assert "MEM" in texts[1]
    for text in texts[1:]:
        width = cv2.getTextSize(text, cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.55, 2)[0][0]
        assert width <= ORIENTED_W - 12


def test_main_preview_only_skips_detection_and_shows_unannotated_frame(monkeypatch, capsys):
    from irobot.tools import vision_diagnostic

    calls = []
    windows = []
    frame = np.full((CAPTURE_H, CAPTURE_W, 3), (10, 20, 30), dtype=np.uint8)

    class Camera:
        camera_controls = {"ExposureValue": (-8.0, 8.0, 0.0)}

        @staticmethod
        def global_camera_info():
            return [{"Model": "ov5647"}]

        def __init__(self, index):
            assert index == 0

        def create_preview_configuration(self, *, main):
            return main

        def configure(self, config):
            calls.append("configure")

        def start(self):
            calls.append("start")

        def set_controls(self, controls):
            calls.append("exposure")

        def capture_array(self):
            return frame

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def close(self):
            calls.append("camera_close")

    monkeypatch.setenv("DISPLAY", ":test")
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "picamera2", SimpleNamespace(Picamera2=Camera))
    monkeypatch.setattr(vision_diagnostic, "OpenCVMarkerDetector", lambda *_: pytest.fail("ArUco detector started"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(vision_diagnostic, "FaceDetector", lambda *_: pytest.fail("face detector started"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(vision_diagnostic.FaceGallery, "load", lambda *_: pytest.fail("gallery started"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(vision_diagnostic, "detect_balls", lambda *_: pytest.fail("ball detector started"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "imshow", lambda name, image: windows.append((name, image.copy())))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "putText", lambda *args: pytest.fail("frame annotated"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "rectangle", lambda *args: pytest.fail("frame annotated"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "waitKey", lambda *args: ord("q"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(cv2, "destroyAllWindows", lambda: calls.append("close"))

    assert vision_diagnostic.main(["--preview-only"]) == 0

    assert calls == ["configure", "exposure", "start", "stop", "camera_close", "close"]
    assert windows[0][0] == "Vision - Preview"
    expected_rgb = FrameEnhancer(SETTINGS).apply(cv2.rotate(frame, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE))
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(windows[0][1], cv2.cvtColor(expected_rgb, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
    assert "Aperçu caméra uniquement" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_both_balls_are_detected_independently_with_existing_distance_model():
    frame = np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8)
    cv2.circle(frame, (ORIENTED_W // 5, ORIENTED_H // 2), 25, (255, 0, 0), -1)
    cv2.circle(frame, (ORIENTED_W * 4 // 5, ORIENTED_H // 2), 35, (255, 255, 0), -1)
    results = detect_balls(frame, RuntimeSettings(camera_ball_diameter_cm=6, camera_focal_px=680))
    red, red_box = results["red"]
    yellow, yellow_box = results["yellow"]
    assert red.label == "red_ball" and red.position == "left"
    assert yellow.label == "yellow_ball" and yellow.position == "right"
    assert red.distance_cm == pytest.approx(680 * 6 / red.diameter_px)
    assert yellow.distance_cm == pytest.approx(680 * 6 / yellow.diameter_px)
    assert red_box[2] == 49
    assert yellow_box[2] == 69
    assert red.distance_cm > yellow.distance_cm


def test_ball_without_bounding_box_is_reported_as_detector_contract_error(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.tools import vision_diagnostic

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        vision_diagnostic.ColorDetector,
        "detect",
        lambda detector, frame: ObjectDetection(label=f"{detector.color_name}_ball"),
    )
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="missing its bounding box"):
        detect_balls(np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8), RuntimeSettings())


def test_absent_balls_and_nonball_objects_are_not_reported_as_balls(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.tools import vision_diagnostic

    frame = np.zeros((ORIENTED_H, ORIENTED_W, 3), np.uint8)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        vision_diagnostic.ColorDetector,
        "detect",
        lambda detector, frame: ObjectDetection(
            label=f"{detector.color_name}_object", bbox_px=(100, 200, 50, 50),
        ),
    )
    assert detect_balls(frame, RuntimeSettings()) == {"red": None, "yellow": None}


def test_object_status_distinguishes_absence_detection_and_cached_face():
    assert format_object_status("FACE", None, cached=True) == "FACE: absent"
    detection = ObjectDetection(label="face", position="center", distance_cm=100, confidence=0.8)
    assert format_object_status("FACE", detection) == (
        "FACE: detected; center; distance=100.0 cm; confidence=0.80"
    )
    assert "memorized" in format_object_status("FACE", detection, cached=True)
    detection.distance_cm = None
    assert "distance=indisponible" in format_object_status("FACE", detection)

@pytest.mark.parametrize("use_llm", [False, True])
def test_approach_me_uses_llm_marker_target(tmp_path, fake_llm, use_llm):
    class MarkerPerception:
        def __init__(self):
            self.targets = []

        def detect(self, *, target):
            from irobot.common.interfaces.perception_contracts import ObjectDetection

            self.targets.append(target)
            return ObjectDetection(label="marker_1", position="center", distance_cm=25.0, confidence=1.0)

    perception = MarkerPerception()
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(
        robot=robot,
        perception=perception,
        use_llm=use_llm,
        trace_dir=str(tmp_path),
    )
    step_json = {"intent": "search_and_approach_object", "target": "face", "parameters": {}}
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [step_json]})
    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("approche toi de moi")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    step = result["steps"][0]
    assert step["intent"].target == "face"
    assert "approach_target" in step["plan"]
    approach_result = step["results"][step["plan"].index("approach_target")]
    assert approach_result.data["close_enough"] is True
    assert perception.targets[0] is PerceptionTarget.FACE
    assert set(perception.targets) == {PerceptionTarget.FACE}
    assert set(robot.commands) == {"stop"}
    assert len(llm.calls) == int(use_llm)
    if use_llm:
        assert "« moi »" in llm.calls[0]["json"]["messages"][0]["content"]
    assert result["source"] == ("llm_plan" if use_llm else "local_parser")


def test_llm_cannot_replace_me_with_yellow(fake_llm, tmp_path):
    wrong = {"intent": "search_and_approach_object", "target": "yellow", "parameters": {}}
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [wrong]})
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("approche toi de moi")

    assert len(llm.calls) == 1
    assert result["status"] == "rejected"
    assert robot.commands == []


def test_llm_compound_marker_approach_preserves_distance_and_next_action(fake_llm):
    expected = [
        {"intent": "search_and_approach_object", "target": "face", "parameters": {"stand_off_cm": 100}},
        {"intent": "turn_left", "target": None, "parameters": {"angle": 90}},
    ]
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": expected})
    intents, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace(
        "rapproche toi à un mètre de moi puis tourne à gauche",
    )

    assert trace["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert len(llm.calls) == 1
    assert [(intent.name, intent.target) for intent in intents] == [
        ("search_and_approach_object", "face"), ("turn_left", None),
    ]
    assert intents[0].parameters == {"stand_off_cm": 100}

def test_voice_runtime_plan_normalizes_search_object_synonym(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.app.interfaces import command_service as runtime_module

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        runtime_module,
        "ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace",
        lambda _text: (
            [
                Intent(
                    name="search_object",
                    parameters={"search": True, "approach": True, "target_object": "bouteille"},
                    confidence=1.0,
                )
            ],
            {"source": "llm_plan"},
        ),
    )

    def _mock_detect(_self, context):
        from irobot.common.domain.skills.base_skill import SkillResult

        context.state["last_object_position"] = "centre"
        return SkillResult(
            status="success",
            message="mock detect",
            data={"position": "centre"},
            confidence=1.0,
            next_action="align_to_target",
        )

    from irobot.common.domain.skills.perception.detect_object import DetectObjectSkill

    monkeypatch.setattr(DetectObjectSkill, "execute", _mock_detect)

    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True)
    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("cherche un objet puis approche toi")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    assert result["steps"][0]["intent"].name == "search_and_approach_object"
    assert "approach_target" in result["steps"][0]["plan"]

def test_voice_runtime_mission_timeout(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.app.interfaces import command_service as runtime_module

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        runtime_module,
        "ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace",
        lambda _text: (
            [Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "cm"}, confidence=1.0)],
            {"source": "llm_plan"},
        ),
    )

    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True)
    runtime.command_service.settings = runtime.command_service.settings.__class__(
        cm_per_second=runtime.command_service.settings.cm_per_second,
        deg_per_second=runtime.command_service.settings.deg_per_second,
        step_timeout_seconds=0.0,
        mission_timeout_seconds=0.0,
        max_step_retries=runtime.command_service.settings.max_step_retries,
        max_plan_steps=runtime.command_service.settings.max_plan_steps,
        max_distance_cm=runtime.command_service.settings.max_distance_cm,
        max_angle_deg=runtime.command_service.settings.max_angle_deg,
        max_duration_sec=runtime.command_service.settings.max_duration_sec,
    )
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance")
    assert result["status"] == "aborted"


def _patch_plan(monkeypatch, intents):
    from irobot.app.interfaces import command_service as runtime_module

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        runtime_module,
        "ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace",
        lambda _text: (intents, {"source": "llm_plan"}),
    )


def test_sanitize_injects_default_duration_and_angle():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)

    forward = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(Intent(name="move_forward"))
    backward = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(Intent(name="move_backward"))
    turn = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(Intent(name="turn_left"))
    turn_timed = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(Intent(name="turn_right", parameters={"duration": 2}))

    assert forward.parameters["duration"] == 1.0
    assert backward.parameters["duration"] == 1.0
    assert turn.parameters["angle"] == 90.0
    assert "angle" not in turn_timed.parameters
    assert turn_timed.parameters["duration"] == 2.0


def test_sanitize_default_duration_bounded_by_max_duration():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    runtime.command_service.settings = runtime.command_service.settings.__class__(
        **{**runtime.command_service.settings.__dict__, "max_duration_sec": 0.5}
    )
    runtime.command_service.plan_validator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    forward = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(Intent(name="move_forward"))

    assert forward.parameters["duration"] == 0.5


def test_sanitize_normalizes_units():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)

    meters = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(
        Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 1, "unit": "mètres"})
    )
    unknown = runtime.command_service.plan_validator._sanitize_primitive_intent(
        Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "furlongs"})
    )

    assert meters.parameters["unit"] == "m"
    assert meters.parameters["distance"] == 1
    assert unknown.parameters["unit"] == "cm"


def test_primitive_resolves_unit_synonyms():
    from irobot.common.domain.skills.base_skill import SkillContext
    from irobot.common.domain.skills.primitives.base_motion_primitive import normalize_distance_unit
    from irobot.common.domain.skills.primitives.move_forward import MoveForwardSkill

    assert normalize_distance_unit("Metres") == "m"
    assert normalize_distance_unit("centimètres") == "cm"
    assert normalize_distance_unit("parsec") == "cm"

    context = SkillContext(
        robot=FakeRobot(), state={"active_intent_parameters": {"distance": 1, "unit": "meters"}}
    )
    payload = MoveForwardSkill()._resolve_motion_parameters(context)
    assert payload["unit"] == "m"


def test_voice_runtime_stops_robot_at_end_of_mission(monkeypatch):
    _patch_plan(monkeypatch, [Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "cm"}, confidence=1.0)])
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True)

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    assert robot.commands[-1] == "stop"


def test_voice_runtime_stops_robot_even_after_exception(monkeypatch):
    _patch_plan(monkeypatch, [Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "cm"}, confidence=1.0)])
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True)

    def _boom(_intent):
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service.controller, "handle_planned_step", _boom)

    try:
        runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance")
        raised = False
    except RuntimeError:
        raised = True

    assert raised
    assert robot.commands == ["stop"]


def test_voice_runtime_ignores_exception_raised_by_final_stop(monkeypatch):
    _patch_plan(monkeypatch, [Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "cm"}, confidence=1.0)])
    robot = FakeRobot()

    def _bad_stop():
        raise OSError("gpio")

    robot.stop = _bad_stop
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True)

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"


def test_voice_runtime_stops_executing_steps_after_failure(monkeypatch):
    _patch_plan(
        monkeypatch,
        [
            Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "cm"}, confidence=1.0),
            Intent(name="turn_left", parameters={"angle": 90}, confidence=1.0),
        ],
    )
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True)
    seen: list[str] = []

    def _fail(step):
        seen.append(step.intent.name)
        return {"intent": step.intent, "status": "failed", "reason": "nope"}

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service.controller, "handle_planned_step", _fail)

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance puis tourne")

    assert result["status"] == "incomplete"
    assert len(result["steps"]) == 1
    assert set(seen) == {"move_forward"}


def test_voice_runtime_rejects_whole_plan_when_one_step_invalid(monkeypatch):
    _patch_plan(
        monkeypatch,
        [
            Intent(name="move_forward", parameters={"distance": 10, "unit": "cm"}, confidence=1.0),
            Intent(name="draw_heart", confidence=1.0),
        ],
    )
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True)

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance puis dessine")

    assert result["status"] == "rejected"
    assert result["reason"] == "invalid plan step"
    assert len(result["validation_errors"]) == 1
    assert "forward" not in robot.commands


def test_effective_step_timeout_with_and_without_duration():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    base = runtime.command_service.settings.step_timeout_seconds
    extra = runtime.command_service.settings.search_timeout_seconds + runtime.command_service.settings.timeout_margin_seconds

    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_step_timeout(Intent(name="stop")) == base
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_step_timeout(Intent(name="follow_target", parameters={"duration_s": 300})) == 300 + extra
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_step_timeout(Intent(name="follow_target", parameters={"duration_s": 0})) == max(base, extra)


def test_effective_mission_timeout_is_sum_of_steps():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    extra = runtime.command_service.settings.search_timeout_seconds + runtime.command_service.settings.timeout_margin_seconds
    intents = [
        Intent(name="follow_target", parameters={"duration_s": 300}),
        Intent(name="stop"),
    ]

    expected = max(
        runtime.command_service.settings.mission_timeout_seconds,
        300 + extra + runtime.command_service.settings.step_timeout_seconds,
    )
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_mission_timeout(intents) == expected
    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_mission_timeout([Intent(name="stop")]) == runtime.command_service.settings.mission_timeout_seconds


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["search_and_approach_object"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("params", "expected"),
    [
        ({}, 43.0),
        ({"tracking_timeout_s": 12, "search_timeout_s": 40}, 62.0),
        ({"tracking_timeout_s": "invalid", "search_timeout_s": None}, 43.0),
        ({"tracking_timeout_s": 0, "search_timeout_s": 0}, 10.2),
    ],
)
def test_approach_timeout_covers_search_tracking_and_margin(name, params, expected):
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    runtime.command_service.settings = RuntimeSettings()
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_step_timeout(Intent(name=name, parameters=params)) == pytest.approx(expected)


def test_approach_timeout_preserves_larger_base():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    runtime.command_service.settings = RuntimeSettings(step_timeout_seconds=60)
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    for name in ("search_and_approach_object", "stop", "move_forward", "turn_right"):
        assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_step_timeout(Intent(name=name)) == 60.0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["stop", "move_forward", "move_backward", "turn_left", "turn_right"])
def test_approach_timeout_does_not_change_simple_command_budgets(name):
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    runtime.command_service.settings = RuntimeSettings()
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_step_timeout(Intent(name=name)) == 6.0


def test_effective_mission_timeout_includes_approach_budget():
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    runtime.command_service.settings = RuntimeSettings()
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings
    intents = [
        Intent(name="search_and_approach_object"),
        Intent(name="follow_object", parameters={"duration_s": 300}),
    ]

    assert runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator._effective_mission_timeout(intents) == 43.0 + 335.0


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("elapsed", "skill_status", "expected_status", "attempts"),
    [
        (13.5, "success", "accepted", 1),
        (33.0, "in_progress", "incomplete", 3),
        (44.0, "success", "aborted", 1),
    ],
)
def test_approach_retry_uses_its_own_timeout(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path, elapsed, skill_status, expected_status, attempts
):
    from irobot.common.domain.skills.base_skill import SkillResult

    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))
    runtime.command_service.settings = RuntimeSettings()
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings
    clock = [0.0]
    calls = []
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.time_fn = lambda: clock[0]

    def execute(step):
        calls.append(step.intent.name)
        clock[0] += elapsed
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": [SkillResult(status=skill_status)]}

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service.controller, "handle_planned_step", execute)
    planned_step = runtime.command_service.planner.plan_intents(
        [Intent(name="search_and_approach_object")]
    )[0]
    steps, _ = runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.execute(
        [planned_step], execute, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )
    result = steps[0]

    assert result["status"] == expected_status
    assert len(calls) == attempts
    if expected_status == "aborted":
        assert result["reason"] == "step timeout"
    elif expected_status == "incomplete":
        assert result["reason"] == "max retries reached"


def test_no_retry_on_aborted_or_timeout(tmp_path):
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))
    calls = []

    def aborted(_intent):
        calls.append(1)
        return {"status": "aborted", "reason": "x", "results": []}

    runtime.command_service.controller.handle_planned_step = aborted
    planned_step = runtime.command_service.planner.plan_intents([Intent(name="stop")])[0]
    steps, _ = runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.execute(
        [planned_step], aborted, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )
    result = steps[0]

    assert result["status"] == "aborted"
    assert len(calls) == 1

    calls.clear()
    runtime.command_service.settings = type(runtime.command_service.settings)(step_timeout_seconds=-1.0)
    runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.settings = runtime.command_service.settings

    def failed(_intent):
        calls.append(1)
        return {"status": "accepted", "results": []}

    runtime.command_service.controller.handle_planned_step = failed
    steps, _ = runtime.command_service.mission_orchestrator.execute(
        [planned_step], failed, stop_requested=lambda: False,
    )
    result = steps[0]
    assert result["reason"] == "step timeout"
    assert len(calls) == 1


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- Étape 8 : LLM local (Qwen2.5-1.5B)

USE_CASE = "cherche la balle jaune approche toi à un mètre de la balle jaune puis suis la pendant cinq minutes"
USE_CASE_NORMALIZED = (
    "cherche la balle jaune approche toi à 100 centimètres de la balle jaune puis suis la pendant 300 secondes"
)
USE_CASE_PLAN = {
    "steps": [
        {"intent": "search_and_approach_object", "target": "yellow", "parameters": {"stand_off_cm": 100}},
        {"intent": "follow_object", "target": "yellow", "parameters": {"stand_off_cm": 100, "duration_s": 300}},
    ]
}
MOVES = {"forward", "backward", "left", "right"}


class _FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, content: str = "", status_code: int = 200):
        self.status_code = status_code
        self._content = content

    def raise_for_status(self) -> None:
        if self.status_code >= 400:
            import requests

            raise requests.HTTPError(f"HTTP {self.status_code}")

    def json(self):
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": self._content}}]}


class _FakeLLM:
    """Remplace requests.post : enregistre les appels et rejoue des réponses."""

    def __init__(self, *responses):
        self.responses = list(responses)
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    def __call__(self, url, json=None, timeout=None):
        self.calls.append({"url": url, "json": json, "timeout": timeout})
        response = self.responses.pop(0) if len(self.responses) > 1 else self.responses[0]
        if isinstance(response, Exception):
            raise response
        if isinstance(response, _FakeResponse):
            return response
        return _FakeResponse(response if isinstance(response, str) else json_dumps(response))

    def user_text(self, index: int = 0) -> str:
        return self.calls[index]["json"]["messages"][-1]["content"]


def json_dumps(value) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False)


@pytest.fixture
def fake_llm(monkeypatch):
    def _install(*responses):
        llm = _FakeLLM(*responses)
        monkeypatch.setattr(voice_llm_transport.requests, "post", llm)
        return llm

    voice_llm_transport.PLAN_RESPONSE_FORMATS.reset()

    for key in ("LLM_MODEL", "LLM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "LLM_READ_TIMEOUT_SECONDS",
                "LLM_MAX_TOKENS_PLAN", "LLM_RESPONSE_FORMAT",
                "LLM_WARMUP_WAIT_SECONDS"):
        monkeypatch.delenv(key, raising=False)
    return _install


def _load_eval_script():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "scenarios" / "eval_prompt_plan.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("eval_prompt_plan", path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("text", "expected"),
    [
        ("tourne de quatre vingt dix degrés", "tourne de 90 degrés"),
        ("avance de soixante et onze centimètres", "avance de 71 centimètres"),
        ("recule de deux cent cinquante centimètres", "recule de 250 centimètres"),
        ("avance d'un mètre", "avance d' 100 centimètres"),
        ("approche toi à un mètre", "approche toi à 100 centimètres"),
        ("suis la pendant cinq minutes", "suis la pendant 300 secondes"),
        ("fais un carré", "fais un carré"),
        ("fais un carré de un mètre", "fais un carré de 100 centimètres"),
        ("tourne à gauche de quarante cinq degrés", "tourne à gauche de 45 degrés"),
    ],
)
def test_preprocess_numbers_and_units(text, expected):
    assert preprocess(text) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("text", "expected"),
    [
        ("avance pendant 1 minute 30 secondes", "avance pendant 90 secondes"),
        ("suis la pendant deux minutes et quinze secondes", "suis la pendant 135 secondes"),
        ("suis la pendant une minute trente secondes", "suis la pendant 90 secondes"),
        ("avance de 1 mètre 50", "avance de 150 centimètres"),
        ("avance d'un mètre cinquante", "avance d' 150 centimètres"),
        ("recule de 1 mètre et 20 centimètres", "recule de 120 centimètres"),
        ("avance de deux mètres cinq centimètres", "avance de 205 centimètres"),
        ("avance de 1 mètre 50 puis tourne", "avance de 150 centimètres puis tourne"),
        # formes simples conservées
        ("suis la pendant cinq minutes", "suis la pendant 300 secondes"),
        ("recule de deux mètres", "recule de 200 centimètres"),
        # pas de fusion quand M porte une autre unité ou dépasse 99
        ("avance de 1 mètre et 2 secondes", "avance de 100 centimètres et 2 secondes"),
        ("avance de 1 mètre 150 centimètres", "avance de 100 centimètres 150 centimètres"),
        ("avance 2 mètres 3 fois", "avance 200 centimètres 3 fois"),
    ],
)
def test_preprocess_compound_durations_and_distances(text, expected):
    assert preprocess(text) == expected


def test_local_parser_keeps_own_minute_handling():
    assert TextIntentParser().parse("avance pendant 1 minute 30 secondes").parameters == {"duration": 90.0}


def test_normalizer_and_prompt_match_eval_script():
    script = _load_eval_script()
    corpus = [case[1] for case in script.CASES] + [
        "quatre vingt dix", "soixante et onze", "deux cent cinquante", "mille deux cent",
        "un carré", "une minute", "dix-sept secondes", "quatre-vingts", "cent un mètres",
    ]
    for text in corpus:
        assert fr_numbers_to_digits(text) == script.fr_numbers_to_digits(text), text
        assert preprocess(text) == script.preprocess(text), text
    assert voice_llm_transport.SYSTEM_PROMPT_PLAN == script.SYSTEM_PROMPT_PLAN


def test_local_parser_understands_spelled_distances_and_angles():
    parser = TextIntentParser()

    forward = parser.parse("avance de cinquante centimètres")
    turn = parser.parse("tourne à droite de quatre vingt dix degrés")
    backward = parser.parse("recule de deux mètres")
    timed = parser.parse("avance pendant trois secondes")

    assert (forward.name, forward.parameters) == ("move_forward", {"distance": 50.0, "unit": "cm"})
    assert forward.raw_text == "avance de cinquante centimètres"
    assert (turn.name, turn.parameters) == ("turn_right", {"angle": 90.0, "unit": "degrees"})
    assert backward.parameters == {"distance": 2.0, "unit": "m"}
    assert timed.parameters == {"duration": 3.0}
    assert parser.parse("avance").parameters == {"duration": 1.0}


def test_llm_use_case_plan_runs_end_to_end(fake_llm, tmp_path):
    llm = fake_llm(USE_CASE_PLAN)
    world = World(distance_cm=180.0)
    settings = RuntimeSettings()
    registry = SkillRegistry.default()
    registry.register(ApproachTargetSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep))
    registry.register(FollowObjectSkill(settings=settings, time_fn=world.time, sleep_fn=world.sleep))
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(
        robot=world,
        perception=world,
                registry=registry,
        use_llm=True,
        trace_dir=str(tmp_path),
    )

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan(USE_CASE)

    assert len(llm.calls) == 1
    payload = llm.calls[0]["json"]
    assert llm.user_text() == USE_CASE_NORMALIZED
    assert payload["messages"][0] == {
        "role": "system", "content": voice_llm_transport.SYSTEM_PROMPT_PLAN,
    }
    assert payload["model"] == "qwen2.5-1.5b"
    assert payload["temperature"] == 0.0
    assert payload["max_tokens"] == 120
    assert payload["stream"] is False
    assert payload["cache_prompt"] is True
    assert payload["response_format"]["type"] == "json_schema"
    assert payload["response_format"]["json_schema"]["strict"] is True
    assert payload["response_format"]["json_schema"]["schema"] == PLAN_SCHEMA
    assert llm.calls[0]["timeout"] == (1.0, 10.0)

    assert result["status"] == "accepted", result
    assert result["source"] == "llm_plan"
    steps = result["steps"]
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in steps] == ["search_and_approach_object", "follow_object"]
    assert all(s["intent"].raw_text == USE_CASE for s in steps)
    assert steps[1]["intent"].parameters["duration_s"] == 300
    assert steps[1]["intent"].parameters["stand_off_cm"] == 100
    assert set(world.targets) == {PerceptionTarget.YELLOW_BALL}
    assert world.now >= 300.0
    assert world.commands[-1] == "stop"

    events = [json.loads(line) for line in Path(result["trace_path"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    plan_raw = next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "plan_raw")
    assert plan_raw["normalized_text"] == USE_CASE_NORMALIZED
    assert plan_raw["intents"][0]["raw_text"] == USE_CASE
    assert next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "input")["text"] == USE_CASE


def test_llm_plan_accepts_backtick_wrapped_json(fake_llm):
    fake_llm("```json\n" + json_dumps({"steps": [{"intent": "stop", "target": None, "parameters": {}}]}) + "\n```")

    intents, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("arrête toi")

    assert [i.name for i in intents] == ["stop"]
    assert trace["source"] == "llm_plan"

    intents, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance de cinquante centimètres")

    assert trace["source"] == "local_parser_fast_fallback"
    assert trace["reason"] == "invalid json"
    assert [(i.name, i.parameters) for i in intents] == [("move_forward", {"distance": 50.0, "unit": "cm"})]
    assert intents[0].raw_text == "avance de cinquante centimètres"

def test_llm_plan_skips_only_malformed_steps(fake_llm):
    fake_llm(
        {
            "steps": [
                {"intent": "move_forward", "parameters": {"distance": 20}},
                "turn_left",
                {"intent": "turn_left", "parameters": ["angle", 90]},
                {"intent": "turn_right", "parameters": {"angle": 45}, "confidence": 0.95},
            ]
        }
    )

    intents, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance puis tourne à droite")

    assert [i.name for i in intents] == ["move_forward", "turn_right"]
    assert trace["skipped_steps"] == [1, 2]
    assert intents[0].confidence == 0.8
    assert intents[1].confidence == 0.95

def test_llm_http_400_walks_format_chain_then_memorizes(fake_llm):
    stop_plan = json_dumps({"steps": [{"intent": "stop", "target": None, "parameters": {}}]})
    llm = fake_llm(_FakeResponse(status_code=400), _FakeResponse(status_code=400), stop_plan)

    intents, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    formats = [call["json"].get("response_format") for call in llm.calls]
    assert formats == build_formats(False, True)[:3]
    assert formats[1] == {"type": "json_object", "schema": PLAN_SCHEMA}
    assert formats[2] == {"type": "json_object"}
    assert llm.calls[2]["json"]["cache_prompt"] is True
    assert trace["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert [i.name for i in intents] == ["stop"]

    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    assert len(llm.calls) == 4
    assert llm.calls[3]["json"]["response_format"] == {"type": "json_object"}


def test_llm_json_schema_accepted_first_is_reused(fake_llm):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "stop"}]})

    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")
    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    assert len(llm.calls) == 2
    assert all(call["json"]["response_format"]["type"] == "json_schema" for call in llm.calls)


def test_llm_http_400_on_every_format_falls_back(fake_llm):
    llm = fake_llm(_FakeResponse(status_code=400))

    _, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance")

    assert len(llm.calls) == 4
    assert "response_format" not in llm.calls[-1]["json"]
    assert trace["source"] == "local_parser_fast_fallback"


def test_llm_settings_from_environment(fake_llm, monkeypatch):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "stop"}]})
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_MODEL", "mon-modele")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "0.5")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_READ_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "2.5")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_MAX_TOKENS_PLAN", "64")

    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    assert llm.calls[0]["json"]["model"] == "mon-modele"
    assert llm.calls[0]["json"]["max_tokens"] == 64
    assert llm.calls[0]["timeout"] == (0.5, 2.5)


def test_load_llm_settings_defaults_and_invalid_values():
    assert load_llm_settings({}) == LLMSettings(
        model="qwen2.5-1.5b",
        connect_timeout_seconds=1.0,
        read_timeout_seconds=10.0,
        max_tokens_plan=120,
    )
    invalid = load_llm_settings(
        {"LLM_MODEL": " ", "LLM_READ_TIMEOUT_SECONDS": "abc", "LLM_MAX_TOKENS_PLAN": "-3",
         "LLM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS": "nan"}
    )
    assert invalid == LLMSettings()


def test_unsupported_command_is_rejected_without_parser_or_motion(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    fake_llm({"steps": []})

    def _no_parser(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("le parseur local ne doit pas être utilisé")

    monkeypatch.setattr(TextIntentParser, "parse_plan", _no_parser)
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("fais un salto arrière")

    assert result["status"] == "rejected"
    assert result["reason"] == "unsupported command"
    assert result["source"] == "llm_unsupported"
    assert result["steps"] == []
    assert not MOVES & set(robot.commands)
    assert robot.commands == []
    events = [json.loads(line) for line in Path(result["trace_path"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    unsupported = [e for e in events if e["event"] == "plan_unsupported"]
    assert len(unsupported) == 1
    assert unsupported[0]["text"] == "fais un salto arrière"
    assert "step_result" not in {e["event"] for e in events}


def test_warm_up_sends_plan_system_prompt_in_background(fake_llm):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": []})

    warmup = LLMWarmup().start()

    assert warmup.wait(timeout=2.0) == "done"
    assert warmup._thread.daemon
    payload = llm.calls[0]["json"]
    assert payload["messages"][0]["content"] == voice_llm_transport.SYSTEM_PROMPT_PLAN
    assert payload["cache_prompt"] is True
    assert payload["max_tokens"] == 1


def test_warm_up_swallows_errors(fake_llm):
    import requests

    fake_llm(requests.ConnectionError("refused"))

    assert warm_up_llm() is False


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- Étape 8b : gardes déterministes


def _events(result) -> list[dict]:
    return [json.loads(line) for line in Path(result["trace_path"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]


def _forbid_parser(monkeypatch):
    def _no_parser(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("le parseur local ne doit pas être utilisé")

    monkeypatch.setattr(TextIntentParser, "parse_plan", _no_parser)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text", ["stop", "arrête toi", "halte", "pause"])
def test_pure_stop_runs_locally_without_llm(fake_llm, tmp_path, text):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "move_forward", "parameters": {"duration": 1}}]})
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan(text)

    assert llm.calls == []
    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    assert result["source"] == "local_stop"
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in result["steps"]] == ["stop"]
    assert "stop" in robot.commands
    assert not MOVES & set(robot.commands)


def test_pure_stop_also_bypasses_parser_without_llm(tmp_path):
    result = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path)).handle_text_as_plan("halte")

    assert result["source"] == "local_stop"
    assert result["status"] == "accepted"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("text", "plan", "expected"),
    [
        (
            "avance puis arrête",
            [{"intent": "move_forward", "parameters": {"duration": 1}}, {"intent": "stop"}],
            ["move_forward", "stop"],
        ),
        (
            "arrête toi et recule",
            [{"intent": "stop"}, {"intent": "move_backward", "parameters": {}}],
            ["stop", "move_backward"],
        ),
    ],
)
def test_stop_inside_longer_command_goes_to_llm(fake_llm, tmp_path, text, plan, expected):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": plan})
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan(text)

    assert len(llm.calls) == 1
    assert result["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in result["steps"]] == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("text", "llm_step"),
    [
        ("chante une chanson", {"intent": "spin_360", "target": None, "parameters": {}}),
        ("ouvre la porte du garage", {"intent": "move_forward", "target": None, "parameters": {"distance": 100}}),
        ("fais un salto arrière", {"intent": "avoid_obstacle", "target": None, "parameters": {}}),
        ("avance de cinquante centimètres", {"intent": "move_forward", "parameters": {"distance": 500}}),
    ],
)
def test_ungrounded_llm_plan_is_rejected_without_motion_or_parser(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path, text, llm_step):
    fake_llm({"steps": [llm_step]})
    _forbid_parser(monkeypatch)
    robot = FakeRobot()
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=robot, use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan(text)

    assert result["status"] == "rejected"
    assert result["reason"] == "unsupported command"
    assert result["source"] == "llm_ungrounded"
    assert result["steps"] == []
    assert robot.commands == []
    events = _events(result)
    unsupported = next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "plan_unsupported")
    assert unsupported["guard_reason"]
    assert unsupported["normalized_text"] == preprocess(text)
    assert "step_result" not in {e["event"] for e in events}


def test_only_parasite_stop_is_rejected(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "stop"}]})
    _forbid_parser(monkeypatch)

    result = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path)).handle_text_as_plan(
        "chante une chanson"
    )

    assert result["status"] == "rejected"
    assert result["reason"] == "unsupported command"
    notes = next(e for e in _events(result) if e["event"] == "plan_unsupported")["guard_notes"]
    assert notes == ["stop parasite supprimé"]


def test_forward_with_invented_distance_gets_default_duration(fake_llm, tmp_path):
    fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "move_forward", "target": None, "parameters": {"distance": 1}}]})

    result = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path)).handle_text_as_plan("avance")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    params = result["steps"][0]["intent"].parameters
    assert params["duration"] == 1
    assert "distance" not in params


def test_parasite_stop_between_moves_is_removed(fake_llm, tmp_path):
    fake_llm(
        {
            "steps": [
                {"intent": "move_forward", "target": None, "parameters": {"distance": 20}},
                {"intent": "stop", "target": None, "parameters": {}},
                {"intent": "move_backward", "target": None, "parameters": {"distance": 10}},
            ]
        }
    )
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("avance de vingt centimètres puis recule de dix centimètres")

    assert result["status"] == "accepted"
    assert [s["intent"].name for s in result["steps"]] == ["move_forward", "move_backward"]
    assert [s["intent"].parameters["distance"] for s in result["steps"]] == [20.0, 10.0]
    plan_raw = next(e for e in _events(result) if e["event"] == "plan_raw")
    assert plan_raw["guard_notes"] == ["stop parasite supprimé"]

def test_read_timeout_default_and_env_override(fake_llm, monkeypatch):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "stop"}]})

    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_READ_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "3")
    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    assert llm.calls[0]["timeout"] == (1.0, 10.0)
    assert llm.calls[1]["timeout"] == (1.0, 3.0)
    assert llm.calls[0]["json"]["max_tokens"] == 120


def test_emergency_listener_checks_pure_stop_before_parser(monkeypatch):
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=False)
    calls = []
    original = runtime.command_service.parser.parse

    def _spy(text):
        calls.append(text)
        return original(text)

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.command_service.parser, "parse", _spy)

    assert runtime._is_stop_phrase("halte")
    assert calls == []
    assert runtime._is_stop_phrase("stop maintenant")
    assert calls == []
    assert not runtime._is_stop_phrase("avance")
