def _plan_raw(result) -> dict:
    return next(e for e in _events(result) if e["event"] == "plan_raw")


def test_read_timeout_default_is_ten_seconds():
    assert LLMSettings().read_timeout_seconds == 10.0
    assert load_llm_settings({}).read_timeout_seconds == 10.0


def test_timeout_trace_has_latency_attempts_and_error_type(fake_llm, tmp_path, capsys):
    import requests

    fake_llm(requests.ReadTimeout("read timeout"))

    result = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path)).handle_text_as_plan("recule")

    raw = _plan_raw(result)
    assert raw["source"] == "local_parser_fast_fallback"
    assert raw["error_type"] == "ReadTimeout"
    assert raw["llm_attempts"] == 1
    assert raw["llm_response_format"] == "json_schema"
    assert isinstance(raw["llm_latency_seconds"], float) and raw["llm_latency_seconds"] >= 0
    assert raw["warmup_status"] == "skipped"
    assert "LLM trop lent (> 10s), repli sur le parseur local" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_unreachable_server_console_message(fake_llm, capsys):
    import requests

    fake_llm(requests.ConnectionError("refused"))

    _, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance")

    assert trace["error_type"] == "ConnectionError"
    assert "serveur LLM injoignable, repli sur le parseur local" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_invalid_json_console_message(fake_llm, capsys):
    fake_llm("pas du json")

    _, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance")

    assert trace["source"] == "local_parser_fast_fallback"
    assert trace["llm_attempts"] == 1
    assert "JSON invalide, repli sur le parseur local" in capsys.readouterr().out


class _FakeResponseWithStats(_FakeResponse):
    def json(self):
        data = super().json()
        data["usage"] = {"prompt_tokens": 412, "completion_tokens": 37, "total_tokens": 449}
        data["timings"] = {"prompt_per_second": 150.5, "predicted_per_second": 9.8, "predicted_n": 37}
        return data


def test_trace_records_usage_timings_and_attempts_with_format_fallback(fake_llm, tmp_path):
    plan = json_dumps({"steps": [{"intent": "move_backward", "target": None, "parameters": {"duration": 1}}]})
    fake_llm(_FakeResponse(status_code=400), _FakeResponseWithStats(plan))

    result = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path)).handle_text_as_plan("recule")

    raw = _plan_raw(result)
    assert raw["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert raw["llm_attempts"] == 2
    assert raw["llm_response_format"] == "json_object_schema"
    assert raw["llm_prompt_tokens"] == 412
    assert raw["llm_completion_tokens"] == 37
    assert raw["llm_timings"] == {"prompt_per_second": 150.5, "predicted_per_second": 9.8, "predicted_n": 37}
    assert raw["error_type"] is None


def test_response_format_none_sends_no_response_format(fake_llm, monkeypatch):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "stop"}]})
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_RESPONSE_FORMAT", "none")

    _, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    assert all("response_format" not in call["json"] for call in llm.calls)
    assert trace["llm_response_format"] == "none"


def test_response_format_json_object_sends_simple_format_only(fake_llm, monkeypatch):
    llm = fake_llm(_FakeResponse(status_code=400))
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_RESPONSE_FORMAT", "json_object")

    _, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance")

    assert [call["json"]["response_format"] for call in llm.calls] == [{"type": "json_object"}]
    assert trace["llm_response_format"] == "json_object"
    assert trace["source"] == "local_parser_fast_fallback"


def test_response_format_unknown_value_keeps_schema_chain(fake_llm, monkeypatch):
    llm = fake_llm({"steps": [{"intent": "stop"}]})
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_RESPONSE_FORMAT", "grammar")

    ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("stop")

    assert llm.calls[0]["json"]["response_format"]["type"] == "json_schema"


class _SlowWarmupLLM:
    """requests.post : le préchauffage (max_tokens 1) dure `delay` s ou échoue ; le plan répond tout de suite."""

    def __init__(self, delay: float = 0.3, warmup_error: Exception | None = None):
        self.delay = delay
        self.warmup_error = warmup_error
        self.events: list[str] = []

    def __call__(self, url, json=None, timeout=None):
        import time

        if json["max_tokens"] == 1:
            self.events.append("warmup_start")
            time.sleep(self.delay)
            self.events.append("warmup_end")
            if self.warmup_error is not None:
                raise self.warmup_error
            return _FakeResponse(json_dumps({"steps": []}))
        self.events.append("plan")
        return _FakeResponse(json_dumps({"steps": [{"intent": "move_backward", "parameters": {"duration": 1}}]}))


def _run_main_text(monkeypatch, tmp_path, llm, text: str = "recule") -> dict:
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_llm_transport.requests, "post", llm)
    monkeypatch.setattr(app, "build_hardware_runtime",
                        lambda settings: {"robot": FakeRobot(), "camera": None, "sensor": None})
    monkeypatch.setattr(app, "load_face_gallery", lambda settings: None)
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.argv", ["robot", "--text", text])

    app.main()
    traces = sorted((tmp_path / "target" / "logs").glob("runtime_trace_*.jsonl"))
    assert len(traces) == 1
    events = [json.loads(line) for line in traces[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    return next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "plan_raw")


def test_text_mode_sends_real_request_after_warmup(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    llm = _SlowWarmupLLM(delay=0.3)

    raw = _run_main_text(monkeypatch, tmp_path, llm)

    assert llm.events == ["warmup_start", "warmup_end", "plan"]
    assert raw["warmup_status"] == "done"
    assert raw["source"] == "llm_plan"


def test_text_mode_failed_warmup_still_processes_command(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path, capsys):
    import requests

    llm = _SlowWarmupLLM(delay=0.0, warmup_error=requests.ConnectionError("refused"))

    raw = _run_main_text(monkeypatch, tmp_path, llm)

    assert llm.events == ["warmup_start", "warmup_end", "plan"]
    assert raw["warmup_status"] == "failed"
    assert raw["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert [i["name"] for i in raw["intents"]] == ["move_backward"]
    assert "Préchauffage en échec" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_main_cleans_hardware_if_runtime_construction_fails(monkeypatch):
    calls = []

    class Robot:
        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("robot cleanup")

    class Camera:
        def close(self):
            calls.append("camera close")

    def fail_runtime_construction(**kwargs):
        raise RuntimeError("runtime construction failed")

    monkeypatch.setattr(app, "build_hardware_runtime", lambda settings: {
        "robot": Robot(), "camera": Camera(), "sensor": None,
    })
    monkeypatch.setattr(app, "VoiceRuntime", fail_runtime_construction)
    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.argv", ["robot", "--no-llm"])

    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        app.main()

    assert calls == ["stop", "robot cleanup", "camera close"]


def test_main_closes_hardware_even_if_runtime_close_fails(monkeypatch):
    calls = []

    class Robot:
        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("robot cleanup")

    class Camera:
        def close(self):
            calls.append("camera close")

    class FailingRuntime:
        def __init__(self, **kwargs):
            self.command_service = type("CommandService", (), {})()

        def clear_emergency_stop(self):
            pass

        def handle_text_as_plan(self, text):
            return {"status": "ok"}

        def close(self):
            raise RuntimeError("abort impossible")

    monkeypatch.setattr(app, "build_hardware_runtime", lambda settings: {
        "robot": Robot(), "camera": Camera(), "sensor": None,
    })
    monkeypatch.setattr(app, "VoiceRuntime", FailingRuntime)
    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.argv", ["robot", "--no-llm", "--text", "avance"])

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="abort impossible"):
        app.main()

    assert calls == ["stop", "robot cleanup", "camera close"]


def test_text_mode_warmup_wait_timeout_still_processes_command(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_WARMUP_WAIT_SECONDS", "0.05")
    llm = _SlowWarmupLLM(delay=0.5)

    raw = _run_main_text(monkeypatch, tmp_path, llm)

    assert llm.events[:2] == ["warmup_start", "plan"]
    assert raw["warmup_status"] == "timeout"
    assert raw["source"] == "llm_plan"


def test_continuous_mode_does_not_wait_for_warmup(fake_llm, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    llm = _SlowWarmupLLM(delay=0.5)
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_llm_transport.requests, "post", llm)
    runtime = VoiceRuntime(robot=FakeRobot(), use_llm=True, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))
    runtime.command_service.warmup = LLMWarmup().start()

    result = runtime.handle_text_as_plan("recule")

    assert llm.events[:2] == ["warmup_start", "plan"]
    assert _plan_raw(result)["warmup_status"] == "running"


def test_llm_warmup_wait_seconds_env():
    wait_seconds = voice_llm_transport.llm_warmup_wait_seconds
    assert wait_seconds({}) == 60.0
    assert wait_seconds({"LLM_WARMUP_WAIT_SECONDS": "15"}) == 15.0
    assert wait_seconds({"LLM_WARMUP_WAIT_SECONDS": "abc"}) == 60.0
    assert wait_seconds({"LLM_WARMUP_WAIT_SECONDS": "-1"}) == 60.0

### FILE: tests/unit/test_voice_runtime_structure.py
"""Structural limits and offline adapter contracts for the voice runtime."""

import ast
from contextlib import contextmanager
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from irobot.common.config.settings import RuntimeSettings
from irobot.app.domain.intent import Intent
from irobot.common.hardware.audio import voice_input
from irobot.common.hardware import runtime_factory
from irobot.app.interfaces import voice_runtime
from irobot.app.interfaces.intent.llm_intent_parser import ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace


PACKAGE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "src" / "irobot"
VOICE_FILES = sorted((PACKAGE / "app" / "interfaces").glob("voice*.py")) + [
    PACKAGE / "app" / "interfaces" / "command_service.py",
    PACKAGE / "app" / "interfaces" / "intent" / "plan_validator.py",
    PACKAGE / "app" / "domain" / "orchestrator" / "mission_orchestrator.py",
    PACKAGE / "common" / "hardware" / "audio" / "voice_input.py",
    PACKAGE / "common" / "hardware" / "runtime_factory.py",
]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", VOICE_FILES, ids=lambda path: path.name)
def test_voice_modules_respect_line_limits(path):
    source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert len(source.splitlines()) <= 500, path.name
    for node in ast.walk(ast.parse(source)):
        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
            start = min([node.lineno] + [item.lineno for item in node.decorator_list])
            assert node.end_lineno - start + 1 <= 50, f"{path.name}:{node.name}"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "path", [path for path in VOICE_FILES if path.parent.name == "interfaces"],
    ids=lambda path: path.name,
)
def test_voice_logic_does_not_import_hardware_at_module_load(path):
    tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for node in tree.body:
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            assert all(not name.name.startswith("irobot.common.hardware") for name in node.names)
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            assert not (node.module or "").startswith("irobot.common.hardware")


def test_audio_stream_terminates_and_closes_on_consumer_error(monkeypatch):
    stream = BytesIO(b"audio")
    calls = []
    process = SimpleNamespace(
        stdout=stream, poll=lambda: None,
        terminate=lambda: calls.append("terminate"),
        wait=lambda **_: calls.append("wait"),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_input.subprocess, "Popen", lambda *_args, **_kwargs: process)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="consumer"):
        with voice_input._arecord_stream("default") as captured:
            assert captured.read() == b"audio"
            raise ValueError("consumer")
    assert calls == ["terminate", "wait"]
    assert stream.closed


def test_audio_stream_kills_when_termination_times_out(monkeypatch):
    stream = BytesIO()
    calls = []

    def wait(**_):
        calls.append("wait")
        if calls.count("wait") == 1:
            raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired("arecord", 1)

    process = SimpleNamespace(
        stdout=stream, poll=lambda: None, terminate=lambda: calls.append("terminate"),
        kill=lambda: calls.append("kill"), wait=wait,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_input.subprocess, "Popen", lambda *_args, **_kwargs: process)
    with voice_input._arecord_stream("default"):
        pass
    assert calls == ["terminate", "wait", "kill", "wait"]
    assert stream.closed


def test_audio_stream_rejects_missing_stdout_and_cleans_up(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    process = SimpleNamespace(
        stdout=None, poll=lambda: None, terminate=lambda: calls.append("terminate"),
        wait=lambda **_: calls.append("wait"),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_input.subprocess, "Popen", lambda *_args, **_kwargs: process)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="flux audio"):
        with voice_input._arecord_stream("default"):
            pytest.fail("An absent stream must not be yielded")
    assert calls == ["terminate", "wait"]


def test_transcription_uses_fake_vosk_and_skips_empty_results(monkeypatch):
    chunks = iter([b"empty", b"speech", b""])
    calls = []
    results = iter(['{"text":""}', '{"text":" avance " }'])
    recognizer = SimpleNamespace(
        AcceptWaveform=lambda raw: True, Result=lambda: next(results),
        Reset=lambda: calls.append("reset"),
    )

    @contextmanager
    def stream(device):
        calls.append(device)
        yield SimpleNamespace(read=lambda size: next(chunks))
        calls.append("closed")

    def model(path):
        assert path == voice_input.MODEL_PATH
        return "synthetic-model"

    def create_recognizer(loaded, rate):
        assert loaded == "synthetic-model"
        assert rate == 16000
        return recognizer

    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "vosk", SimpleNamespace(
        Model=model, KaldiRecognizer=create_recognizer,
    ))
    monkeypatch.setattr(voice_input, "_arecord_stream", stream)
    assert list(voice_input.transcribe_loop("synthetic-device")) == ["avance"]
    assert calls == ["synthetic-device", "reset", "closed"]


def test_transcription_reports_missing_vosk(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "vosk", None)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="vosk.*absente"):
        next(voice_input.transcribe_loop("unused"))


def test_hardware_factory_stays_offline_on_mac(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime_factory.sys, "platform", "darwin")
    assert runtime_factory.build_hardware_runtime(settings=RuntimeSettings()) == {
        "robot": None, "camera": None, "sensor": None,
    }


def test_hardware_factory_injects_sensor_and_cleans_resources_separately(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.common.hardware.smart_car import smart_car
    from irobot.common.hardware.runtime_factory import close_hardware_runtime

    class Sensor:
        def __init__(self):
            self.cleanup_calls = 0

        def read_distance_cm(self):
            return 25.0

        def cleanup(self):
            self.cleanup_calls += 1

    class Car:
        def __init__(self, *, drive_speed):
            self.speed = drive_speed

        def cleanup(self):
            pass

        def stop(self):
            pass

    sensor = Sensor()
    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime_factory.sys, "platform", "linux")
    monkeypatch.setattr(smart_car, "SmartCar2WD", Car)
    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "irobot.common.hardware.sensors.hc_sr04",
        SimpleNamespace(HC_SR04=lambda: sensor),
    )
    camera_kwargs = {}

    def create_camera(**kwargs):
        camera_kwargs.update(kwargs)
        return object()

    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "irobot.common.hardware.camera.camera_live_perception_pipeline",
        SimpleNamespace(CameraLivePerceptionPipeline=create_camera),
    )

    hardware = runtime_factory.build_hardware_runtime(
        settings=RuntimeSettings(obstacle_threshold_cm=30.0),
    )

    assert hardware["sensor"] is sensor
    assert hardware["sensor"].read_distance_cm() == 25.0
    assert not hasattr(hardware["robot"], "read_distance_cm")
    assert hardware["robot"].check_obstacle() is True
    assert camera_kwargs["sensor"] is sensor
    close_hardware_runtime(hardware)
    assert sensor.cleanup_calls == 1


def test_hardware_factory_cleans_up_if_camera_build_fails(monkeypatch):
    from irobot.common.hardware.smart_car import smart_car

    calls = []

    class Sensor:
        def read_distance_cm(self):
            return None

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("sensor cleanup")

    class Car:
        def __init__(self, *, drive_speed):
            self.speed = drive_speed

        def stop(self):
            calls.append("robot stop")

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("robot cleanup")

    def fail_camera(**kwargs):
        raise RuntimeError("camera init failed")

    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime_factory.sys, "platform", "linux")
    monkeypatch.setattr(smart_car, "SmartCar2WD", Car)
    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "irobot.common.hardware.sensors.hc_sr04",
        SimpleNamespace(HC_SR04=Sensor),
    )
    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "irobot.common.hardware.camera.camera_live_perception_pipeline",
        SimpleNamespace(CameraLivePerceptionPipeline=fail_camera),
    )

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="camera init failed"):
        runtime_factory.build_hardware_runtime(settings=RuntimeSettings())

    assert calls == ["robot stop", "robot cleanup", "sensor cleanup"]


def test_hardware_runtime_cleanup_attempts_every_resource():
    calls = []

    class Robot:
        def stop(self):
            calls.append("stop")
            raise RuntimeError("stop failed")

        def cleanup(self):
            calls.append("robot cleanup")
            raise RuntimeError("robot cleanup failed")

    class Camera:
        def close(self):
            calls.append("camera close")
            raise RuntimeError("camera close failed")

    with pytest.raises(ExceptionGroup) as error:
        runtime_factory.close_hardware_runtime({"robot": Robot(), "camera": Camera()})

    assert len(error.value.exceptions) == 3
    assert calls == ["stop", "robot cleanup", "camera close"]


def test_llm_interpretation_accepts_an_injected_transport():
    def post(payload, timeout, formats, meta=None):
        meta["llm_attempts"] = 1
        return '{"steps":[{"intent":"move_forward","parameters":{"distance":20}}]}'

    intents, trace = ask_llm_for_intent_plan_with_trace("avance de 20 cm", post=post)
    assert [intent.name for intent in intents] == ["move_forward"]
    assert trace["source"] == "llm_plan"
    assert trace["llm_attempts"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["spin_360", "move_square", "contour_obstacle"])
def test_expanded_steps_preserve_metadata_and_own_parameters(tmp_path, name):
    runtime = voice_runtime.VoiceRuntime(use_llm=False, trace_dir=str(tmp_path))
    original = Intent(name=name, target="red", confidence=0.7, raw_text="original")
    steps = runtime.command_service.planner.expand_intents([original])
    assert len({id(step.parameters) for step in steps}) == len(steps)
    assert all(step.target == "red" and step.confidence == 0.7 for step in steps)
    assert all(step.raw_text == "original" for step in steps)
    assert original.parameters == {}
